FLOW FACTORY 3D — ALL SOURCE CODE IN ONE FILE
Version 0.3.1 · generated 2026-11-04
==============================================================================
What this is: a 3D puzzle game for Android built with Unity 6 (C#). You rotate conveyor-belt tiles, press RUN,
and a parcel travels the belts to the target. 36 levels, stars, coins, a shop of cosmetic themes, hints, a daily gift.
This single text file contains every source file of the project (no images, no generated files), in this order:
Core logic (plain C#, no Unity) -> Runtime (rendering, UI, input) -> Editor tools -> Shaders -> Tests -> Tools/scripts.

Architecture in short:
  Assets/Scripts/Core     pure C# game logic: RouteValidator, LevelManager, LevelCatalog (36 levels), SaveService,
                          Economy (stars, coins, shop, daily gift, ad policy). Tested outside Unity with `dotnet test`.
  Assets/Scripts/Runtime  Unity side: GridManager/ConveyorTile/ParcelController (3D), GameUIController/MonetizationUi (UI),
                          InputBridge (mouse/touch), simulated ad and store services (TEST only, no real SDK).
  Assets/Scripts/Editor   menu tools: generate/validate level assets, project setup, Android build, screenshot tour.
  Assets/Resources        three small shaders (toon, ground, blob shadow).
  Assets/Tests            EditMode + PlayMode tests (NUnit / Unity Test Framework).
  Tools                   level generator (Python), installers (PowerShell/bash), dotnet test harness, preview simulation.

Honest status: logic is covered by 47 automated tests that pass with dotnet. The Unity-side code compiles against
Unity's libraries but has NOT yet been run inside the Unity editor or built to an APK.

====================================================================================================
# FLOW FACTORY 3D — גרסה 0.3

משחק חידות תלת־ממדי (Unity 6.3 LTS, C#). מסובבים אריחי מסוע, לוחצים **RUN**, וחבילה נעה לפי חיבורי המסועים עד היעד.
ממשק המשחק באנגלית פשוטה; כל הקוד כתוב מפרימיטיבים של Unity, בלי נכסים בתשלום ובלי רכיבי ענן. **פרסומות ורכישות הן מדומות בלבד (TEST, בלי SDK ובלי כסף אמיתי)** — ראה פרק "כלכלה, מודעות ורכישות".

> **סטטוס אימות (חשוב):** הקוד נכתב בסביבה ללא Unity Editor. לוגיקת המשחק נבדקה ורצה באמת (47 בדיקות עוברות), וקוד ה-Runtime עבר קומפילציה מול ספריות Unity 2021.3. **לא** בוצעו הרצה ב-Play Mode, בדיקת מגע, Build ל-Android או בדיקת FPS. פירוט בסוף המסמך.

## מבנה התיקייה

```
FlowFactory3D/
  Assets/
    Scripts/Core/      לוגיקה טהורה, ללא Unity: Direction, TileKind, LevelData, RouteValidator,
                       LevelManager (מצבי Idle/Running/Success/Failure), SaveService, LevelCatalog (36 שלבים), Economy (מטבעות, כוכבים, חנות, פרס יומי, מדיניות מודעות)
    Scripts/Runtime/   תצוגה וקלט: GridManager, ConveyorTile, ParcelController, GameUIController,
                       FlowFactoryApp, AudioService, LocalTelemetry, FpsCounter, SafeAreaFitter ...
    Scripts/Editor/    תפריט Flow Factory (יצירת שלבים, אימות, הגדרת פרויקט, Build ל-APK)
    Resources/Shaders/ שיידר שטוח פשוט שעובד גם ב-URP וגם ב-Built-in
    Tests/EditMode/    בדיקות NUnit ל-Unity Test Runner
  Tools/CoreTests/     הרצת אותן בדיקות לוגיקה עם dotnet בלבד (בלי Unity)
  Packages/manifest.json
```

הארכיטקטורה: אימות המסלול (`RouteValidator`) הוא פונקציה טהורה על נתונים ואינו תלוי בתצוגה או באנימציה.
`ConveyorTile`/`ParcelController` רק מציגים. השלבים הם **נתונים** (`LevelDefinition` ScriptableObject או `LevelCatalog` מובנה),
ויש סצנת משחק גנרית אחת שנבנית בזמן ריצה — אין 12 סצנות.

## התקנה והרצה

1. התקן **Unity Hub** ואת **Unity 6.3 LTS** (6000.3.x) עם מודולים: *Android Build Support*, *OpenJDK*, *Android SDK & NDK Tools*.
2. ב-Unity Hub צור פרויקט חדש מהתבנית **Universal 3D (URP)**, למשל `FlowFactory3D`.
3. סגור את העורך והעתק לפרויקט החדש את התיקייה `Assets/Scripts`, `Assets/Resources`, `Assets/Tests` (ואם רוצה גם `Tools`).
   **אל תדרוס** את `Packages/manifest.json` של הפרויקט; רק ודא שהחבילות **Input System** ו-**Test Framework** מותקנות
   (ב-*Window > Package Manager*; אם Unity שואל על החלפת מערכת הקלט, אשר). אפשר גם להשתמש בקובץ `Packages/manifest.json` שבתיקייה כנקודת התחלה.
4. פתח את הפרויקט וחכה לקומפילציה. ב-Console לא אמורות להופיע שגיאות.
5. הרץ מהתפריט **Flow Factory > Setup Project (portrait, Android, Main scene)** — מגדיר מצב אנכי, Android (IL2CPP, ARM64),
   יוצר את 12 נתוני השלבים ב-`Assets/Resources/Levels`, יוצר את `Assets/Scenes/Main.unity` ומוסיף אותה ל-Build Settings.
6. פתח את Game View, בחר יחס **9:16** (למשל 1080x1920) ולחץ **Play**. אפשר גם ללחוץ Play בכל סצנה אחרת: `GameBootstrap` מפעיל את המשחק לבד.

**משחק בעכבר:** קליק על אריח כחול (עם כפתור כתום) מסובב אותו 90°; **RUN** משגר חבילה; **RESET** מחזיר את כל הסיבובים; `<` חזרה לבחירת שלב; `Esc` = כפתור Back.

### בדיקות
- **בעורך:** *Window > General > Test Runner > EditMode > Run All* (בדיקות ה-Core + `UnityLevelAssetTests`).
- **אימות פתרונות הידועים:** *Flow Factory > Validate All Levels (known solutions)*.
- **בלי Unity** (בדיקות הלוגיקה בלבד): `cd Tools/CoreTests && dotnet test` (דורש .NET SDK 8).

## בניית APK ל-Android

1. *File > Build Profiles* (או Build Settings) — ודא שפלטפורמת Android מותקנת ונבחרה (*Switch Platform*).
2. *Flow Factory > Build Android APK (development, FPS display)* — נוצר `Builds/Android/FlowFactory3D-dev.apk` עם תצוגת FPS.
   גרסת `release` נבנית מהתפריט המקביל (חתימת debug כברירת מחדל; לפרסום יש להגדיר Keystore משלך).
   משורת פקודה: `Unity -batchmode -quit -projectPath <path> -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.BuildAndroidBatch`.
3. במכשיר: הפעל *Developer options > USB debugging*, חבר כבל, ואז `adb install -r Builds/Android/FlowFactory3D-dev.apk`
   (או העתק את ה-APK למכשיר ופתח אותו; יש לאשר התקנה ממקור לא מוכר).
4. יומן אירועי הבדיקה: `adb shell run-as com.flowfactory.prototype3d cat files/telemetry.log` (בגרסת Development).

## מה יש בגרסה 0.1

- 12 שלבים ידניים (4x4 בשלבים 1–4, 5x5 מ-5), כולם עם פתרון ידוע שנבדק אוטומטית; שלבים 1–2 הם הדרכה חזותית (נקודה צהובה מעל האריח הבא ללחיצה, ואז כפתור RUN פועם).
- אריחים: ישר, פינה, מקור, יעד, וארגז דקורטיבי חוסם. אריחים ניתנים לסיבוב מסומנים בכחול עם כפתור כתום.
- RUN פעיל גם כשהמסלול שגוי: החבילה נעצרת/נופלת, האריח הבעייתי מהבהב באדום, מופיעה הודעה קצרה, וחוזרים לעריכה בלי קנס.
- מסך הצלחה, פתיחת השלב הבא, שמירה מקומית (`PlayerPrefs`, עם גרסת סכימה, עמיד בפני נתונים פגומים), המשך מהשלב הפתוח.
- תפריט ראשי, בחירת שלב (עם כיתוב DONE/PLAY/LOCKED ולא רק צבע), הגדרות צליל (מופעל/כבוי, עוצמה), איפוס התקדמות בשני לחיצות.
- צלילים מיוצרים בקוד; Safe Area; ממשק לפי 1080x1920 עם `CanvasScaler`; כפתורים גדולים.
- תצוגת FPS רק בעורך/Development build. `LocalTelemetry` כותב לקובץ מקומי בלבד, בלי מזהים אישיים:
  `session_start, tutorial_start, tutorial_complete, level_start, level_success, level_retry, session_end`.

## התקנה מקומית אוטומטית (v0.3.1)

`Tools/Install/install_flowfactory.ps1` (Windows) או `install_flowfactory.sh` (macOS/Linux) יוצרים פרויקט Universal 3D, מעתיקים את הקבצים, מריצים Setup Project ובדיקות ובונים APK אם אפשר, ומפיקים דוח אמת. פרטים: `docs/INSTALL_LOCAL_HE.md`.

## v0.3: אימות אמיתי ב-Unity (חשוב לקרוא)

הדו"ח המלא: `docs/V0_3_REPORT_HE.md`. בקצרה: **עדיין לא הורץ ב-Unity** (לא היה חיבור לעורך). נוספו מסועים נעים, צל לחבילה, אפקט סיום, אנימציות כפתורים ומעברים, איחוד רשתות, הגדרות Android, בדיקות PlayMode, סיור צילומי מסך ודוח סביבה.
סדר מומלץ אצלך: `Flow Factory > Setup Project` → `Environment Report` → Test Runner (EditMode ואז PlayMode) → `Capture Screenshot Tour` → `Build Android APK (development)`. סקריפטים: `Tools/UnityChecks/`.

## גרסה 0.2: שלבים וכלכלה

**36 שלבים בשלושה עולמות של 12** (עולם 2 ו-3 נוצרו פעם אחת בכלי `Tools/LevelGen/gen_levels.py` ושמורים כנתונים סטטיים; כל שלב נבדק: הפתרון הידוע תקין והמצב ההתחלתי אינו פתור).
בעולמות המתקדמים: מסלולים ארוכים יותר (עד 16 תאים), יותר פניות, יותר אריחי הטעיה וארגזים, ואריחים שכבר במצב הנכון ואסור לגעת בהם.
בחירת השלב מחולקת לעמודים (עולמות), וסוף כל עולם מפעיל את אנימציית המפעל.

**כוכבים:** 1–3 כוכבים לפי מספר הנגיעות בלבד (אף פעם לא זמן): 3 = המינימום האפשרי, 2 = עד כמה נגיעות מעבר, 1 = אחרת. שיפור כוכבים משלם הפרש במטבעות ונותן סיבה לשחק שוב.

**מטבעות:** 25–40 למעבר ראשון (תלוי בכוכבים ובשלב), 5+ לחזרה, פרס יומי של 7 ימים (20 ← 100 מטבעות, רצף נשבר אם מפספסים יום), ופרסומת מתגמלת אופציונלית.
**מה קונים במטבעות:** רמזים (15 מטבעות, אפשר גם בפרסומת אחת אם אין מספיק), 4 ערכות נושא ו-4 עיצובי חבילה (150–500 מטבעות). הכול קוסמטי או רמז; **שום דבר לא חוסם התקדמות**.
**רכישות (מדומות):** חבילת מטבעות קטנה ($0.99), חבילה גדולה עם בונוס ($2.99, "BEST VALUE"), וחבילת פתיחה חד־פעמית ($1.99: 1000 מטבעות + נושא Candy + עיצוב Gift Box).

### עקרונות עיצוב (נאכפים בקוד ובבדיקות)
- פרסומת היא תמיד **תוספת שהשחקן לוחץ עליה** (כפל מטבעות בסוף שלב, רמז חינם, מטבעות חינם בחנות). אין פרסומות בזמן חידה, אין פרסומות כפויות, אין חסימת התקדמות. תקרה של 5 ביום והשהיה של 30 שניות (`PlayerEconomy`).
- אין טיימרי לחץ, חלונות "מבצע מוגבל בזמן" או הטעיה. תזכורת היחידה: בסוף שלב, אם אפשר לקנות פריט חדש, מופיעה שורה אחת; נקודה אדומה על כפתור החנות כשיש מתנה יומית או פריט שאפשר לקנות.
- לא מוכרים יתרון משחקי במזומן: חבילות מטבעות הן קיצור דרך לקוסמטיקה ולרמזים.
- איפוס שלבים (בהגדרות) לא מוחק מטבעות ורכישות.

### להפוך את זה לאמיתי (לא נעשה כאן)
1. **מודעות:** כתוב מימוש אמיתי ל-`IRewardedAdService` (למשל Unity LevelPlay) במקום `SimulatedAdService`, עם חשבון משלך, **Test Ads בלבד בפיתוח**, ומדיניות פרטיות. לפני כן בדוק את מדיניות Google Play למודעות ולילדים; אם הקהל כולל ילדים, השתמש רק בספקים ובהגדרות שמותרים לקהל זה (בלי מודעות מותאמות אישית).
2. **רכישות:** מימוש ל-`IStoreService` (למשל Unity IAP) עם מזהי המוצרים שב-`ShopCatalog` (`ff_coins_500`, `ff_coins_1500`, `ff_starter`) שתגדיר ב-Google Play Console. חובה: שחזור רכישות, אימות קבלות בשרת אם יש ערך כספי גדול, ותנאי שימוש.
3. אירועי הבדיקה המקומיים החדשים: `shop_open, item_bought, purchase_started, purchase_completed (test=1), ad_offered, ad_completed, ad_skipped, hint_used, daily_claimed`; `level_success` כולל `stars` ו-`coins`.
4. כוונון המספרים נמצא כולו ב-`EconomyConfig`, `ShopCatalog` ו-`PlayerEconomy.AwardLevel`. אל תסיק מהם על הכנסות: אין נתוני שחקנים.

## מראה ותפאורה

הכול בנוי מפרימיטיבים של Unity, בלי קבצי תמונה: מרקמים מיוצרים בקוד (`ProceduralTextures`: לוחות מתכת עם ברגים, גלילי מסוע, עץ, פסי אזהרה),
שיידר שטוח עם מרקם (`FlowFactoryToon`) ורצפת מפעל עם דהייה (`FlowFactoryGround`), פלטה חמה (קרם, טורקיז, אלמוגי, ענבר), אריחים מפורטים
(מסגרות, גלילי קצה, פלטת סיבוב לפינות, חוגה על אריחים ניתנים לסיבוב, מכונת מקור, פח יעד זוהר), עמודי תאורה, חביות, גלגל שיניים מסתובב, צינור וארגזים,
קונפטי בניצחון, כפתורים מעוגלים עם צל וברק, ותפריט ראשי עם לוח חי שבו חבילה עושה את דרכה בלולאה. ערכות הנושא (Candy, Midnight, Golden Age) ועיצובי החבילה משנים את הצבעים (`Themes.cs`).
התמונות ב-`docs/preview_images` הן **סימולציה מקורבת** (three.js), לא צילומי מסך של Unity.

## מה חסר / נדחה (במכוון)

- **שלבים 9–10 ממפרט המוצר** (שני סוגי חבילות) נדחו לגרסה 0.2 כי המודל תומך כרגע במקור ויעד יחידים; במקומם שלבי חבילה יחידה מורכבים יותר.
  כיוון שיש חבילה אחת בלבד, אין עדיין הבחנה בין צבעי חבילות; בממשק, מצב שלב מופיע גם בכיתוב ולא רק בצבע.
- זיהוי **לולאה** קיים (מצב חוזר + מגבלת צעדים), אך עם סוגי האריחים הנוכחיים לולאה אמיתית **אי אפשר לבנות** (המסלול הפיך, ולכן תמיד יגיע לכישלון אחר קודם). הבדיקה מכסה את מגבלת הצעדים בלבד.
- אין אנימציית "מפעל מתעורר" מלאה — רק אנימציה קצרה וניתנת לדילוג בסוף כל עולם (שלבים 12, 24, 36). אין הרחבת מפעל או ענן.
- **מודעות ורכישות אמיתיות לא קיימות.** `IRewardedAdService` ו-`IStoreService` מומשו רק כסימולציה מסומנת TEST.

## מה אומת ומה לא

| נושא | סטטוס |
|---|---|
| לוגיקה (מסלול תקין/חסום/חיבור חסר/חיבור שגוי/יציאה מהלוח/מגבלת צעדים, reset, נעילה ופתיחת שלבים, save/load, 36 שלבים פתירים, כלכלה וחנות) | **הורצה**: 47 בדיקות עוברות עם `dotnet test` (net8.0, C# 9 כמו Unity) |
| קוד Runtime (UI/תצוגה/קלט) | **עבר קומפילציה** מול `UnityEngine.Modules` 2021.3.33 ו-stubs ל-uGUI. ענף ה-Input System החדש, קוד ה-Editor והשיידר **לא הורכבו** |
| Play Mode, מראה ויזואלי, מסגור המצלמה, מגע, צלילים | **לא הורצו** |
| Build ל-Android, התקנה, FPS 30 יציב, חימום | **לא בוצעו** (דורש Unity + Android SDK ומכשיר) |
| 1080x2400 / 720x1600, Safe Area על מכשיר | **לא נבדק** (נבנה לפי Safe Area ו-CanvasScaler) |

## שיפור משחקיות מוצע לשלב הבא (לפני מונטיזציה)

1. אריח "מתג" או מסוע דו־כיווני כדי שיהיו מסלולים אמיתיים עם מספר פתרונות, ואז ללכת על חבילות בשני סוגים (שלבים 9–10 המקוריים).
2. דירוג כוכבים לפי מספר הנגיעות (לא זמן), עם הסבר ברור על "פתרון אופטימלי" — בלי לחץ זמן.
3. אחרי כל שלב להוסיף חלק קטן למפעל (גרסה 0.2), כדי שההתקדמות תיראה לעין.
4. מבחן איכותני עם 10–20 שחקנים (בהסכמה מתאימה לפי גיל ומקום) לפני כל החלטה על מונטיזציה.

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/Direction.cs   (82 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>Compass direction on the board. North is "up" in grid space (row - 1).</summary>
    public enum Direction
    {
        North = 0,
        East = 1,
        South = 2,
        West = 3
    }

    public static class DirectionExtensions
    {
        public static Direction Opposite(this Direction d)
        {
            return (Direction)(((int)d + 2) & 3);
        }

        /// <summary>Rotates clockwise by the given number of quarter turns (may be negative).</summary>
        public static Direction Rotate(this Direction d, int quarterTurns)
        {
            return (Direction)((((int)d + quarterTurns) % 4 + 4) % 4);
        }

        public static int DeltaX(this Direction d)
        {
            return d == Direction.East ? 1 : d == Direction.West ? -1 : 0;
        }

        public static int DeltaY(this Direction d)
        {
            return d == Direction.South ? 1 : d == Direction.North ? -1 : 0;
        }

        public static int Bit(this Direction d)
        {
            return 1 << (int)d;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>A cell coordinate. X grows to the east, Y grows to the south (row index).</summary>
    [Serializable]
    public struct GridPoint : IEquatable<GridPoint>
    {
        public int x;
        public int y;

        public GridPoint(int x, int y)
        {
            this.x = x;
            this.y = y;
        }

        public GridPoint Step(Direction d)
        {
            return new GridPoint(x + d.DeltaX(), y + d.DeltaY());
        }

        public bool Equals(GridPoint other)
        {
            return x == other.x && y == other.y;
        }

        public override bool Equals(object obj)
        {
            return obj is GridPoint other && Equals(other);
        }

        public override int GetHashCode()
        {
            return x * 397 ^ y;
        }

        public override string ToString()
        {
            return "(" + x + "," + y + ")";
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/Economy.cs   (322 lines)

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>All tunable numbers of the economy in one place.</summary>
    public static class EconomyConfig
    {
        public const int HintCost = 15;
        public const int FreeCoinsAdReward = 30;
        public const int DailyAdLimit = 5;
        public const int AdCooldownSeconds = 30;
        public static readonly int[] DailyRewards = { 20, 25, 30, 40, 50, 60, 100 };
    }

    public enum ItemKind
    {
        Theme,
        Parcel,
        /// <summary>Real-money coin pack.</summary>
        CoinPack,
        /// <summary>One-time real-money bundle of coins and cosmetics.</summary>
        Bundle
    }

    public sealed class ShopItem
    {
        public string Id;
        public ItemKind Kind;
        public string Name;
        public string Description;
        /// <summary>Price in coins; 0 with no ProductId = free from the start. -1 = not sold for coins.</summary>
        public int CoinPrice;
        /// <summary>Store product id for real-money items, otherwise null.</summary>
        public string ProductId;
        /// <summary>Price text shown for real-money items (the store reports the real localized price).</summary>
        public string DisplayPrice;
        public int CoinsGranted;
        public string[] ItemsGranted = new string[0];
        public string Badge;
    }

    public static class ShopCatalog
    {
        public const string DefaultTheme = "theme.classic";
        public const string DefaultParcel = "parcel.box";

        private static readonly List<ShopItem> Items = new List<ShopItem>
        {
            new ShopItem { Id = "theme.classic", Kind = ItemKind.Theme, Name = "Classic", Description = "Warm cream plates on teal.", CoinPrice = 0 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "theme.candy", Kind = ItemKind.Theme, Name = "Candy", Description = "Pink and mint sweets factory.", CoinPrice = 150 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "theme.midnight", Kind = ItemKind.Theme, Name = "Midnight", Description = "Dark plates with neon belts.", CoinPrice = 250 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "theme.gold", Kind = ItemKind.Theme, Name = "Golden Age", Description = "Brass and marble luxury.", CoinPrice = 500, Badge = "RARE" },

            new ShopItem { Id = "parcel.box", Kind = ItemKind.Parcel, Name = "Classic Box", Description = "The original delivery.", CoinPrice = 0 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "parcel.gift", Kind = ItemKind.Parcel, Name = "Gift Box", Description = "Red wrapping, gold ribbon.", CoinPrice = 120 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "parcel.ocean", Kind = ItemKind.Parcel, Name = "Ocean Box", Description = "Cool blue crate.", CoinPrice = 120 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "parcel.gold", Kind = ItemKind.Parcel, Name = "Golden Parcel", Description = "Shiny and proud.", CoinPrice = 300, Badge = "RARE" },

            new ShopItem { Id = "coins.small", Kind = ItemKind.CoinPack, Name = "Pile of Coins", Description = "500 coins", CoinPrice = -1,
                ProductId = "ff_coins_500", DisplayPrice = "$0.99", CoinsGranted = 500 },
            new ShopItem { Id = "coins.big", Kind = ItemKind.CoinPack, Name = "Crate of Coins", Description = "1500 coins + 300 bonus", CoinPrice = -1,
                ProductId = "ff_coins_1500", DisplayPrice = "$2.99", CoinsGranted = 1800, Badge = "BEST VALUE" },
            new ShopItem { Id = "bundle.starter", Kind = ItemKind.Bundle, Name = "Starter Pack", Description = "1000 coins + Candy theme + Gift Box", CoinPrice = -1,
                ProductId = "ff_starter", DisplayPrice = "$1.99", CoinsGranted = 1000, ItemsGranted = new[] { "theme.candy", "parcel.gift" }, Badge = "ONE TIME" },
        };

        public static IList<ShopItem> All
        {
            get { return Items; }
        }

        public static ShopItem Find(string id)
        {
            foreach (ShopItem item in Items)
            {
                if (item.Id == id) return item;
            }
            return null;
        }

        public static ShopItem FindByProduct(string productId)
        {
            foreach (ShopItem item in Items)
            {
                if (item.ProductId == productId) return item;
            }
            return null;
        }

        public static IList<ShopItem> OfKind(ItemKind kind)
        {
            var result = new List<ShopItem>();
            foreach (ShopItem item in Items)
            {
                if (item.Kind == kind) result.Add(item);
            }
            return result;
        }

        /// <summary>Free items count as owned; everything else must be in the owned set.</summary>
        public static bool IsOwned(ICollection<string> owned, string id)
        {
            ShopItem item = Find(id);
            if (item == null) return false;
            if (item.CoinPrice == 0 && item.ProductId == null) return true;
            return owned.Contains(id);
        }
    }

    public static class StarRating
    {
        /// <summary>3 stars for the minimum number of taps, 2 for a few extra, otherwise 1. Time never matters.</summary>
        public static int Calculate(int taps, int minimumTaps)
        {
            if (taps <= minimumTaps) return 3;
            if (taps <= minimumTaps + Math.Max(2, minimumTaps / 2)) return 2;
            return 1;
        }
    }

    public struct LevelReward
    {
        public int Stars;
        public int Coins;
        public bool FirstClear;
        public bool ImprovedStars;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Coins, stars, shop and ad rules on top of the saved progress. Pure logic: no Unity types and no clock
    /// (callers pass the day number and a time in seconds), so every rule is unit-testable.
    /// Design rules: nothing here ever blocks progress; coins buy cosmetics and optional hints only.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class PlayerEconomy
    {
        private readonly SaveService save;
        private double lastAdTime = -1e9;

        public PlayerEconomy(SaveService save)
        {
            this.save = save;
        }

        public ProgressData Data
        {
            get { return save.Data; }
        }

        public int Coins
        {
            get { return save.Data.Coins; }
        }

        /// <summary>Whole days since 2024-01-01 (local date); stable across the day, easy to compare.</summary>
        public static int DayNumber(DateTime localNow)
        {
            return (int)(localNow.Date - new DateTime(2024, 1, 1)).TotalDays + 1;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ levels

        public LevelReward AwardLevel(LevelData level, int taps)
        {
            ProgressData data = save.Data;
            bool first = !data.IsCompleted(level.id);
            int stars = StarRating.Calculate(taps, level.MinimumTaps());
            int previousBest = data.StarsFor(level.id);
            bool improved = stars > previousBest;

            int coins;
            if (first) coins = 10 + 5 * stars + 10 + level.id / 6;
            else if (improved) coins = 5 + 5 * (stars - previousBest);
            else coins = 5;

            if (improved) data.Stars[level.id] = stars;
            data.MarkCompleted(level.id, SaveLevelCount);
            data.Coins += coins;
            save.Save();
            return new LevelReward { Stars = stars, Coins = coins, FirstClear = first, ImprovedStars = improved };
        }

        private int SaveLevelCount
        {
            get { return save.LevelCount; }
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ coins

        public void AddCoins(int amount)
        {
            if (amount <= 0) return;
            save.Data.Coins += amount;
            save.Save();
        }

        public bool TrySpend(int amount)
        {
            if (amount < 0 || save.Data.Coins < amount) return false;
            save.Data.Coins -= amount;
            save.Save();
            return true;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ shop

        public bool IsOwned(string itemId)
        {
            return ShopCatalog.IsOwned(save.Data.Owned, itemId);
        }

        public bool IsEquipped(string itemId)
        {
            return save.Data.Theme == itemId || save.Data.Parcel == itemId;
        }

        /// <summary>Buys a coin-priced cosmetic. Fails (nothing changes) when unknown, already owned or too expensive.</summary>
        public bool TryBuyWithCoins(string itemId)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.Find(itemId);
            if (item == null || item.CoinPrice <= 0 || IsOwned(itemId)) return false;
            if (!TrySpend(item.CoinPrice)) return false;
            save.Data.Owned.Add(itemId);
            save.Save();
            return true;
        }

        public bool Equip(string itemId)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.Find(itemId);
            if (item == null || !IsOwned(itemId)) return false;
            if (item.Kind == ItemKind.Theme) save.Data.Theme = itemId;
            else if (item.Kind == ItemKind.Parcel) save.Data.Parcel = itemId;
            else return false;
            save.Save();
            return true;
        }

        /// <summary>Whether a real-money product can still be bought (one-time bundles only once).</summary>
        public bool CanBuyProduct(string productId)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.FindByProduct(productId);
            if (item == null) return false;
            return item.Kind != ItemKind.Bundle || !save.Data.Owned.Contains(item.Id);
        }

        /// <summary>Called after the store confirmed a purchase. Grants coins and items; returns false if unknown or repeated.</summary>
        public bool GrantProduct(string productId)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.FindByProduct(productId);
            if (item == null || !CanBuyProduct(productId)) return false;

            save.Data.Coins += item.CoinsGranted;
            foreach (string granted in item.ItemsGranted) save.Data.Owned.Add(granted);
            if (item.Kind == ItemKind.Bundle) save.Data.Owned.Add(item.Id);
            save.Save();
            return true;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ daily gift

        public bool DailyAvailable(int today)
        {
            return save.Data.LastDailyDay != today;
        }

        /// <summary>The coins the gift for the current streak would give (day 1-7, then it starts over).</summary>
        public int NextDailyReward(int today)
        {
            int streak = NextStreak(today);
            return EconomyConfig.DailyRewards[(streak - 1) % EconomyConfig.DailyRewards.Length];
        }

        public int NextStreak(int today)
        {
            ProgressData data = save.Data;
            return data.LastDailyDay == today - 1 ? data.DailyStreak + 1 : 1;
        }

        /// <summary>Claims today's gift. Returns the coins granted, or 0 if it was already claimed today.</summary>
        public int ClaimDaily(int today)
        {
            if (!DailyAvailable(today)) return 0;
            int streak = NextStreak(today);
            int coins = EconomyConfig.DailyRewards[(streak - 1) % EconomyConfig.DailyRewards.Length];
            save.Data.DailyStreak = streak;
            save.Data.LastDailyDay = today;
            save.Data.Coins += coins;
            save.Save();
            return coins;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ rewarded ads (optional extras only)

        public int AdsRemainingToday(int today)
        {
            ProgressData data = save.Data;
            int used = data.AdsDay == today ? data.AdsToday : 0;
            return Math.Max(0, EconomyConfig.DailyAdLimit - used);
        }

        /// <summary>A rewarded ad may be offered when today's cap and the cooldown allow it.</summary>
        public bool CanOfferRewardedAd(int today, double nowSeconds)
        {
            return AdsRemainingToday(today) > 0 && nowSeconds - lastAdTime >= EconomyConfig.AdCooldownSeconds;
        }

        public void RegisterAdWatched(int today, double nowSeconds)
        {
            ProgressData data = save.Data;
            if (data.AdsDay != today)
            {
                data.AdsDay = today;
                data.AdsToday = 0;
            }
            data.AdsToday++;
            lastAdTime = nowSeconds;
            save.Save();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/Future/IRewardedAdService.cs   (20 lines)

using System;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Rewarded ads. The game talks only to this interface. The project ships with a clearly labelled TEST
    /// implementation (a fake 3-second screen, no SDK, no network); a real one (for example Unity LevelPlay) must
    /// use the developer's own ad accounts, Test Ads while developing, and a privacy/age-policy review.
    /// Rules the game follows: ads are always optional extras the player taps, never shown during a puzzle and
    /// never required to progress.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IRewardedAdService
    {
        bool IsReady { get; }

        /// <summary>Shows a rewarded ad the player asked for; reports whether the reward was earned.</summary>
        void Show(Action<bool> onCompleted);
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/Future/IStoreService.cs   (21 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Real-money purchases. The game talks only to this interface. The project ships with a clearly labelled
    /// TEST implementation (no money, no store); a real one (for example Unity IAP) must be written against
    /// the developer's own store accounts, product ids and privacy setup before any release.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IStoreService
    {
        bool IsReady { get; }

        /// <summary>Localized price text for a product, or null if unknown.</summary>
        string PriceFor(string productId);

        /// <summary>Starts a purchase the player asked for; reports whether it completed.</summary>
        void Purchase(string productId, Action<bool> onCompleted);
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Authoring source for the 36 levels (3 worlds of 12), written as ASCII grids of the SOLVED board.
    /// The editor tool turns these into LevelDefinition assets; the game falls back to them if no
    /// assets were generated. Cells are separated by spaces:
    ///   .            empty cell                 #            decorative block
    ///   S^ S&gt; Sv S&lt;  source, open side         T^ T&gt; Tv T&lt;  target, open side
    ///   H V          straight, solved orientation (H = east-west, V = north-south)
    ///   NE ES SW WN  corner, the two open sides in the solved board
    ///   Lower case (h v ne es sw wn) = fixed tile the player cannot rotate.
    ///   A suffix ":n" on a rotatable tile = taps needed from the start (n = 1..3 quarter turns).
    /// Rows are listed north to south.
    /// </summary>
    public static class LevelCatalog
    {
        private sealed class Spec
        {
            public string Title;
            public string Hint;
            public bool Tutorial;
            public string[] Rows;

            public Spec(string title, string hint, bool tutorial, params string[] rows)
            {
                Title = title;
                Hint = hint;
                Tutorial = tutorial;
                Rows = rows;
            }
        }

        private static readonly Spec[] Specs =
        {
            new Spec("First Turn", "Tap the belt to turn it, then press RUN.", true,
                ". . . .",
                ". ES:1 T< .",
                ". S^ . .",
                ". . . ."),

            new Spec("Two Belts", "Turn both belts so the parcel can pass.", true,
                ". . . .",
                ". . Tv .",
                "S> H:1 WN:2 .",
                ". . . ."),

            new Spec("Around the Corner", "Not every belt needs turning.", false,
                "S> h H:1 SW:1",
                ". . . v",
                ". # . T^",
                ". . . ."),

            new Spec("Look Twice", "Some belts only look useful.", false,
                ". SW:1 NE:2 .",
                "ES:1 H:1 SW:1 H:1",
                "V:1 NE:2 V:1 .",
                "S^ . T^ #"),

            new Spec("Three Turns", "Follow the belt with your eyes first.", false,
                ". # . . .",
                "S> H:1 SW:1 . .",
                ". NE:2 V:1 SW:1 Tv",
                ". . NE:1 H:1 WN:1",
                "# . . . ."),

            new Spec("Sideways", "Straight belts can face the wrong way, too.", false,
                ". . . . .",
                ". . . Tv .",
                ". # . v .",
                ". NE:1 SW:2 v #",
                "S> H:1 H:1 wn ."),

            new Spec("Dead End", "Crates block the way. Find another one.", false,
                ". . . # .",
                ". . ES:1 H:1 T<",
                "# SW:2 V:1 NE:1 .",
                "S> H:1 WN:1 . .",
                ". . . . #"),

            new Spec("Staircase", "Corners and straights are not the same.", false,
                "S> SW:1 . . .",
                ". NE:2 SW:1 H:1 .",
                ". V:1 NE:1 SW:2 .",
                ". . . NE:3 T<",
                ". . . . ."),

            new Spec("Snake", "A long way around.", false,
                "S> H:1 H:1 SW:1 .",
                ". . . V:1 .",
                ". ES:1 H:1 WN:3 .",
                ". NE:2 H:1 H:1 SW:1",
                ". . . . T^"),

            new Spec("Backwards", "Start on the right, end on the left.", false,
                ". . ES:1 H:1 S<",
                ". Tv V:1 . .",
                ". v NE:1 H:1 SW:2",
                ". v . . V:1",
                ". NE:2 H:1 H:1 WN:1"),

            new Spec("Full House", "Lots of belts. Only one way through.", false,
                "NE:1 H:1 SW:2 V:1 ES:3",
                "SW:1 NE:2 ES:1 SW:1 H:1",
                "S> H:1 WN:1 V:1 SW:2",
                "NE:2 SW:1 ES:2 WN:1 V:1",
                "H:1 ES:1 NE:1 H:1 T<"),

            new Spec("Factory Awakens", "Wake the factory!", false,
                "S> H:1 H:1 H:1 SW:1",
                "# . H:1 . V:1",
                ". ES:1 T< . V:1",
                ". V:1 . # V:1",
                ". NE:2 H:1 H:1 WN:1"),

            // ---- World 2 (13-24) and World 3 (25-36): generated once with Tools/LevelGen/gen_levels.py and
            // committed as static data. Longer routes, more turns, more decoys and crates.
            new Spec("Overtime", "Trace the route from the source first.", false,
                ". . . # .",
                ". . . Tv .",
                ". S> sw v #",
                ". es WN:1 V:1 ES",
                ". NE:3 H:1 WN:2 ."),

            new Spec("Night Shift", "Count the turns before you tap.", false,
                ". # . # SW:1",
                "# v . . .",
                "H:1 wn . ES:3 Sv",
                "v es SW:3 ES:3 WN:3",
                "ne T^ NE:3 WN:3 SW:1"),

            new Spec("Loading Bay", "Belts that are already right can be left alone.", false,
                ". # . NE:2 WN:3",
                ". V v . .",
                "ES:1 SW:1 ES:1 SW:2 .",
                "S^ NE:3 WN:2 NE:2 T<",
                "# . . . ."),

            new Spec("Conveyor Maze", "Crates never move. Plan around them.", false,
                "es S< ES:3 Tv #",
                "NE:3 h SW:3 V:1 .",
                ". NE:3 NE:3 WN:2 #",
                ". wn # H:1 .",
                ". . . H V:1"),

            new Spec("Fast Lane", "Some belts only look useful.", false,
                ". . # . .",
                ". V:1 ES:2 SW:1 V",
                "V:1 . V:1 NE:3 SW",
                ". . ne SW:1 V:1",
                ". sw S> WN:3 T^"),

            new Spec("Sorting Hall", "Work backwards from the target.", false,
                ". ES:1 # # .",
                "WN:3 SW:3 V . .",
                "ES:1 T< ES:1 SW:2 Sv",
                "NE:1 SW:2 V NE:2 WN:1",
                ". NE:1 WN:3 . ."),

            new Spec("Detour", "A long way round can be the only way.", false,
                "ES:1 H:1 h S< es",
                "V:1 . ES:3 SW:1 .",
                "NE:1 SW:2 . . .",
                "T> WN:2 . # .",
                ". . V:1 . ."),

            new Spec("Zig Zag", "Fix the corners, then the straights.", false,
                "ne ES:1 SW:2 # .",
                "es WN:3 V:1 . .",
                "V:1 NE V:1 ES:3 .",
                "T^ v NE:2 H:1 S<",
                ". . . V V:1"),

            new Spec("Packing Line", "Trace the route from the source first.", false,
                "WN:3 V:1 es SW:2 Tv",
                ". H V:1 V:1 v",
                ". S> wn V:1 V:1",
                "v SW:3 . NE:3 WN:2",
                "NE:3 ES . SW H"),

            new Spec("Mixed Up", "Count the turns before you tap.", false,
                ". ES # # V",
                "h . V ES:1 SW:2",
                ". . . T^ V:1",
                ". . ES:1 S< V:1",
                "v wn NE:3 H WN:2"),

            new Spec("Tight Squeeze", "Belts that are already right can be left alone.", false,
                "ES:2 SW:3 wn SW #",
                "V:1 v S> SW:1 .",
                "T^ V:1 . V h",
                "sw NE:1 H:1 WN:1 .",
                ". . # # ."),

            new Spec("Grand Opening", "Crates never move. Plan around them.", false,
                "h ES:3 H:1 S< .",
                "ES:1 WN:3 . ES:3 H",
                "NE:2 SW:2 V . WN:3",
                "es v WN ES:2 SW:2",

            new Spec("Second Wind", "Some belts only look useful.", false,
                "WN . # ES S<",

                ". NE:3 ne sw V:1",

            new Spec("Rush Order", "Work backwards from the target.", false,
                "ES:3 SW:2 ES:1 SW:1 SW:1",
                "V NE:2 WN:2 NE:2 SW:2",
                "V:1 H . S> WN",
                "NE:2 T< sw H .",
                "NE:1 . . H:1 H"),

            new Spec("Back Alley", "A long way round can be the only way.", false,
                "v . . ES SW:2",
                "ES . . V:1 S^",
                "ES:3 T< H:1 V:1 NE:1",
                "NE:1 SW:2 V V:1 H:1",
                "SW NE:2 H:1 WN:1 ."),

            new Spec("Crossroads", "Fix the corners, then the straights.", false,
                "h ES:2 V ES:3 SW:3",
                "Tv . S> WN:3 v",
                "NE:1 H:1 H:1 SW:3 V:1",

            new Spec("Spiral Stairs", "Trace the route from the source first.", false,

            new Spec("Hidden Path", "Belts that are already right can be left alone.", false,
                "ES:3 SW:1 SW:3 . .",

                "T^ NE:2 WN:2 NE:3 V:1",
                "# . H:1 . S^",
                ". # ne SW:3 ."),

            new Spec("Clockwork", "Crates never move. Plan around them.", false,
                "Sv ne ES:3 h SW:2",
                "V:1 # V:1 ES:1 WN:2",
                "V:1 ES:1 WN:2 V:1 NE:2",
                "NE:3 WN:2 NE:2 NE:1 T<",
                "NE:3 SW:1 V SW ."),

            new Spec("Double Back", "Some belts only look useful.", false,
                "ES:3 SW:2 ES SW:2 Sv",
                "v NE:1 WN NE:2 WN",
                "V:1 . es NE:2 wn",
                "v v V V:1 SW:1",
                "NE H:1 T< WN:2 V"),

            new Spec("Foreman's Test", "Work backwards from the target.", false,
                "SW:2 ES:2 SW:3 ES:1 SW:3",
                "ES:1 WN NE WN:3 V",
                "NE:3 SW:2 NE:3 V V:1",
                "S> WN:3 . ES T^",
                "# H:1 H:1 V:1 ."),

            new Spec("Master Plan", "A long way round can be the only way.", false,
                "SW:2 ES:2 ES:1 SW:3 ES:2",
                ". . V:1 NE:2 SW:3",
                "V ES:3 V T> WN:1",
                "NE WN V:1 ES:3 SW",
                "sw SW:3 NE:3 WN:1 S^"),

            new Spec("Factory Legend", "Fix the corners, then the straights.", false,
                "NE NE:3 H:1 . Tv",
                "ES:3 SW H WN:2 V:1",
                "V:1 S^ V:1 V:1 V:1",
                "NE:2 H:1 SW:3 ES:1 WN:3",
                "V h NE WN:3 ES"),
        };

        public static int Count
        {
            get { return Specs.Length; }
        }

        public static IList<LevelData> BuildAll()
        {
            var result = new List<LevelData>(Specs.Length);
            for (int i = 0; i < Specs.Length; i++)
            {
                result.Add(Parse(i + 1, Specs[i]));
            }
            return result;
        }

        private static LevelData Parse(int id, Spec spec)
        {
            int height = spec.Rows.Length;
            string[][] cells = new string[height][];
            int width = 0;
            for (int y = 0; y < height; y++)
            {
                cells[y] = spec.Rows[y].Split(new[] { ' ' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
                if (y == 0) width = cells[y].Length;
                else if (cells[y].Length != width) throw new FormatException("Level " + id + " row " + y + " has the wrong width");
            }

            var level = new LevelData
            {
                id = id,
                title = spec.Title,
                hint = spec.Hint,
                isTutorial = spec.Tutorial,
                width = width,
                height = height,
                tiles = new TileSpec[width * height],
                solution = new int[width * height]
            };

            for (int y = 0; y < height; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < width; x++)
                {
                    int solved;
                    int taps;
                    TileSpec tile = ParseCell(id, cells[y][x], out solved, out taps);
                    int i = y * width + x;
                    level.solution[i] = solved;
                    tile.rotation = tile.rotatable ? ((solved - taps) % 4 + 4) % 4 : solved;
                    level.tiles[i] = tile;
                }
            }
            return level;
        }

        private static TileSpec ParseCell(int levelId, string token, out int solvedRotation, out int taps)
        {
            taps = 0;
            string body = token;
            int colon = token.IndexOf(':');
            if (colon >= 0)
            {
                body = token.Substring(0, colon);
                taps = int.Parse(token.Substring(colon + 1));
            }

            bool rotatable = body.Length > 0 && !char.IsLower(body[0]) && body[0] != '.' && body[0] != '#';
            string key = body.ToUpperInvariant();
            solvedRotation = 0;

            switch (key)
            {
                case ".": return new TileSpec(TileKind.Empty, 0, false);
                case "#": return new TileSpec(TileKind.Block, 0, false);
                case "V": return new TileSpec(TileKind.Straight, 0, rotatable);
                case "H": solvedRotation = 1; return new TileSpec(TileKind.Straight, 1, rotatable);
                case "NE": return new TileSpec(TileKind.Corner, 0, rotatable);
                case "ES": solvedRotation = 1; return new TileSpec(TileKind.Corner, 1, rotatable);
                case "SW": solvedRotation = 2; return new TileSpec(TileKind.Corner, 2, rotatable);
                case "WN": solvedRotation = 3; return new TileSpec(TileKind.Corner, 3, rotatable);
            }

            if (key.Length == 2 && (key[0] == 'S' || key[0] == 'T') && "^><V".IndexOf(key[1]) >= 0)
            {
                // Source and target are written with their open side as an arrow.
                string arrow = body.Substring(1);
                Direction side = arrow == "^" ? Direction.North : arrow == ">" ? Direction.East : arrow == "v" ? Direction.South : Direction.West;
                solvedRotation = (int)side;
                return new TileSpec(key[0] == 'S' ? TileKind.Source : TileKind.Target, solvedRotation, false);
            }

            throw new FormatException("Level " + levelId + ": unknown cell '" + token + "'");
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Authoring data for one level. Pure data: no view or Unity types, so it is shared by the
    /// ScriptableObject, the catalog, the tests and the route validator.
    /// Cells are stored row-major: index = y * width + x, y = 0 is the top (north) row.
    /// </summary>
    [Serializable]
    public class LevelData
    {
        public int id;
        public string title;
        public string hint;
        public bool isTutorial;
        public int width;
        public int height;
        public TileSpec[] tiles;
        /// <summary>Known solution: one rotation (0-3) per cell. Only rotatable cells matter.</summary>
        public int[] solution;

        public int CellCount
        {
            get { return width * height; }
        }

        public bool InBounds(int x, int y)
        {
            return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < width && y < height;
        }

        public int IndexOf(int x, int y)
        {
            return y * width + x;
        }

        public TileSpec GetTile(int x, int y)
        {
            return tiles[IndexOf(x, y)];
        }

        public bool TryFind(TileKind kind, out GridPoint point)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < tiles.Length; i++)
            {
                if (tiles[i].kind == kind)
                {
                    point = new GridPoint(i % width, i / width);
                    return true;
                }
            }
            point = default(GridPoint);
            return false;
        }

        public int[] InitialRotations()
        {
            var result = new int[tiles.Length];
            for (int i = 0; i < tiles.Length; i++)
            {
                result[i] = tiles[i].rotation;
            }
            return result;
        }

        public int RotatableCount
        {
            get
            {
                int count = 0;
                for (int i = 0; i < tiles.Length; i++)
                {
                    if (tiles[i].rotatable) count++;
                }
                return count;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Minimum number of taps to reach the known solution's connections from the initial state
        /// (a straight only needs a 180 degree-equivalent orientation).
        /// </summary>
        public int MinimumTaps()
        {
            int taps = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < tiles.Length; i++)
            {
                TileSpec t = tiles[i];
                if (!t.rotatable) continue;
                for (int n = 0; n < 4; n++)
                {
                    int r = (t.rotation + n) & 3;
                    if (TileGeometry.Openings(t.kind, r) == TileGeometry.Openings(t.kind, solution[i]))
                    {
                        taps += n;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
            return taps;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Structural checks used by the editor tool and the unit tests.</summary>
    public static class LevelValidator
    {
        public const int MaxBoardSize = 5;

        /// <summary>Returns an empty list when the level is well formed and its solution works.</summary>
        public static List<string> Validate(LevelData level)
        {
            var errors = new List<string>();
            if (level == null)
            {
                errors.Add("level is null");
                return errors;
            }

            string name = "Level " + level.id;
            if (level.width < 2 || level.height < 2 || level.width > MaxBoardSize || level.height > MaxBoardSize)
            {
                errors.Add(name + ": board size " + level.width + "x" + level.height + " out of range");
                return errors;
            }

            int count = level.width * level.height;
            if (level.tiles == null || level.tiles.Length != count)
            {
                errors.Add(name + ": tiles array must have " + count + " entries");
                return errors;
            }
            if (level.solution == null || level.solution.Length != count)
            {
                errors.Add(name + ": solution array must have " + count + " entries");
                return errors;
            }

            int sources = 0;
            int targets = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                TileSpec t = level.tiles[i];
                if (t.rotation < 0 || t.rotation > 3) errors.Add(name + ": tile " + i + " has invalid rotation");
                if (level.solution[i] < 0 || level.solution[i] > 3) errors.Add(name + ": solution " + i + " has invalid rotation");
                if (t.kind == TileKind.Source) sources++;
                if (t.kind == TileKind.Target) targets++;
                if (t.rotatable && !TileGeometry.IsConveyor(t.kind)) errors.Add(name + ": only conveyors may be rotatable (tile " + i + ")");
                if (!t.rotatable && level.solution[i] != t.rotation) errors.Add(name + ": solution changes fixed tile " + i);
            }
            if (sources != 1) errors.Add(name + ": needs exactly one source, found " + sources);
            if (targets != 1) errors.Add(name + ": needs exactly one target, found " + targets);
            if (errors.Count > 0) return errors;

            RouteResult solved = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.solution);
            if (!solved.Success) errors.Add(name + ": known solution does not work (" + solved.Reason + ")");

            RouteResult initial = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.InitialRotations());
            if (initial.Success) errors.Add(name + ": level is already solved in its initial state");

            return errors;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/LevelManager.cs   (119 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    public enum LevelState
    {
        /// <summary>The player is editing belts.</summary>
        Idle,
        /// <summary>A parcel is travelling; input is locked.</summary>
        Running,
        Success,
        /// <summary>The last run failed; the view is showing why. Call <see cref="LevelManager.ReturnToEditing"/> afterwards.</summary>
        Failure
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Game state of the level being played: current rotations and the Idle/Running/Success/Failure
    /// state machine. Pure logic; the view reacts to its events.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class LevelManager
    {
        public LevelData Level { get; private set; }
        public int[] Rotations { get; private set; }
        public LevelState State { get; private set; }
        public RouteResult LastResult { get; private set; }
        /// <summary>Number of times Run was pressed on this level since it was loaded.</summary>
        public int Attempts { get; private set; }
        public int TapCount { get; private set; }

        public event Action<int, int> TileRotated;
        public event Action<LevelState> StateChanged;
        public event Action LevelReset;

        public void Load(LevelData level)
        {
            Level = level;
            Rotations = level.InitialRotations();
            Attempts = 0;
            TapCount = 0;
            LastResult = null;
            SetState(LevelState.Idle);
        }

        /// <summary>Rotates one tile by 90 degrees clockwise. Only possible while editing.</summary>
        public bool TryRotate(int x, int y)
        {
            if (State != LevelState.Idle || Level == null || !Level.InBounds(x, y))
            {
                return false;
            }

            int index = Level.IndexOf(x, y);
            if (!Level.tiles[index].rotatable)
            {
                return false;
            }

            Rotations[index] = (Rotations[index] + 1) & 3;
            TapCount++;
            if (TileRotated != null) TileRotated(x, y);
            return true;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Starts a run. Ignored (returns null) unless the level is idle, so a double press can never
        /// launch two parcels.
        /// </summary>
        public RouteResult Run()
        {
            if (State != LevelState.Idle || Level == null)
            {
                return null;
            }

            Attempts++;
            LastResult = RouteValidator.Validate(Level, Rotations);
            SetState(LevelState.Running);
            return LastResult;
        }

        /// <summary>Called by the view when the parcel animation has ended.</summary>
        public void CompleteRun()
        {
            if (State != LevelState.Running)
            {
                return;
            }
            SetState(LastResult != null && LastResult.Success ? LevelState.Success : LevelState.Failure);
        }

        public void ReturnToEditing()
        {
            if (State == LevelState.Failure)
            {
                SetState(LevelState.Idle);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Restores every tile to its starting rotation and goes back to editing, from any state.</summary>
        public void ResetLevel()
        {
            if (Level == null)
            {
                return;
            }
            Rotations = Level.InitialRotations();
            LastResult = null;
            SetState(LevelState.Idle);
            if (LevelReset != null) LevelReset();
        }

        private void SetState(LevelState state)
        {
            State = state;
            if (StateChanged != null) StateChanged(state);
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/RouteValidator.cs   (121 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    public enum FailureReason
    {
        None = 0,
        NoSource,
        /// <summary>The parcel left the board.</summary>
        OffBoard,
        /// <summary>The next cell has no tile.</summary>
        EmptyCell,
        /// <summary>The next cell is a decorative block.</summary>
        BlockedCell,
        /// <summary>The next tile is there but its connector does not face the parcel.</summary>
        WrongConnection,
        /// <summary>The parcel came back to a state it was already in.</summary>
        Loop,
        /// <summary>Safety net: the route was longer than any sane route.</summary>
        StepLimit
    }

    public sealed class RouteResult
    {
        public bool Success;
        public FailureReason Reason;
        /// <summary>Cells the parcel travels through, from the source to the last cell it entered.</summary>
        public readonly List<GridPoint> Path = new List<GridPoint>();
        /// <summary>The cell where it went wrong: the offending neighbour, or the last cell when leaving the board.</summary>
        public GridPoint FailCell;
        /// <summary>Direction of travel when the parcel failed (towards the offending side).</summary>
        public Direction FailDirection;
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// Pure route simulation. Follows connectors from the source one cell at a time; contains no
    /// view or animation code.
    /// </summary>
    public static class RouteValidator
    {
        public static RouteResult Validate(LevelData level, int[] rotations, int maxSteps = -1)
        {
            var result = new RouteResult();
            GridPoint start;
            if (!level.TryFind(TileKind.Source, out start))
            {
                result.Reason = FailureReason.NoSource;
                return result;
            }

            if (maxSteps < 0) maxSteps = level.CellCount * 4 + 4;

            GridPoint pos = start;
            result.Path.Add(pos);
            Direction heading = TileGeometry.TerminalSide(rotations[level.IndexOf(pos.x, pos.y)]);
            var seen = new HashSet<int>();
            int steps = 0;

            while (true)
            {
                steps++;
                if (steps > maxSteps)
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.StepLimit, pos, heading);
                }

                GridPoint next = pos.Step(heading);
                if (!level.InBounds(next.x, next.y))
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.OffBoard, pos, heading);
                }

                int index = level.IndexOf(next.x, next.y);
                TileKind kind = level.tiles[index].kind;
                if (kind == TileKind.Empty)
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.EmptyCell, next, heading);
                }
                if (kind == TileKind.Block)
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.BlockedCell, next, heading);
                }

                Direction entry = heading.Opposite();
                if (!TileGeometry.IsOpen(kind, rotations[index], entry))
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.WrongConnection, next, heading);
                }
                if (kind == TileKind.Source)
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.Loop, next, heading);
                }
                if (!seen.Add(index * 4 + (int)entry))
                {
                    return Fail(result, FailureReason.Loop, next, heading);
                }

                result.Path.Add(next);
                if (kind == TileKind.Target)
                {
                    result.Success = true;
                    result.Reason = FailureReason.None;
                    return result;
                }

                TileGeometry.TryGetExit(kind, rotations[index], entry, out heading);
                pos = next;
            }
        }

        private static RouteResult Fail(RouteResult result, FailureReason reason, GridPoint cell, Direction heading)
        {
            result.Success = false;
            result.Reason = reason;
            result.FailCell = cell;
            result.FailDirection = heading;
            return result;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/SaveService.cs   (288 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Text;

namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    /// <summary>Minimal string storage so progress can be tested without Unity (PlayerPrefs implements it at runtime).</summary>
    public interface IKeyValueStore
    {
        bool TryGetString(string key, out string value);
        void SetString(string key, string value);
        void DeleteKey(string key);
        void Flush();
    }

    public sealed class MemoryStore : IKeyValueStore
    {
        private readonly Dictionary<string, string> values = new Dictionary<string, string>();

        public bool TryGetString(string key, out string value)
        {
            return values.TryGetValue(key, out value);
        }

        public void SetString(string key, string value)
        {
            values[key] = value;
        }

        public void DeleteKey(string key)
        {
            values.Remove(key);
        }

        public void Flush()
        {
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Everything that persists between sessions.</summary>
    public sealed class ProgressData
    {
        /// <summary>1 = levels and settings, 2 = adds coins, stars, shop items, daily reward and ad counters.</summary>
        public const int SchemaVersion = 2;

        public int HighestUnlocked = 1;
        public int LastLevel = 1;
        public bool SoundOn = true;
        /// <summary>0 = quiet, 1 = medium, 2 = full.</summary>
        public int VolumeStep = 2;
        public readonly SortedSet<int> Completed = new SortedSet<int>();

        public int Coins;
        /// <summary>Best star rating (1-3) per completed level.</summary>
        public readonly SortedDictionary<int, int> Stars = new SortedDictionary<int, int>();
        /// <summary>Ids of owned shop items (free items are always owned, see ShopCatalog).</summary>
        public readonly SortedSet<string> Owned = new SortedSet<string>(StringComparer.Ordinal);
        public string Theme = ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme;
        public string Parcel = ShopCatalog.DefaultParcel;
        public int DailyStreak;
        /// <summary>Day number (see PlayerEconomy.DayNumber) of the last claimed daily gift; 0 = never.</summary>
        public int LastDailyDay;
        public int AdsDay;
        public int AdsToday;

        public bool IsUnlocked(int levelId)
        {
            return levelId >= 1 && levelId <= HighestUnlocked;
        }

        public bool IsCompleted(int levelId)
        {
            return Completed.Contains(levelId);
        }

        public int StarsFor(int levelId)
        {
            int stars;
            return Stars.TryGetValue(levelId, out stars) ? stars : 0;
        }

        public int TotalStars
        {
            get
            {
                int total = 0;
                foreach (int value in Stars.Values) total += value;
                return total;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Marks a level done and unlocks the next one (up to <paramref name="levelCount"/>).</summary>
        public void MarkCompleted(int levelId, int levelCount)
        {
            Completed.Add(levelId);
            int next = Math.Min(levelId + 1, levelCount);
            if (next > HighestUnlocked) HighestUnlocked = next;
        }

        public string Serialize()
        {
            var sb = new StringBuilder();
            sb.Append("v=").Append(SchemaVersion);
            sb.Append(";unlocked=").Append(HighestUnlocked);
            sb.Append(";last=").Append(LastLevel);
            sb.Append(";sound=").Append(SoundOn ? 1 : 0);
            sb.Append(";volume=").Append(VolumeStep);
            sb.Append(";completed=").Append(string.Join(",", Completed));
            sb.Append(";coins=").Append(Coins);
            var stars = new List<string>();
            foreach (KeyValuePair<int, int> pair in Stars) stars.Add(pair.Key + ":" + pair.Value);
            sb.Append(";stars=").Append(string.Join(",", stars));
            sb.Append(";owned=").Append(string.Join(",", Owned));
            sb.Append(";theme=").Append(Theme);
            sb.Append(";parcel=").Append(Parcel);
            sb.Append(";streak=").Append(DailyStreak);
            sb.Append(";daily=").Append(LastDailyDay);
            sb.Append(";adsday=").Append(AdsDay);
            sb.Append(";ads=").Append(AdsToday);
            return sb.ToString();
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Parses saved text (schema 1 or 2). Returns null if the text is empty, damaged or written by a newer
        /// schema, in which case the caller starts from defaults instead of crashing.
        /// </summary>
        public static ProgressData Parse(string text, int levelCount)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text)) return null;

            var map = new Dictionary<string, string>();
            foreach (string part in text.Split(';'))
            {
                int eq = part.IndexOf('=');
                if (eq <= 0) return null;
                map[part.Substring(0, eq)] = part.Substring(eq + 1);
            }

            int version;
            if (!TryInt(map, "v", out version) || version < 1 || version > SchemaVersion) return null;

            var data = new ProgressData();
            int value;
            if (TryInt(map, "unlocked", out value)) data.HighestUnlocked = Clamp(value, 1, levelCount);
            if (TryInt(map, "last", out value)) data.LastLevel = Clamp(value, 1, levelCount);
            if (TryInt(map, "sound", out value)) data.SoundOn = value != 0;
            if (TryInt(map, "volume", out value)) data.VolumeStep = Clamp(value, 0, 2);

            string list;
            if (map.TryGetValue("completed", out list) && list.Length > 0)
            {
                foreach (string item in list.Split(','))
                {
                    int id;
                    if (int.TryParse(item, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out id) && id >= 1 && id <= levelCount)
                    {
                        data.Completed.Add(id);
                    }
                }
            }

            // A completed level always implies the next one is unlocked.
            foreach (int id in data.Completed)
            {
                data.HighestUnlocked = Math.Max(data.HighestUnlocked, Math.Min(id + 1, levelCount));
            }
            data.LastLevel = Clamp(data.LastLevel, 1, data.HighestUnlocked);

            // Schema 2 fields; a schema 1 save simply has none of them.
            if (TryInt(map, "coins", out value)) data.Coins = Math.Max(0, value);
            if (map.TryGetValue("stars", out list) && list.Length > 0)
            {
                foreach (string item in list.Split(','))
                {
                    string[] pair = item.Split(':');
                    int id, stars;
                    if (pair.Length == 2 && int.TryParse(pair[0], NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out id) &&
                        int.TryParse(pair[1], NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out stars) &&
                        id >= 1 && id <= levelCount && stars >= 1 && stars <= 3)
                    {
                        data.Stars[id] = stars;
                    }
                }
            }
            if (map.TryGetValue("owned", out list) && list.Length > 0)
            {
                foreach (string item in list.Split(','))
                {
                    if (item.Length > 0) data.Owned.Add(item);
                }
            }
            string equipped;
            if (map.TryGetValue("theme", out equipped) && ShopCatalog.Find(equipped) != null && ShopCatalog.Find(equipped).Kind == ItemKind.Theme)
            {
                data.Theme = equipped;
            }
            if (map.TryGetValue("parcel", out equipped) && ShopCatalog.Find(equipped) != null && ShopCatalog.Find(equipped).Kind == ItemKind.Parcel)
            {
                data.Parcel = equipped;
            }
            // You can only wear what you own.
            if (!ShopCatalog.IsOwned(data.Owned, data.Theme)) data.Theme = ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme;
            if (!ShopCatalog.IsOwned(data.Owned, data.Parcel)) data.Parcel = ShopCatalog.DefaultParcel;
            if (TryInt(map, "streak", out value)) data.DailyStreak = Math.Max(0, value);
            if (TryInt(map, "daily", out value)) data.LastDailyDay = Math.Max(0, value);
            if (TryInt(map, "adsday", out value)) data.AdsDay = Math.Max(0, value);
            if (TryInt(map, "ads", out value)) data.AdsToday = Math.Max(0, value);
            return data;
        }

        private static bool TryInt(Dictionary<string, string> map, string key, out int value)
        {
            value = 0;
            string text;
            return map.TryGetValue(key, out text) && int.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Integer, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out value);
        }

        private static int Clamp(int value, int min, int max)
        {
            return value < min ? min : value > max ? max : value;
        }
    }

    public sealed class SaveService
    {
        public const string Key = "flowfactory.progress";

        private readonly IKeyValueStore store;
        private readonly int levelCount;

        public ProgressData Data { get; private set; }

        public int LevelCount
        {
            get { return levelCount; }
        }

        public SaveService(IKeyValueStore store, int levelCount)
        {
            this.store = store;
            this.levelCount = levelCount;
            Load();
        }

        public void Load()
        {
            string text;
            ProgressData parsed = store.TryGetString(Key, out text) ? ProgressData.Parse(text, levelCount) : null;
            Data = parsed ?? new ProgressData();
        }

        public void Save()
        {
            store.SetString(Key, Data.Serialize());
            store.Flush();
        }

        public void CompleteLevel(int levelId)
        {
            Data.MarkCompleted(levelId, levelCount);
            Save();
        }

        public void SetLastLevel(int levelId)
        {
            Data.LastLevel = levelId;
            Save();
        }

        /// <summary>Starts the levels over (unlocks and stars) but keeps coins, purchases and settings.</summary>
        public void ResetLevelProgress()
        {
            Data.Completed.Clear();
            Data.Stars.Clear();
            Data.HighestUnlocked = 1;
            Data.LastLevel = 1;
            Save();
        }

        public void ResetAll()
        {
            store.DeleteKey(Key);
            Data = new ProgressData();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Core/TileKind.cs   (110 lines)
====================================================================================================
namespace FlowFactory.Core
{
    public enum TileKind
    {
        Empty = 0,
        Straight = 1,
        Corner = 2,
        Source = 3,
        Target = 4,
        /// <summary>Decorative, impassable crate.</summary>
        Block = 5
    }

    /// <summary>The initial state of one board cell as authored in a level.</summary>
    [System.Serializable]
    public struct TileSpec
    {
        public TileKind kind;
        /// <summary>Clockwise quarter turns (0-3) applied to the tile's base connectors.</summary>
        public int rotation;
        /// <summary>Whether the player may rotate this tile.</summary>
        public bool rotatable;

        public TileSpec(TileKind kind, int rotation, bool rotatable)
        {
            this.kind = kind;
            this.rotation = rotation;
            this.rotatable = rotatable;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Connector geometry of every tile kind.</summary>
    public static class TileGeometry
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Bit mask of open sides (see <see cref="DirectionExtensions.Bit"/>) for a tile.
        /// Base orientation (rotation 0): Straight N+S, Corner N+E, Source N, Target N.
        /// </summary>
        public static int Openings(TileKind kind, int rotation)
        {
            int baseMask;
            switch (kind)
            {
                case TileKind.Straight:
                    baseMask = Direction.North.Bit() | Direction.South.Bit();
                    break;
                case TileKind.Corner:
                    baseMask = Direction.North.Bit() | Direction.East.Bit();
                    break;
                case TileKind.Source:
                case TileKind.Target:
                    baseMask = Direction.North.Bit();
                    break;
                default:
                    return 0;
            }

            int result = 0;
            for (int d = 0; d < 4; d++)
            {
                if ((baseMask & (1 << d)) != 0)
                {
                    result |= ((Direction)d).Rotate(rotation).Bit();
                }
            }
            return result;
        }

        public static bool IsOpen(TileKind kind, int rotation, Direction side)
        {
            return (Openings(kind, rotation) & side.Bit()) != 0;
        }

        public static bool IsConveyor(TileKind kind)
        {
            return kind == TileKind.Straight || kind == TileKind.Corner;
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// For a conveyor entered through <paramref name="entrySide"/>, finds the side it leaves through.
        /// Returns false if the tile is not open on the entry side.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool TryGetExit(TileKind kind, int rotation, Direction entrySide, out Direction exit)
        {
            exit = entrySide;
            if (!IsConveyor(kind) || !IsOpen(kind, rotation, entrySide))
            {
                return false;
            }

            int rest = Openings(kind, rotation) & ~entrySide.Bit();
            for (int d = 0; d < 4; d++)
            {
                if ((rest & (1 << d)) != 0)
                {
                    exit = (Direction)d;
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        }

        /// <summary>The single open side of a Source or Target.</summary>
        public static Direction TerminalSide(int rotation)
        {
            return Direction.North.Rotate(rotation);
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/AudioService.cs   (96 lines)
====================================================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    public enum Sfx
    {
        Click,
        Rotate,
        Run,
        Deliver,
        Success,
        Fail,
        Coin
    }

    /// <summary>Plays short procedurally generated sounds (no audio files needed). Respects the saved settings.</summary>
    public sealed class AudioService : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const int SampleRate = 44100;
        private static readonly float[] Volumes = { 0.25f, 0.6f, 1f };

        private AudioSource source;
        private AudioClip[] clips;

        public bool Enabled = true;
        public int VolumeStep = 2;

        private void Awake()
        {
            source = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioSource>();
            source.playOnAwake = false;
            source.spatialBlend = 0f;

            clips = new AudioClip[7];
            clips[(int)Sfx.Click] = Tone("click", 0.05f, 880f, 880f);
            clips[(int)Sfx.Rotate] = Tone("rotate", 0.07f, 520f, 640f);
            clips[(int)Sfx.Run] = Tone("run", 0.22f, 260f, 520f);
            clips[(int)Sfx.Deliver] = Tone("deliver", 0.16f, 330f, 200f);
            clips[(int)Sfx.Success] = Arpeggio("success", new[] { 523.25f, 659.25f, 783.99f, 1046.5f }, 0.11f);
            clips[(int)Sfx.Fail] = Tone("fail", 0.30f, 220f, 110f);
            clips[(int)Sfx.Coin] = Arpeggio("coin", new[] { 987.77f, 1318.5f }, 0.07f);
        }

        public void Play(Sfx sfx)
        {
            if (!Enabled || clips == null) return;
            float volume = Volumes[Mathf.Clamp(VolumeStep, 0, Volumes.Length - 1)];
            source.PlayOneShot(clips[(int)sfx], volume);
        }

        private static AudioClip Tone(string name, float seconds, float startHz, float endHz)
        {
            int count = Mathf.CeilToInt(seconds * SampleRate);
            var data = new float[count];
            float phase = 0f;
            for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                float t = i / (float)count;
                float hz = Mathf.Lerp(startHz, endHz, t);
                phase += 2f * Mathf.PI * hz / SampleRate;
                data[i] = Mathf.Sin(phase) * Envelope(t) * 0.5f;
            }
            return MakeClip(name, data);
        }

        private static AudioClip Arpeggio(string name, float[] notes, float noteSeconds)
        {
            int perNote = Mathf.CeilToInt(noteSeconds * SampleRate);
            var data = new float[perNote * notes.Length];
            for (int n = 0; n < notes.Length; n++)
            {
                float phase = 0f;
                for (int i = 0; i < perNote; i++)
                {
                    phase += 2f * Mathf.PI * notes[n] / SampleRate;
                    data[n * perNote + i] = Mathf.Sin(phase) * Envelope(i / (float)perNote) * 0.5f;
                }
            }
            return MakeClip(name, data);
        }

        /// <summary>Short fade in and out so clips never click.</summary>
        private static float Envelope(float t)
        {
            return Mathf.Clamp01(t / 0.08f) * Mathf.Clamp01((1f - t) / 0.5f);
        }

        private static AudioClip MakeClip(string name, float[] data)
        {
            AudioClip clip = AudioClip.Create(name, data.Length, 1, SampleRate, false);
            clip.SetData(data, 0);
            return clip;
        }
    }
}
