FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/Confetti.cs   (74 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>A short burst of coloured paper squares when a parcel is delivered. Primitives only, no particle system.</summary>
    public sealed class Confetti : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private static readonly Color[] Colors =
        {
            new Color(1.00f, 0.80f, 0.20f), new Color(0.95f, 0.40f, 0.35f), new Color(0.25f, 0.80f, 0.65f),
            new Color(0.35f, 0.65f, 0.95f), new Color(0.98f, 0.92f, 0.82f)
        };

        public void Burst(Vector3 origin)
        {
            StartCoroutine(GlowRing(origin));
            for (int i = 0; i < 28; i++)
            {
                StartCoroutine(Piece(origin));
            }
        }

        /// <summary>A golden light pulse that expands and fades on the floor where the parcel arrived.</summary>
        private IEnumerator GlowRing(Vector3 origin)
        {
            Material shared = GameMaterials.GetBlobShadow();
            if (shared == null) yield break;

            var material = new Material(shared);
            GameObject ring = Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Quad, transform, new Vector3(origin.x, 0.13f, origin.z), Vector3.one * 0.5f,
                material, Quaternion.Euler(90f, 0f, 0f), "GlowRing");
            const float life = 0.8f;
            for (float t = 0f; t < life; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                float k = t / life;
                float size = Mathf.Lerp(0.6f, 3.6f, 1f - (1f - k) * (1f - k));
                ring.transform.localScale = new Vector3(size, size, 1f);
                material.SetColor("_Color", new Color(1f, 0.85f, 0.35f, 0.85f * (1f - k)));
                yield return null;
            }
            Destroy(ring);
            Destroy(material);
        }

        public void Clear()
        {
            StopAllCoroutines();
            foreach (Transform child in transform)
            {
                Destroy(child.gameObject);
            }
        }

        private IEnumerator Piece(Vector3 origin)
        {
            GameObject piece = Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, transform, origin, new Vector3(0.09f, 0.02f, 0.06f),
                Colors[Random.Range(0, Colors.Length)], "Confetti");
            Vector3 velocity = new Vector3(Random.Range(-1.6f, 1.6f), Random.Range(2.6f, 4.4f), Random.Range(-1.6f, 1.6f));
            Vector3 spin = new Vector3(Random.Range(-540f, 540f), Random.Range(-540f, 540f), Random.Range(-540f, 540f));
            const float life = 1.3f;
            for (float t = 0f; t < life; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                velocity += Vector3.down * (9f * Time.deltaTime);
                piece.transform.position += velocity * Time.deltaTime;
                piece.transform.Rotate(spin * Time.deltaTime);
                piece.transform.localScale = new Vector3(0.09f, 0.02f, 0.06f) * Mathf.Clamp01((life - t) / 0.4f);
                yield return null;
            }
            Destroy(piece);
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/ConveyorTile.cs   (348 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The 3D look of one board tile, built from primitives. Pure presentation: whether a route works is
    /// decided by RouteValidator from the rotation data, never from this object.
    /// Local layout at rotation 0: straight runs north-south, corner opens north and east,
    /// source and target open to the north.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class ConveyorTile : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const float TurnSeconds = 0.14f;

        public TileKind Kind { get; private set; }
        public bool Rotatable { get; private set; }

        /// <summary>How fast belt rollers scroll while a parcel is on the route (texture units per second).</summary>
        private const float FlowUvSpeed = 1.6f;

        private sealed class BeltPart
        {
            public MeshRenderer Renderer;
            public Vector3 LocalDirection;
            public bool AlongZ;
            public float Flow;
            public float Offset;
        }

        private Transform visual;
        private readonly List<BeltPart> belts = new List<BeltPart>();
        private readonly List<Mesh> mergedMeshes = new List<Mesh>();
        private MaterialPropertyBlock block;
        private bool flowing;
        private readonly List<MeshRenderer> renderers = new List<MeshRenderer>();
        private readonly List<Material> originals = new List<Material>();
        private float currentAngle;
        private float targetAngle;
        private float pulse;
        private Coroutine flashRoutine;

        public void Init(TileSpec spec, int rotation)
        {
            Kind = spec.kind;
            Rotatable = spec.rotatable;

            visual = new GameObject("Visual").transform;
            visual.SetParent(transform, false);

            switch (spec.kind)
            {
                case TileKind.Straight:
                    BuildBelt(false);
                    break;
                case TileKind.Corner:
                    BuildBelt(true);
                    break;
                case TileKind.Source:
                    BuildSource();
                    break;
                case TileKind.Target:
                    BuildTarget();
                    break;
                case TileKind.Block:
                    BuildCrate();
                    break;
            }

            MergeStaticParts();
            foreach (MeshRenderer r in visual.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshRenderer>())
            {
                renderers.Add(r);
                originals.Add(r.sharedMaterial);
            }
            SnapTo(rotation);
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Combines all parts that never move on their own into one mesh per material, which cuts the number of
        /// draw calls on a phone from ~12 per tile to ~5. Belts stay separate because they scroll.
        /// Skipped (harmlessly) if the primitive meshes are not readable.
        /// </summary>
        private void MergeStaticParts()
        {
            if (!Primitives.MeshesReadable)
            {
                return;
            }

            var groups = new Dictionary<Material, List<CombineInstance>>();
            var consumed = new List<GameObject>();
            foreach (MeshFilter filter in visual.GetComponentsInChildren<MeshFilter>())
            {
                if (filter.sharedMesh == null || filter.gameObject.name == "Belt") continue;
                var meshRenderer = filter.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();
                List<CombineInstance> list;
                if (!groups.TryGetValue(meshRenderer.sharedMaterial, out list))
                {
                    list = new List<CombineInstance>();
                    groups[meshRenderer.sharedMaterial] = list;
                }
                list.Add(new CombineInstance
                {
                    mesh = filter.sharedMesh,
                    transform = visual.worldToLocalMatrix * filter.transform.localToWorldMatrix
                });
                consumed.Add(filter.gameObject);
            }

            foreach (KeyValuePair<Material, List<CombineInstance>> pair in groups)
            {
                var mesh = new Mesh { name = "FF_TileMerged" };
                mesh.CombineMeshes(pair.Value.ToArray(), true, true);
                mergedMeshes.Add(mesh);

                var go = new GameObject("Merged");
                go.transform.SetParent(visual, false);
                go.AddComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh = mesh;
                var meshRenderer = go.AddComponent<MeshRenderer>();
                meshRenderer.sharedMaterial = pair.Key;
                meshRenderer.shadowCastingMode = UnityEngine.Rendering.ShadowCastingMode.Off;
                meshRenderer.receiveShadows = false;
            }

            foreach (GameObject part in consumed)
            {
                part.SetActive(false);
                Destroy(part);
            }
        }

        private void OnDestroy()
        {
            foreach (Mesh mesh in mergedMeshes)
            {
                if (mesh != null) Destroy(mesh);
            }
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ belt flow

        /// <summary>
        /// Makes the belt rollers scroll along the parcel's route: towards the exit side, away from the entry side.
        /// Sides are world directions; <paramref name="rotation"/> is this tile's current logical rotation.
        /// </summary>
        public void SetFlow(Direction? entrySide, Direction? exitSide, int rotation)
        {
            foreach (BeltPart part in belts)
            {
                Direction arm = LocalToDirection(part.LocalDirection).Rotate(rotation);
                part.Flow = exitSide.HasValue && exitSide.Value == arm ? 1f : entrySide.HasValue && entrySide.Value == arm ? -1f : 0f;
            }
            flowing = belts.Count > 0;
        }

        public void StopFlow()
        {
            flowing = false;
            foreach (BeltPart part in belts)
            {
                part.Flow = 0f;
                part.Offset = 0f;
                part.Renderer.SetPropertyBlock(null);
            }
        }

        private static Direction LocalToDirection(Vector3 local)
        {
            if (local.z > 0.5f) return Direction.North;
            if (local.z < -0.5f) return Direction.South;
            return local.x > 0f ? Direction.East : Direction.West;
        }

        private void UpdateFlow()
        {
            if (block == null) block = new MaterialPropertyBlock();
            Vector2 signs = Primitives.CubeTopUvSigns;
            foreach (BeltPart part in belts)
            {
                if (part.Flow == 0f) continue;
                // A feature moves along +axis when the texture offset moves against the uv slope.
                float velocity = part.Flow * (part.AlongZ ? part.LocalDirection.z : part.LocalDirection.x);
                part.Offset += -(part.AlongZ ? signs.y : signs.x) * velocity * FlowUvSpeed * Time.deltaTime;
                part.Offset = Mathf.Repeat(part.Offset, 1f);
                block.SetVector("_MainTex_ST", part.AlongZ ? new Vector4(1f, 1f, 0f, part.Offset) : new Vector4(1f, 1f, part.Offset, 0f));
                part.Renderer.SetPropertyBlock(block);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Starts a smooth 90 degree clockwise turn.</summary>
        public void RotateStep()
        {
            targetAngle += 90f;
            pulse = 1f;
        }

        public void SnapTo(int rotation)
        {
            currentAngle = targetAngle = rotation * 90f;
            pulse = 0f;
            Apply();
        }

        public void Flash(Color color, float seconds)
        {
            if (flashRoutine != null) StopCoroutine(flashRoutine);
            flashRoutine = StartCoroutine(FlashRoutine(color, seconds));
        }

        private IEnumerator FlashRoutine(Color color, float seconds)
        {
            Material flash = GameMaterials.Get(color);
            for (int i = 0; i < renderers.Count; i++) renderers[i].sharedMaterial = flash;
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(seconds);
            for (int i = 0; i < renderers.Count; i++) renderers[i].sharedMaterial = originals[i];
            flashRoutine = null;
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (flowing)
            {
                UpdateFlow();
            }

            bool turning = !Mathf.Approximately(currentAngle, targetAngle);
            if (!turning && pulse <= 0f)
            {
                return;
            }

            currentAngle = Mathf.MoveTowards(currentAngle, targetAngle, 90f / TurnSeconds * Time.deltaTime);
            pulse = Mathf.Max(0f, pulse - Time.deltaTime / 0.25f);
            Apply();
        }

        private void Apply()
        {
            visual.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, currentAngle, 0f);
            float scale = 1f + 0.10f * Mathf.Sin(pulse * Mathf.PI);
            visual.localScale = new Vector3(scale, 1f, scale);
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ parts

        private GameObject Box(Vector3 position, Vector3 scale, Color color, string name, TextureKind texture = TextureKind.None, Vector2? tiling = null)
        {
            return Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, visual, position, scale, color, texture, tiling ?? Vector2.one, name);
        }

        private GameObject Cylinder(Vector3 position, Vector3 scale, Color color, string name, Quaternion? rotation = null)
        {
            return Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, visual, position, scale, GameMaterials.Get(color), rotation ?? Quaternion.identity, name);
        }

        private void BuildBelt(bool corner)
        {
            Color belt = Rotatable ? Palette.BeltRotatable : Palette.BeltFixed;
            Color rail = Rotatable ? Palette.RailRotatable : Palette.RailFixed;

            Vector3 second = corner ? Vector3.right : Vector3.back;
            // On a corner the rails facing the other arm are left out, otherwise they cross in the middle.
            AddArm(Vector3.forward, belt, rail, corner ? second : (Vector3?)null);
            AddArm(second, belt, rail, corner ? Vector3.forward : (Vector3?)null);

            if (corner)
            {
                // A round turntable where the two arms meet.
                Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.03f, 0f), new Vector3(0.58f, 0.06f, 0.58f), Palette.Frame, "Frame");
                Cylinder(new Vector3(0f, 0.09f, 0f), new Vector3(0.52f, 0.03f, 0.52f), belt, "Turntable");
                Cylinder(new Vector3(0f, 0.125f, 0f), new Vector3(0.30f, 0.01f, 0.30f), Color.Lerp(belt, Color.white, 0.25f), "TurntableCap");
            }
            if (Rotatable)
            {
                // A dial tells the player this belt can be turned.
                Cylinder(new Vector3(0f, 0.16f, 0f), new Vector3(0.22f, 0.03f, 0.22f), Palette.Knob, "Knob");
                Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.195f, 0f), new Vector3(0.16f, 0.02f, 0.05f), Palette.KnobHandle, "KnobHandle");
            }
        }

        private void AddArm(Vector3 direction, Color belt, Color rail, Vector3? skipRailToward = null)
        {
            Vector3 side = Vector3.Cross(Vector3.up, direction);
            bool alongZ = Mathf.Abs(direction.z) > 0.5f;
            Vector3 frameScale = alongZ ? new Vector3(0.58f, 0.06f, 0.54f) : new Vector3(0.54f, 0.06f, 0.58f);
            Vector3 beltScale = alongZ ? new Vector3(0.44f, 0.04f, 0.54f) : new Vector3(0.54f, 0.04f, 0.44f);
            Vector3 railScale = alongZ ? new Vector3(0.05f, 0.10f, 0.54f) : new Vector3(0.54f, 0.10f, 0.05f);

            Box(direction * 0.25f + Vector3.up * 0.03f, frameScale, Palette.Frame, "Frame");
            GameObject beltObject = Box(direction * 0.25f + Vector3.up * 0.085f, beltScale, belt, "Belt", alongZ ? TextureKind.Rollers : TextureKind.RollersH);
            belts.Add(new BeltPart { Renderer = beltObject.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>(), LocalDirection = direction, AlongZ = alongZ });

            bool skipPositive = skipRailToward.HasValue && Vector3.Dot(side, skipRailToward.Value) > 0.5f;
            bool skipNegative = skipRailToward.HasValue && Vector3.Dot(side, skipRailToward.Value) < -0.5f;
            if (!skipPositive) Box(direction * 0.25f + side * 0.255f + Vector3.up * 0.10f, railScale, rail, "Rail");
            if (!skipNegative) Box(direction * 0.25f - side * 0.255f + Vector3.up * 0.10f, railScale, rail, "Rail");

            // A roller at the tile edge, so neighbouring belts look connected.
            Quaternion axis = alongZ ? Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, 90f) : Quaternion.Euler(90f, 0f, 0f);
            Cylinder(direction * 0.47f + Vector3.up * 0.085f, new Vector3(0.10f, 0.22f, 0.10f), Palette.Metal, "Roller", axis);
        }

        private void BuildSource()
        {
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.04f, 0f), new Vector3(0.86f, 0.08f, 0.86f), Palette.Frame, "Plinth", TextureKind.Panel);
            // Low and set back so the waiting parcel is not hidden behind the machine in the isometric view.
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.23f, -0.17f), new Vector3(0.68f, 0.30f, 0.44f), Palette.Source, "Machine");
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.30f, 0.055f), new Vector3(0.70f, 0.10f, 0.02f), Palette.SourceStripe, "Stripe");
            Cylinder(new Vector3(-0.2f, 0.50f, -0.30f), new Vector3(0.14f, 0.14f, 0.14f), Palette.Metal, "Chimney");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Sphere, visual, new Vector3(0.2f, 0.42f, -0.28f), Vector3.one * 0.13f, Palette.Lamp, "Lamp");
            AddArm(Vector3.forward, Palette.BeltFixed, Palette.RailFixed);
        }

        private void BuildTarget()
        {
            // An open bin with a glowing drop zone: the parcel disappears into it.
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.04f, 0f), new Vector3(0.88f, 0.08f, 0.88f), Palette.Target, "Pad", TextureKind.Panel);
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.095f, 0f), new Vector3(0.46f, 0.03f, 0.46f), new Color(0.05f, 0.10f, 0.14f), "Hole");
            Cylinder(new Vector3(0f, 0.115f, 0f), new Vector3(0.34f, 0.01f, 0.34f), Palette.TargetGlow, "Glow");
            Color wall = Palette.TargetWall;
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.19f, -0.31f), new Vector3(0.66f, 0.22f, 0.06f), wall, "WallS");
            Box(new Vector3(-0.31f, 0.19f, 0f), new Vector3(0.06f, 0.22f, 0.56f), wall, "WallW");
            Box(new Vector3(0.31f, 0.19f, 0f), new Vector3(0.06f, 0.22f, 0.56f), wall, "WallE");
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.15f, 0.31f), new Vector3(0.66f, 0.10f, 0.06f), wall, "WallN");
            Cylinder(new Vector3(-0.34f, 0.36f, -0.34f), new Vector3(0.03f, 0.17f, 0.03f), Palette.Metal, "Pole");
            Box(new Vector3(-0.26f, 0.47f, -0.34f), new Vector3(0.16f, 0.10f, 0.02f), Palette.Knob, "Flag");
        }

        private void BuildCrate()
        {
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.31f, 0f), new Vector3(0.68f, 0.56f, 0.68f), Palette.Crate, "Crate", TextureKind.Wood);
            Color frame = Palette.CrateFrame;
            foreach (float sx in new[] { -0.35f, 0.35f })
            {
                foreach (float sz in new[] { -0.35f, 0.35f })
                {
                    Box(new Vector3(sx, 0.31f, sz), new Vector3(0.08f, 0.62f, 0.08f), frame, "Post");
                }
            }
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.60f, 0f), new Vector3(0.74f, 0.05f, 0.74f), frame, "Lid");
            Box(new Vector3(0f, 0.03f, 0f), new Vector3(0.74f, 0.06f, 0.74f), frame, "Foot");
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/FactoryAwakening.cs   (83 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Short, skippable animation for the last level: a small factory pops up next to the board,
    /// its windows light up and smoke rises. Primitives only.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class FactoryAwakening : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public bool SkipRequested;

        /// <summary>Removes the factory and any smoke (called when a level is left or restarted).</summary>
        public void Clear()
        {
            StopAllCoroutines();
            foreach (Transform child in transform)
            {
                Destroy(child.gameObject);
            }
        }

        public IEnumerator Play(Vector3 position)
        {
            SkipRequested = false;
            var root = new GameObject("Factory").transform;
            root.SetParent(transform, false);
            root.position = position;
            root.localScale = Vector3.zero;

            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, 0.45f, 0f), new Vector3(2.2f, 0.9f, 1.4f), new Color(0.62f, 0.66f, 0.74f), "Hall");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, 1.0f, 0f), new Vector3(2.3f, 0.2f, 1.5f), new Color(0.78f, 0.28f, 0.22f), "Roof");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, root, new Vector3(0.7f, 1.55f, 0.25f), new Vector3(0.38f, 0.65f, 0.38f), new Color(0.45f, 0.30f, 0.26f), "Chimney");
            var lamps = new[]
            {
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(-0.65f, 0.55f, -0.72f), new Vector3(0.38f, 0.30f, 0.05f), new Color(0.25f, 0.28f, 0.33f), "Window"),
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, 0.55f, -0.72f), new Vector3(0.38f, 0.30f, 0.05f), new Color(0.25f, 0.28f, 0.33f), "Window"),
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0.65f, 0.55f, -0.72f), new Vector3(0.38f, 0.30f, 0.05f), new Color(0.25f, 0.28f, 0.33f), "Window")
            };

            // Pop up with a little overshoot.
            const float grow = 0.6f;
            for (float t = 0f; t < grow && !SkipRequested; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                float k = t / grow;
                float overshoot = 1f + 0.18f * Mathf.Sin(k * Mathf.PI);
                root.localScale = Vector3.one * Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, k) * overshoot;
                yield return null;
            }
            root.localScale = Vector3.one;

            // Windows light up one after another.
            Material lit = GameMaterials.Get(new Color(1f, 0.92f, 0.45f));
            for (int i = 0; i < lamps.Length; i++)
            {
                lamps[i].GetComponent<MeshRenderer>().sharedMaterial = lit;
                if (!SkipRequested) yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.18f);
            }

            // Smoke puffs.
            for (int puff = 0; puff < 4 && !SkipRequested; puff++)
            {
                StartCoroutine(Smoke(root.position + new Vector3(0.7f, 1.9f, 0.25f)));
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.25f);
            }
            if (!SkipRequested) yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.5f);
        }

        private IEnumerator Smoke(Vector3 start)
        {
            GameObject puff = Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Sphere, transform, start, Vector3.one * 0.25f, new Color(0.88f, 0.9f, 0.94f), "Smoke");
            for (float t = 0f; t < 1.4f; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                puff.transform.position = start + new Vector3(0.1f * t, 1.1f * t, 0f);
                puff.transform.localScale = Vector3.one * Mathf.Lerp(0.25f, 0.7f, t / 1.4f) * (1f - t / 1.4f * 0.6f);
                yield return null;
            }
            Destroy(puff);
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/FlowFactoryApp.cs   (825 lines)

using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Wires everything together: camera, UI, board, parcel, audio, save and telemetry, and drives the
    /// flow between menu, level select, play and settings. One generic game; levels are loaded as data.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class FlowFactoryApp : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private enum AppScreen
        {
            Menu,
            LevelSelect,
            Playing,
            Settings
        }

        private const float ParcelSpeed = 4.2f;
        private const int TutorialLastLevel = 2;

        private IList<LevelData> levels;
        private SaveService save;
        private PlayerEconomy economy;
        private MonetizationUi monetizationUi;
        private MonetizationController money;
        private LevelReward lastReward;
        private bool dailyOffered;
        private LocalTelemetry telemetry;
        private AudioService audioService;
        private GameUIController ui;
        private GridManager grid;
        private ParcelController parcel;
        private FactoryAwakening awakening;
        private Confetti confetti;
        private Coroutine demoRoutine;
        private FpsCounter fps;
        private Camera cam;
        private readonly LevelManager manager = new LevelManager();

        private AppScreen screen = AppScreen.Menu;
        private int levelIndex;
        private float levelStartTime;
        private Coroutine runRoutine;
        private bool awakeningRunning;
        private bool tutorialStartLogged;
        private bool tutorialCompleteLogged;
        private int lastScreenWidth;
        private int lastScreenHeight;

        private const string Smooth60Key = "ff.smooth60";
        private bool smooth60;

        private static FlowFactoryApp instance;

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- setup

        private void Awake()
        {
            if (instance != null && instance != this)
            {
                Destroy(gameObject);
                return;
            }
            instance = this;

            smooth60 = PlayerPrefs.GetInt(Smooth60Key, 0) == 1;
            ApplyFrameRate();
            if (Application.isMobilePlatform)
            {
                QualitySettings.vSyncCount = 0;
                Screen.sleepTimeout = SleepTimeout.NeverSleep;
                Screen.orientation = ScreenOrientation.Portrait;
            }
            // The scene is simple: no shadows, no MSAA, and a render-resolution cap on very dense screens.
            QualitySettings.shadows = ShadowQuality.Disable;
            QualitySettings.antiAliasing = 0;
#if UNITY_ANDROID && !UNITY_EDITOR
            if (Screen.width > 1080)
            {
                int height = Mathf.RoundToInt(Screen.height * 1080f / Screen.width);
                Screen.SetResolution(1080, height, true);
            }
#endif

            levels = LevelRepository.LoadAll();
            save = new SaveService(new PlayerPrefsStore(), levels.Count);
            economy = new PlayerEconomy(save);
            ThemeCatalog.Apply(save.Data.Theme, save.Data.Parcel);
            telemetry = new LocalTelemetry();
            telemetry.Log("session_start", "version=0.1 levels=" + levels.Count);

            SetUpCamera();

            audioService = gameObject.AddComponent<AudioService>();
            audioService.Enabled = save.Data.SoundOn;
            audioService.VolumeStep = save.Data.VolumeStep;

            InputBridge.EnsureEventSystem();

            ui = new GameObject("UI").AddComponent<GameUIController>();
            ui.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            ui.Build();

            monetizationUi = new GameObject("MonetizationUi").AddComponent<MonetizationUi>();
            monetizationUi.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            monetizationUi.Build(ui.SafeRoot);
            money = new MonetizationController(economy, monetizationUi, ui, new SimulatedAdService(monetizationUi),
                new SimulatedStoreService(monetizationUi), telemetry, audioService);

            HookUi();

            grid = new GameObject("Grid").AddComponent<GridManager>();
            grid.transform.SetParent(transform, false);

            parcel = new GameObject("Parcel").AddComponent<ParcelController>();
            parcel.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            parcel.Build();
            parcel.Hide();
            money.AppearanceChanged += () => parcel.Build();
            money.ShopClosed += ShowMenu;

            awakening = new GameObject("Awakening").AddComponent<FactoryAwakening>();
            awakening.transform.SetParent(transform, false);

            confetti = new GameObject("Confetti").AddComponent<Confetti>();
            confetti.transform.SetParent(transform, false);

            // The factory floor stays in place behind whatever board or menu demo is showing.
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, transform, new Vector3(0f, -0.62f, 0f), new Vector3(90f, 0.1f, 90f),
                GameMaterials.GetGround(), Quaternion.identity, "Ground");

            fps = gameObject.AddComponent<FpsCounter>();
            fps.enabled = FpsCounter.Available;

            manager.TileRotated += (x, y) =>
            {
                grid.RotateTile(x, y);
                UpdateHint();
            };
            manager.LevelReset += () =>
            {
                grid.SnapRotations(manager.Rotations);
                UpdateHint();
            };

            ShowMenu();

            string tourFolder;
            if (ScreenshotTour.Requested(out tourFolder))
            {
                StartCoroutine(ScreenshotTour.Run(this, tourFolder));
            }
        }

        private void ApplyFrameRate()
        {
            // 30 fps is stable and cool on mid-range phones; 60 fps is an opt-in for strong devices.
            Application.targetFrameRate = smooth60 ? 60 : (Application.isMobilePlatform ? 30 : 60);
        }

        private void SetUpCamera()
        {
            cam = Camera.main;
            if (cam == null)
            {
                var camObject = new GameObject("Main Camera") { tag = "MainCamera" };
                cam = camObject.AddComponent<Camera>();
                camObject.AddComponent<AudioListener>();
            }
            cam.orthographic = true;
            cam.clearFlags = CameraClearFlags.SolidColor;
            cam.backgroundColor = Palette.Background;
            cam.nearClipPlane = 0.1f;
            cam.farClipPlane = 120f;

            if (Object.FindFirstObjectByType<Light>() == null)
            {
                var lightObject = new GameObject("Light");
                var light = lightObject.AddComponent<Light>();
                light.type = LightType.Directional;
                light.shadows = LightShadows.None;
                lightObject.transform.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(50f, -30f, 0f);
            }
        }

        private void HookUi()
        {
            ui.AnyButton += () => audioService.Play(Sfx.Click);
            ui.PlayPressed += OnPlay;
            ui.LevelsPressed += ShowLevelSelect;
            ui.SettingsPressed += ShowSettings;
            ui.ShopPressed += () => money.OpenShop();
            ui.HintPressed += OnHint;
            ui.DoubleCoinsPressed += OnDoubleCoins;
            ui.MenuPressed += ShowMenu;
            ui.LevelChosen += id => StartLevel(id - 1);
            ui.RunPressed += OnRun;
            ui.ResetPressed += OnReset;
            ui.BackPressed += ShowLevelSelect;
            ui.NextPressed += () => StartLevel(levelIndex + 1);
            ui.ReplayPressed += () => StartLevel(levelIndex);
            ui.SoundToggled += () =>
            {
                save.Data.SoundOn = !save.Data.SoundOn;
                audioService.Enabled = save.Data.SoundOn;
                save.Save();
                ui.RefreshSettings(save.Data, fps.Visible, smooth60);
            };
            ui.VolumeCycled += () =>
            {
                save.Data.VolumeStep = (save.Data.VolumeStep + 1) % 3;
                audioService.VolumeStep = save.Data.VolumeStep;
                save.Save();
                ui.RefreshSettings(save.Data, fps.Visible, smooth60);
            };
            ui.FrameRateToggled += () =>
            {
                smooth60 = !smooth60;
                PlayerPrefs.SetInt(Smooth60Key, smooth60 ? 1 : 0);
                ApplyFrameRate();
                ui.RefreshSettings(save.Data, fps.Visible, smooth60);
            };
            ui.FpsToggled += () =>
            {
                fps.Visible = !fps.Visible;
                ui.RefreshSettings(save.Data, fps.Visible, smooth60);
            };
            ui.ProgressResetConfirmed += () =>
            {
                save.ResetLevelProgress();
                tutorialStartLogged = false;
                tutorialCompleteLogged = false;
            };
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- screens

        private void ShowMenu()
        {
            LeaveLevel();
            screen = AppScreen.Menu;
            StartDemo();
            ui.ShowMenu();
            money.RefreshBadges();
            if (!dailyOffered && money.DailyAvailable)
            {
                dailyOffered = true;
                money.ShowDailyGift();
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Behind the main menu: the last level, solved, with a parcel making its trip over and over.</summary>
        private void StartDemo()
        {
            LevelData demo = levels[levels.Count - 1];
            grid.Build(demo, demo.solution, false);
            ConfigureCamera(demo, 0.57f);
            demoRoutine = StartCoroutine(DemoLoop(demo, RouteValidator.Validate(demo, demo.solution)));
        }

        private IEnumerator DemoLoop(LevelData demo, RouteResult route)
        {
            List<Vector3> points = grid.BuildWaypoints(route);
            Vector3 start = ParcelStartFor(demo, demo.solution);
            GridPoint end = route.Path[route.Path.Count - 1];
            while (true)
            {
                parcel.PlaceAt(start);
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.8f);
                yield return parcel.Travel(points, 3.4f);
                yield return parcel.Deliver(grid.CellCenter(end));
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(1.0f);
            }
        }

        private void ShowLevelSelect()
        {
            LeaveLevel();
            screen = AppScreen.LevelSelect;
            ui.ShowLevelSelect(levels, save.Data);
        }

        private void ShowSettings()
        {
            screen = AppScreen.Settings;
            ui.ShowSettings(save.Data, fps.Visible, smooth60);
        }

        private void LeaveLevel()
        {
            if (demoRoutine != null)
            {
                StopCoroutine(demoRoutine);
                demoRoutine = null;
            }
            StopRun();
            confetti.Clear();
            ui.HideSuccess();
            awakening.Clear();
            parcel.Hide();
            grid.Clear();
        }

        private void OnPlay()
        {
            ProgressData data = save.Data;
            int target = data.IsUnlocked(data.LastLevel) && !data.IsCompleted(data.LastLevel) ? data.LastLevel : data.HighestUnlocked;
            StartLevel(target - 1);
        }

        private void StartLevel(int index)
        {
            if (index < 0 || index >= levels.Count || !save.Data.IsUnlocked(index + 1))
            {
                ShowLevelSelect();
                return;
            }

            LeaveLevel();
            levelIndex = index;
            LevelData level = levels[index];

            manager.Load(level);
            grid.Build(level, manager.Rotations);
            ConfigureCamera(level);
            parcel.PlaceAt(ParcelStart());
            save.SetLastLevel(level.id);

            screen = AppScreen.Playing;
            ui.ShowHud(level);
            ui.SetRunInteractable(true);
            UpdateHint();

            levelStartTime = Time.realtimeSinceStartup;
            telemetry.Log("level_start", "level=" + level.id);
            if (level.id == 1 && !tutorialStartLogged && !save.Data.IsCompleted(1))
            {
                tutorialStartLogged = true;
                telemetry.Log("tutorial_start");
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- camera

        /// <summary>Fixed isometric view: orthographic, 45 degrees around, tilted down, fitted to the board width.</summary>
        private void ConfigureCamera(LevelData level, float boardScreenY = 0.55f)
        {
            lastScreenWidth = Screen.width;
            lastScreenHeight = Screen.height;

            Transform t = cam.transform;
            t.rotation = Quaternion.Euler(52f, 45f, 0f);
            t.position = -t.forward * 40f;

            float halfW = level.width * 0.5f;
            float halfH = level.height * 0.5f;
            float minX = float.MaxValue, maxX = float.MinValue, minY = float.MaxValue, maxY = float.MinValue;
            for (int cx = -1; cx <= 1; cx += 2)
            {
                for (int cz = -1; cz <= 1; cz += 2)
                {
                    foreach (float y in new[] { -0.5f, 1.2f })
                    {
                        Vector3 local = t.InverseTransformPoint(new Vector3(cx * halfW, y, cz * halfH));
                        minX = Mathf.Min(minX, local.x);
                        maxX = Mathf.Max(maxX, local.x);
                        minY = Mathf.Min(minY, local.y);
                        maxY = Mathf.Max(maxY, local.y);
                    }
                }
            }

            float aspect = Mathf.Max(0.2f, (float)Screen.width / Mathf.Max(1, Screen.height));
            float size = Mathf.Max((maxX - minX) * 0.5f / aspect + 0.9f, (maxY - minY));
            cam.orthographicSize = size;
            t.position += -t.up * (size * 2f * (boardScreenY - 0.5f));

            Vector3 flatForward = new Vector3(t.forward.x, 0f, t.forward.z).normalized;
            // The factory goes in front of the board (towards the camera), where the screen is empty,
            // so it does not cover the title and hint at the top.
            awakeningPosition = -flatForward * (Mathf.Max(level.width, level.height) * 0.55f + 2.4f);
        }

        private Vector3 awakeningPosition;

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- per-frame

        private void Update()
        {
            if (InputBridge.BackPressed() && !money.OverlayOpen)
            {
                HandleBack();
            }

            if (screen != AppScreen.Playing || money.OverlayOpen)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (Screen.width != lastScreenWidth || Screen.height != lastScreenHeight)
            {
                ConfigureCamera(manager.Level);
            }

            Vector2 press;
            if (!InputBridge.TryGetPress(out press))
            {
                return;
            }

            if (awakeningRunning)
            {
                awakening.SkipRequested = true;
                return;
            }
            if (ui.IsBlockingPoint(press) || manager.State != LevelState.Idle)
            {
                return;
            }

            GridPoint cell;
            if (grid.TryPickCell(cam, press, out cell) && manager.TryRotate(cell.x, cell.y))
            {
                audioService.Play(Sfx.Rotate);
            }
        }

        private void HandleBack()
        {
            switch (screen)
            {
                case AppScreen.Playing:
                    ShowLevelSelect();
                    break;
                case AppScreen.LevelSelect:
                case AppScreen.Settings:
                    ShowMenu();
                    break;
                default:
                    Application.Quit();
                    break;
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- playing

        private Vector3 ParcelStart()
        {
            return ParcelStartFor(levels[levelIndex], manager.Rotations);
        }

        private Vector3 ParcelStartFor(LevelData level, int[] rotations)
        {
            GridPoint source;
            level.TryFind(TileKind.Source, out source);
            Direction side = TileGeometry.TerminalSide(rotations[level.IndexOf(source.x, source.y)]);
            return grid.CellCenter(source) + GridManager.WorldDirection(side) * 0.42f + Vector3.up * GridManager.ParcelHeight;
        }

        private void UpdateHint()
        {
            LevelData level = manager.Level;
            if (level == null || manager.State != LevelState.Idle)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (!level.isTutorial)
            {
                ui.SetHint(level.hint);
                grid.HideHint();
                ui.SetRunPulse(false);
                return;
            }

            // Tutorial: point at the next belt that still faces the wrong way, then at the Run button.
            GridPoint cell;
            if (TryFindHintTile(out cell))
            {
                grid.ShowHint(cell);
                ui.SetHint("Tap the glowing belt to turn it.");
                ui.SetRunPulse(false);
                return;
            }
            grid.HideHint();
            ui.SetHint("Now press RUN!");
            ui.SetRunPulse(true);
        }

        /// <summary>The first belt that does not yet match the known solution.</summary>
        private bool TryFindHintTile(out GridPoint cell)
        {
            LevelData level = manager.Level;
            for (int i = 0; i < level.tiles.Length; i++)
            {
                TileSpec tile = level.tiles[i];
                if (tile.rotatable && TileGeometry.Openings(tile.kind, manager.Rotations[i]) != TileGeometry.Openings(tile.kind, level.solution[i]))
                {
                    cell = new GridPoint(i % level.width, i / level.width);
                    return true;
                }
            }
            cell = default(GridPoint);
            return false;
        }

        private void OnHint()
        {
            if (screen != AppScreen.Playing || manager.State != LevelState.Idle)
            {
                return;
            }

            GridPoint cell;
            bool available = TryFindHintTile(out cell);
            money.RequestHint(available, () =>
            {
                // Recompute: the board is unchanged while the dialog was open, but stay safe.
                GridPoint target;
                if (manager.State == LevelState.Idle && TryFindHintTile(out target))
                {
                    grid.ShowHint(target);
                    ui.SetHint("Tap the glowing belt to turn it.");
                }
            });
        }

        private void OnDoubleCoins()
        {
            money.OfferDoubleCoins(lastReward.Coins, added => ui.ShowDoubled(added));
        }

        private void OnRun()
        {
            if (screen != AppScreen.Playing || manager.State != LevelState.Idle)
            {
                return;
            }

            RouteResult result = manager.Run();
            if (result == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (manager.Attempts > 1)
            {
                telemetry.Log("level_retry", "level=" + manager.Level.id + " attempt=" + manager.Attempts);
            }

            audioService.Play(Sfx.Run);
            grid.StartFlow(result, manager.Rotations);
            grid.HideHint();
            ui.SetRunPulse(false);
            ui.HideToast();
            ui.SetRunInteractable(false);
            runRoutine = StartCoroutine(RunRoutine(result));
        }

        private void OnReset()
        {
            if (screen != AppScreen.Playing)
            {
                return;
            }

            StopRun();
            grid.StopFlow();
            awakening.Clear();
            telemetry.Log("level_retry", "level=" + manager.Level.id + " reason=reset");
            manager.ResetLevel();
            parcel.PlaceAt(ParcelStart());
            ui.HideToast();
            ui.SetRunInteractable(true);
            UpdateHint();
        }

        private void StopRun()
        {
            if (runRoutine != null)
            {
                StopCoroutine(runRoutine);
                runRoutine = null;
            }
            awakeningRunning = false;
        }

        private IEnumerator RunRoutine(RouteResult result)
        {
            LevelData level = manager.Level;
            List<Vector3> waypoints = grid.BuildWaypoints(result);
            yield return parcel.Travel(waypoints, ParcelSpeed);

            if (result.Success)
            {
                yield return parcel.Deliver(grid.CellCenter(result.Path[result.Path.Count - 1]));
                grid.FlashCell(result.Path[result.Path.Count - 1], Palette.FlashOk, 0.5f);
                confetti.Burst(grid.CellCenter(result.Path[result.Path.Count - 1]) + Vector3.up * 0.4f);
                audioService.Play(Sfx.Deliver);
                audioService.Play(Sfx.Success);
                manager.CompleteRun();
                grid.StopFlow();

                lastReward = economy.AwardLevel(level, manager.TapCount);
                money.RefreshBadges();
                audioService.Play(Sfx.Coin);
                telemetry.Log("level_success", "level=" + level.id + " seconds=" + (Time.realtimeSinceStartup - levelStartTime).ToString("0.0") +
                                               " attempts=" + manager.Attempts + " taps=" + manager.TapCount + " stars=" + lastReward.Stars + " coins=" + lastReward.Coins);
                if (level.id == TutorialLastLevel && !tutorialCompleteLogged)
                {
                    tutorialCompleteLogged = true;
                    telemetry.Log("tutorial_complete");
                }

                bool last = levelIndex == levels.Count - 1;
                bool worldEnd = level.id % GameUIController.LevelsPerWorld == 0;
                if (worldEnd)
                {
                    awakeningRunning = true;
                    yield return awakening.Play(awakeningPosition);
                    awakeningRunning = false;
                }
                else
                {
                    yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.45f);
                }
                ui.ShowSuccess(level, !last, worldEnd, last, lastReward, money.CanOfferAd() && lastReward.Coins > 0, money.ShopNudge());
            }
            else
            {
                yield return FailureRoutine(result);
                grid.StopFlow();
                manager.CompleteRun();
                yield return new WaitForSeconds(0.6f);
                manager.ReturnToEditing();
                parcel.PlaceAt(ParcelStart());
                ui.SetRunInteractable(true);
                UpdateHint();
            }
            runRoutine = null;
        }

        private IEnumerator FailureRoutine(RouteResult result)
        {
            Vector3 lastCenter = grid.CellCenter(result.Path[result.Path.Count - 1]) + Vector3.up * GridManager.ParcelHeight;
            Vector3 heading = GridManager.WorldDirection(result.FailDirection);

            if (result.Reason == FailureReason.OffBoard)
            {
                yield return parcel.Travel(new[] { lastCenter + heading * 0.5f }, ParcelSpeed);
                audioService.Play(Sfx.Fail);
                yield return parcel.FallOff(heading);
            }
            else
            {
                yield return parcel.Travel(new[] { lastCenter + heading * 0.3f }, ParcelSpeed);
                grid.FlashCell(result.FailCell, Palette.FlashFail, 0.7f);
                audioService.Play(Sfx.Fail);
                yield return parcel.Bump(heading);
            }
            ui.ShowToast(FailureMessage(result.Reason));
        }

        private static string FailureMessage(FailureReason reason)
        {
            switch (reason)
            {
                case FailureReason.WrongConnection: return "The belts don't line up here. Try turning one!";
                case FailureReason.EmptyCell: return "Nothing here to carry the parcel.";
                case FailureReason.BlockedCell: return "A crate is in the way!";
                case FailureReason.OffBoard: return "Oops! The parcel fell off the board.";
                case FailureReason.Loop:
                case FailureReason.StepLimit: return "The parcel went in circles.";
                default: return "The parcel got stuck. Try again!";
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- automation
        // Used by the PlayMode tests and the screenshot tour. The game itself never calls these.

        public static FlowFactoryApp Instance
        {
            get { return instance; }
        }

        public int LevelCount
        {
            get { return levels.Count; }
        }

        public SaveService Progress
        {
            get { return save; }
        }

        public PlayerEconomy Economy
        {
            get { return economy; }
        }

        public string ScreenName
        {
            get { return screen.ToString(); }
        }

        public LevelState CurrentLevelState
        {
            get { return manager.State; }
        }

        public int CurrentLevelId
        {
            get { return manager.Level != null ? manager.Level.id : 0; }
        }

        public int CurrentTapCount
        {
            get { return manager.TapCount; }
        }

        public bool SuccessCardOpen
        {
            get { return ui.OverlayOpen; }
        }

        public bool AnyMoneyOverlayOpen
        {
            get { return money.OverlayOpen; }
        }

        public void Automation_ResetEverything()
        {
            save.ResetAll();
            ThemeCatalog.Apply(save.Data.Theme, save.Data.Parcel);
            parcel.Build();
            dailyOffered = true;
            ShowMenu();
        }

        public void Automation_UnlockAll()
        {
            save.Data.HighestUnlocked = levels.Count;
            save.Save();
        }

        public void Automation_ShowMenu() { ShowMenu(); }
        public void Automation_ShowLevelSelect() { ShowLevelSelect(); }
        public void Automation_ShowSettings() { ShowSettings(); }
        public void Automation_OpenLevel(int levelId) { StartLevel(levelId - 1); }
        public void Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab tab) { money.OpenShop(tab); }
        public void Automation_ShowDailyGift() { money.ShowDailyGift(); }
        public void Automation_Next() { StartLevel(levelIndex + 1); }
        public void Automation_PressRun() { OnRun(); }
        public void Automation_PressHint() { OnHint(); }

        /// <summary>Taps every belt that still faces the wrong way (through the normal rotate path), then presses Run.</summary>
        public void Automation_ApplySolutionTaps()
        {
            LevelData level = manager.Level;
            for (int i = 0; i < level.tiles.Length; i++)
            {
                TileSpec tile = level.tiles[i];
                if (!tile.rotatable) continue;
                int guard = 0;
                while (guard++ < 4 && TileGeometry.Openings(tile.kind, manager.Rotations[i]) != TileGeometry.Openings(tile.kind, level.solution[i]))
                {
                    if (!manager.TryRotate(i % level.width, i / level.width)) break;
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Solves the open level like a player and waits until the success card is showing. Returns false on timeout.</summary>
        public IEnumerator Automation_SolveAndWait(float timeoutSeconds = 60f)
        {
            Automation_ApplySolutionTaps();
            yield return null;
            OnRun();
            float waited = 0f;
            while (!ui.OverlayOpen && waited < timeoutSeconds)
            {
                waited += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                yield return null;
            }
            AutomationResult = ui.OverlayOpen;
        }

        public bool AutomationResult { get; private set; }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle

        private void OnApplicationPause(bool paused)
        {
            if (telemetry == null)
            {
                return;
            }

            if (paused)
            {
                save.Save();
                telemetry.Log("session_end", "reason=pause");
            }
            else
            {
                telemetry.Reopen();
                telemetry.Log("session_start", "resume=1");
            }
        }

        private void OnApplicationQuit()
        {
            if (telemetry == null)
            {
                return;
            }
            save.Save();
            telemetry.Log("session_end", "reason=quit");
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Small FPS read-out for device testing. Only exists in the editor and development builds.</summary>
    public sealed class FpsCounter : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public static bool Available
        {
            get { return Debug.isDebugBuild || Application.isEditor; }
        }

        public bool Visible = true;

        private float smoothed = 30f;
        private float worstFrame;
        private float windowStart;
        private float shown;
        private GUIStyle style;

        private void Update()
        {
            float dt = Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            if (dt <= 0f) return;
            smoothed = Mathf.Lerp(smoothed, 1f / dt, 0.1f);
            worstFrame = Mathf.Max(worstFrame, dt);
            if (Time.unscaledTime - windowStart > 1f)
            {
                windowStart = Time.unscaledTime;
                shown = worstFrame * 1000f;
                worstFrame = 0f;
            }
        }

        private void OnGUI()
        {
            if (!Available || !Visible) return;
            if (style == null)
            {
                style = new GUIStyle(GUI.skin.label) { alignment = TextAnchor.UpperRight, fontStyle = FontStyle.Bold };
                style.normal.textColor = Color.yellow;
            }
            style.fontSize = Mathf.Max(18, Screen.height / 70);
            Rect safe = Screen.safeArea;
            var rect = new Rect(safe.xMax - 320f, Screen.height - safe.yMax + 4f, 316f, style.fontSize * 2.4f);
            GUI.Label(rect, smoothed.ToString("0") + " FPS\nworst " + shown.ToString("0") + " ms", style);
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Starts the game in whatever scene is open, so pressing Play works even in an empty project scene.
    /// The generic game "scene" is built at runtime from level data; there is one app object, not 12 scenes.
    /// </summary>
    public static class GameBootstrap
    {
        [RuntimeInitializeOnLoadMethod(RuntimeInitializeLoadType.AfterSceneLoad)]
        private static void Start()
        {
            if (Object.FindFirstObjectByType<FlowFactoryApp>() != null)
            {
                return;
            }
            new GameObject("FlowFactoryApp").AddComponent<FlowFactoryApp>();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/GameMaterials.cs   (267 lines)

using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Colour (and optionally textured) materials, one shared instance per combination.</summary>
    public static class GameMaterials
    {
        private static Shader shader;
        private static Shader groundShader;
        private static readonly Dictionary<string, Material> Cache = new Dictionary<string, Material>();

        public static Material Get(Color color)
        {
            return Get(color, TextureKind.None, Vector2.one);
        }

        public static Material Get(Color color, TextureKind texture, Vector2 tiling)
        {
            Color32 c = color;
            string key = c.r + "," + c.g + "," + c.b + "," + texture + "," + tiling.x.ToString("0.##") + "," + tiling.y.ToString("0.##");
            Material material;
            if (Cache.TryGetValue(key, out material) && material != null)
            {
                return material;
            }

            material = new Material(FindShader()) { name = "FF_" + key };
            material.color = color;
            if (material.HasProperty("_BaseColor")) material.SetColor("_BaseColor", color);
            Texture2D tex = ProceduralTextures.Get(texture);
            if (tex != null && material.HasProperty("_MainTex"))
            {
                material.mainTexture = tex;
                material.mainTextureScale = tiling;
            }
            Cache[key] = material;
            return material;
        }

        /// <summary>The factory floor material (tiled texture with a bright centre and dark edges).</summary>
        public static Material GetGround()
        {
            Material material;
            if (Cache.TryGetValue("ground", out material) && material != null)
            {
                return material;
            }

            if (groundShader == null)
            {
                groundShader = Resources.Load<Shader>("Shaders/FlowFactoryGround");
            }
            if (groundShader != null && groundShader.isSupported)
            {
                material = new Material(groundShader) { name = "FF_Ground" };
                material.SetColor("_Inner", Palette.GroundInner);
                material.SetColor("_Outer", Palette.GroundOuter);
                material.SetFloat("_Radius", 11f);
                material.SetTexture("_MainTex", ProceduralTextures.Get(TextureKind.Grid));
            }
            else
            {
                material = Get(Palette.GroundInner);
            }
            Cache["ground"] = material;
            return material;
        }

        /// <summary>Soft contact shadow material (alpha blended). Falls back to a plain dark material if unsupported.</summary>
        public static Material GetBlobShadow()
        {
            Material material;
            if (Cache.TryGetValue("blob", out material) && material != null)
            {
                return material;
            }

            Shader blob = Resources.Load<Shader>("Shaders/FlowFactoryBlob");
            if (blob != null && blob.isSupported)
            {
                material = new Material(blob) { name = "FF_Blob" };
                material.SetColor("_Color", new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.42f));
                material.SetTexture("_MainTex", ProceduralTextures.Get(TextureKind.Blob));
            }
            else
            {
                material = null; // callers skip the shadow when there is no blend shader
            }
            Cache["blob"] = material;
            return material;
        }

        /// <summary>Re-reads the ground colours after the theme changed.</summary>
        public static void RefreshGround()
        {
            Material material;
            if (Cache.TryGetValue("ground", out material) && material != null && material.HasProperty("_Inner"))
            {
                material.SetColor("_Inner", Palette.GroundInner);
                material.SetColor("_Outer", Palette.GroundOuter);
            }
        }

        private static Shader FindShader()
        {
            if (shader != null)
            {
                return shader;
            }

            shader = Resources.Load<Shader>("Shaders/FlowFactoryToon");
            if (shader == null || !shader.isSupported)
            {
                string[] fallbacks = { "Universal Render Pipeline/Unlit", "Unlit/Color", "Standard", "Sprites/Default" };
                for (int i = 0; i < fallbacks.Length && (shader == null || !shader.isSupported); i++)
                {
                    shader = Shader.Find(fallbacks[i]);
                }
            }
            return shader;
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Builds simple renderer-only primitives (no colliders, no physics).</summary>
    public static class Primitives
    {
        private static readonly Dictionary<PrimitiveType, Mesh> Meshes = new Dictionary<PrimitiveType, Mesh>();

        public static GameObject Create(PrimitiveType type, Transform parent, Vector3 localPosition, Vector3 localScale, Color color, string name = null)
        {
            return Create(type, parent, localPosition, localScale, GameMaterials.Get(color), Quaternion.identity, name);
        }

        public static GameObject Create(PrimitiveType type, Transform parent, Vector3 localPosition, Vector3 localScale, Color color,
            TextureKind texture, Vector2 tiling, string name = null)
        {
            return Create(type, parent, localPosition, localScale, GameMaterials.Get(color, texture, tiling), Quaternion.identity, name);
        }

        public static GameObject Create(PrimitiveType type, Transform parent, Vector3 localPosition, Vector3 localScale, Material material,
            Quaternion rotation, string name = null)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(name ?? type.ToString());
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            go.transform.localPosition = localPosition;
            go.transform.localRotation = rotation;
            go.transform.localScale = localScale;
            go.AddComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh = GetMesh(type);
            var meshRenderer = go.AddComponent<MeshRenderer>();
            meshRenderer.sharedMaterial = material;
            meshRenderer.shadowCastingMode = UnityEngine.Rendering.ShadowCastingMode.Off;
            meshRenderer.receiveShadows = false;
            return go;
        }

        private static bool? meshesReadable;
        private static Vector2? topUvSigns;

        /// <summary>
        /// True when the built-in primitive meshes can be read on the CPU (needed to merge meshes at runtime).
        /// If not, the game simply keeps one object per part.
        /// </summary>
        public static bool MeshesReadable
        {
            get
            {
                if (!meshesReadable.HasValue)
                {
                    meshesReadable = GetMesh(PrimitiveType.Cube).isReadable && GetMesh(PrimitiveType.Cylinder).isReadable && GetMesh(PrimitiveType.Sphere).isReadable;
                }
                return meshesReadable.Value;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>
        /// Direction in which the cube's top-face texture coordinates grow: x = sign of du/dx, y = sign of dv/dz.
        /// Measured from the mesh so scrolling belts always move the right way; (1, 1) if the mesh is unreadable.
        /// </summary>
        public static Vector2 CubeTopUvSigns
        {
            get
            {
                if (!topUvSigns.HasValue)
                {
                    Vector2 result = Vector2.one;
                    Mesh cube = GetMesh(PrimitiveType.Cube);
                    if (cube.isReadable)
                    {
                        Vector3[] vertices = cube.vertices;
                        Vector3[] normals = cube.normals;
                        Vector2[] uvs = cube.uv;
                        int minX = -1, maxX = -1, minZ = -1, maxZ = -1;
                        for (int i = 0; i < vertices.Length && i < normals.Length && i < uvs.Length; i++)
                        {
                            if (normals[i].y < 0.9f) continue;
                            if (minX < 0 || vertices[i].x < vertices[minX].x) minX = i;
                            if (maxX < 0 || vertices[i].x > vertices[maxX].x) maxX = i;
                            if (minZ < 0 || vertices[i].z < vertices[minZ].z) minZ = i;
                            if (maxZ < 0 || vertices[i].z > vertices[maxZ].z) maxZ = i;
                        }
                        if (minX >= 0)
                        {
                            float du = uvs[maxX].x - uvs[minX].x;
                            float dv = uvs[maxZ].y - uvs[minZ].y;
                            result = new Vector2(du < 0f ? -1f : 1f, dv < 0f ? -1f : 1f);
                        }
                    }
                    topUvSigns = result;
                }
                return topUvSigns.Value;
            }
        }

        private static Mesh GetMesh(PrimitiveType type)
        {
            Mesh mesh;
            if (Meshes.TryGetValue(type, out mesh) && mesh != null)
            {
                return mesh;
            }

            GameObject temp = GameObject.CreatePrimitive(type);
            mesh = temp.GetComponent<MeshFilter>().sharedMesh;
            Object.Destroy(temp);
            Meshes[type] = mesh;
            return mesh;
        }
    }

    public static class Palette
    {
        // Warm "toy factory" palette: cream floor plates on a teal slab, coral machines, amber parcels.
        public static readonly Color Background = new Color(0.05f, 0.10f, 0.14f);
        public static Color GroundInner = new Color(0.24f, 0.40f, 0.46f);
        public static Color GroundOuter = new Color(0.04f, 0.09f, 0.12f);
        public static Color FloorLight = new Color(0.97f, 0.93f, 0.84f);
        public static Color FloorDark = new Color(0.88f, 0.83f, 0.73f);
        public static Color Slab = new Color(0.10f, 0.25f, 0.31f);
        public static Color SlabBase = new Color(0.07f, 0.17f, 0.22f);
        public static readonly Color Frame = new Color(0.17f, 0.19f, 0.23f);
        public static readonly Color BeltFixed = new Color(0.36f, 0.38f, 0.43f);
        public static Color BeltRotatable = new Color(0.10f, 0.58f, 0.78f);
        public static readonly Color RailFixed = new Color(0.62f, 0.64f, 0.68f);
        public static Color RailRotatable = new Color(1.00f, 0.80f, 0.20f);
        public static readonly Color Knob = new Color(1.00f, 0.50f, 0.15f);
        public static readonly Color KnobHandle = new Color(1.00f, 0.95f, 0.85f);
        public static readonly Color Source = new Color(0.92f, 0.38f, 0.30f);

        public static Color ParcelTape = new Color(0.98f, 0.92f, 0.78f);

        public static readonly Color Hint = new Color(1.00f, 0.92f, 0.25f);

        public static readonly Color Barrel = new Color(0.85f, 0.32f, 0.28f);
        public static readonly Color BarrelTeal = new Color(0.16f, 0.55f, 0.62f);
        public static readonly Color Metal = new Color(0.55f, 0.60f, 0.66f);
    }

using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The game screens (menu, level select, in-game HUD, success card, settings), built from code on one
    /// canvas inside the safe area. Contains no game rules: it only raises events and shows what it is told.
    /// Money-related screens live in <see cref="MonetizationUi"/>.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class GameUIController : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const int LevelsPerWorld = 12;

        public event Action PlayPressed;
        public event Action LevelsPressed;
        public event Action SettingsPressed;
        public event Action ShopPressed;
        public event Action MenuPressed;
        public event Action<int> LevelChosen;
        public event Action RunPressed;
        public event Action HintPressed;
        public event Action ResetPressed;
        public event Action BackPressed;
        public event Action NextPressed;
        public event Action ReplayPressed;
        public event Action DoubleCoinsPressed;
        public event Action SoundToggled;
        public event Action VolumeCycled;
        public event Action FpsToggled;
        public event Action FrameRateToggled;
        public event Action ProgressResetConfirmed;
        /// <summary>Raised for every button press so the app can play a click.</summary>
        public event Action AnyButton;

        private RectTransform safeRoot;
        private GameObject menuScreen;
        private GameObject levelScreen;
        private GameObject hudScreen;
        private GameObject settingsScreen;
        private GameObject successOverlay;

        private RectTransform levelGrid;
        private Text worldLabel;
        private Button prevWorld;
        private Button nextWorld;
        private int worldPage;
        private IList<LevelData> levelList;
        private ProgressData levelProgress;

        private Text hudTitle;
        private Text hudHint;
        private Button runButton;
        private Button hintButton;
        private Text hintCost;
        private Button hudBackButton;
        private Button hudResetButton;
        private GameObject toast;
        private Text toastText;
        private float toastHideAt;

        private readonly List<Text> coinLabels = new List<Text>();
        private Image shopDot;

        private Text successTitle;
        private Text successBody;
        private Button nextButton;
        private Button doubleButton;
        private readonly Image[] successStars = new Image[3];

        private Button soundButton;
        private Button volumeButton;
        private Button fpsButton;
        private Button frameRateButton;
        private Button resetProgressButton;
        private bool resetArmed;

        private bool pulseRun;

        public RectTransform SafeRoot
        {
            get { return safeRoot; }
        }

        public bool OverlayOpen
        {
            get { return successOverlay != null && successOverlay.activeSelf; }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- build

        public void Build()
        {
            var canvasObject = new GameObject("UI", typeof(RectTransform));
            canvasObject.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            var canvas = canvasObject.AddComponent<Canvas>();
            canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
            canvas.sortingOrder = 10;
            var scaler = canvasObject.AddComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
            scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1080f, 1920f);
            scaler.screenMatchMode = CanvasScaler.ScreenMatchMode.MatchWidthOrHeight;
            scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = 0.5f;
            canvasObject.AddComponent<GraphicRaycaster>();

            safeRoot = Ui.NewRect("SafeArea", canvasObject.transform);
            Ui.Stretch(safeRoot);
            safeRoot.gameObject.AddComponent<SafeAreaFitter>();

            BuildMenu();
            BuildLevelSelect();
            BuildHud();
            BuildSettings();
            BuildSuccess();
            HideAll();
        }

        private Button Btn(string name, Transform parent, string text, Color fill, int size, Action onClick, Action raise)
        {
            return Ui.NewButton(name, parent, text, fill, size, () =>
            {
                if (AnyButton != null) AnyButton();
                if (onClick != null) onClick();
                if (raise != null) raise();
            });
        }

        private GameObject NewScreen(string name, Color? background)
        {
            RectTransform rect = Ui.NewRect(name, safeRoot);
            Ui.Stretch(rect);
            if (background.HasValue)
            {
                // Backgrounds are drawn on the full canvas, not only inside the safe area.
                Image bg = Ui.NewImage("Background", rect, background.Value);
                bg.raycastTarget = true;
                RectTransform bgRect = bg.rectTransform;
                bgRect.anchorMin = new Vector2(-1f, -1f);
                bgRect.anchorMax = new Vector2(2f, 2f);
                bgRect.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
                bgRect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
            }
            rect.gameObject.AddComponent<ScreenFx>();
            return rect.gameObject;
        }

        private static Color ScreenTint
        {
            get { return new Color(0.04f, 0.09f, 0.13f, 0.97f); }
        }
