        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- screens

        private void HideAll()
        {
            menuScreen.SetActive(false);
            levelScreen.SetActive(false);
            hudScreen.SetActive(false);
            settingsScreen.SetActive(false);
            successOverlay.SetActive(false);
            pulseRun = false;
        }

        public void ShowMenu()
        {
            HideAll();
            menuScreen.SetActive(true);
        }

        public void SetShopBadge(bool visible)
        {
            if (shopDot != null) shopDot.gameObject.SetActive(visible);
        }

        public void SetCoins(int coins)
        {
            foreach (Text label in coinLabels)
            {
                label.text = coins.ToString();
            }
        }

        public void ShowLevelSelect(IList<LevelData> levels, ProgressData progress)
        {
            HideAll();
            levelScreen.SetActive(true);
            levelList = levels;
            levelProgress = progress;
            worldPage = Mathf.Clamp((progress.LastLevel - 1) / LevelsPerWorld, 0, WorldCount - 1);
            RebuildLevelGrid();
        }

        private int WorldCount
        {
            get { return Mathf.Max(1, (levelList.Count + LevelsPerWorld - 1) / LevelsPerWorld); }
        }

        private void ChangeWorld(int delta)
        {
            worldPage = Mathf.Clamp(worldPage + delta, 0, WorldCount - 1);
            RebuildLevelGrid();
        }

        private void RebuildLevelGrid()
        {
            foreach (Transform child in levelGrid)
            {
                Destroy(child.gameObject);
            }

            int first = worldPage * LevelsPerWorld;
            int last = Mathf.Min(first + LevelsPerWorld, levelList.Count);
            int worldStars = 0;
            for (int i = first; i < last; i++) worldStars += levelProgress.StarsFor(levelList[i].id);
            worldLabel.text = "WORLD " + (worldPage + 1) + "\n" + worldStars + "/" + ((last - first) * 3) + " stars";
            prevWorld.interactable = worldPage > 0;
            nextWorld.interactable = worldPage < WorldCount - 1;

            const int columns = 3;
            const float cell = 280f;
            int rows = (last - first + columns - 1) / columns;
            for (int i = first; i < last; i++)
            {
                int id = levelList[i].id;
                int local = i - first;
                int col = local % columns;
                int row = local / columns;
                bool unlocked = levelProgress.IsUnlocked(id);
                bool done = levelProgress.IsCompleted(id);

                Color fill = !unlocked ? Ui.Disabled : done ? Ui.Primary : Ui.Secondary;
                Button button = Btn("Level" + id, levelGrid, id.ToString(), fill, 96, null, null);
                Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)button.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), new Vector2(250f, 250f),
                    new Vector2((col - (columns - 1) * 0.5f) * cell, ((rows - 1) * 0.5f - row) * cell));
                button.interactable = unlocked;
                button.GetComponentInChildren<Text>().rectTransform.offsetMax = new Vector2(-12f, -45f);

                if (!unlocked || !done)
                {
                    Text caption = Ui.NewLabel("Caption", button.transform, !unlocked ? "LOCKED" : "PLAY", 34, Ui.TextColor);
                    Ui.PlaceCentered(caption.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0f), new Vector2(230f, 60f), new Vector2(0f, 45f));
                }
                else
                {
                    // Earned stars (gold) and missing stars (dim) under the number.
                    int stars = levelProgress.StarsFor(id);
                    for (int s = 0; s < 3; s++)
                    {
                        Image star = Ui.NewImage("Star" + s, button.transform, s < stars ? new Color(1f, 0.82f, 0.2f) : new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.35f));
                        star.sprite = UiSprites.Star;
                        Ui.PlaceCentered(star.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0f), new Vector2(62f, 62f), new Vector2((s - 1) * 66f, 46f));
                    }
                }

                int captured = id;
                button.onClick.AddListener(() =>
                {
                    if (LevelChosen != null) LevelChosen(captured);
                });
            }
        }

        public void ShowSettings(ProgressData progress, bool fpsOn, bool smooth60)
        {
            HideAll();
            settingsScreen.SetActive(true);
            RefreshSettings(progress, fpsOn, smooth60);
        }

        public void RefreshSettings(ProgressData progress, bool fpsOn, bool smooth60)
        {
            Ui.SetText(soundButton, progress.SoundOn ? "Sound: ON" : "Sound: OFF");
            string[] names = { "Low", "Medium", "High" };
            Ui.SetText(volumeButton, "Volume: " + names[Mathf.Clamp(progress.VolumeStep, 0, 2)]);
            volumeButton.interactable = progress.SoundOn;
            Ui.SetText(frameRateButton, smooth60 ? "Frame rate: 60 (smooth)" : "Frame rate: 30 (battery)");
            fpsButton.gameObject.SetActive(FpsCounter.Available);
            Ui.SetText(fpsButton, fpsOn ? "FPS display: ON" : "FPS display: OFF");
            resetArmed = false;
            Ui.SetText(resetProgressButton, "RESET LEVELS");
        }

        private void OnResetProgressPressed()
        {
            if (!resetArmed)
            {
                resetArmed = true;
                Ui.SetText(resetProgressButton, "TAP AGAIN TO CONFIRM");
                StartCoroutine(DisarmReset());
                return;
            }
            resetArmed = false;
            Ui.SetText(resetProgressButton, "PROGRESS RESET");
            if (ProgressResetConfirmed != null) ProgressResetConfirmed();
        }

        private IEnumerator DisarmReset()
        {
            yield return new WaitForSecondsRealtime(3f);
            if (resetArmed)
            {
                resetArmed = false;
                Ui.SetText(resetProgressButton, "RESET LEVELS");
            }
        }

        public void ShowHud(LevelData level)
        {
            HideAll();
            hudScreen.SetActive(true);
            toast.SetActive(false);
            hudTitle.text = "LEVEL " + level.id;
            hudHint.text = level.hint ?? "";
            runButton.interactable = true;
            hintButton.interactable = true;
        }

        public void SetHint(string text)
        {
            hudHint.text = text ?? "";
        }

        public void SetRunPulse(bool pulse)
        {
            pulseRun = pulse;
            if (!pulse) ((RectTransform)runButton.transform).localScale = Vector3.one;
        }

        public void SetRunInteractable(bool interactable)
        {
            runButton.interactable = interactable;
            hintButton.interactable = interactable;
        }

        public void ShowToast(string message, float seconds = 2.4f)
        {
            toastText.text = message;
            toast.SetActive(true);
            toastHideAt = Time.unscaledTime + seconds;
        }

        public void HideToast()
        {
            toast.SetActive(false);
        }

        public void ShowSuccess(LevelData level, bool hasNext, bool worldEnd, bool finalLevel, LevelReward reward, bool canDouble, string nudge)
        {
            successOverlay.SetActive(true);
            pulseRun = false;
            successTitle.text = worldEnd ? "FACTORY AWAKE!" : "DELIVERED!";

            string coins = "<color=#FFD24A>+" + reward.Coins + " coins</color>" + (reward.FirstClear ? "" : "  (replay)");
            string extra = finalLevel ? "All " + level.id + " levels complete!" : (string.IsNullOrEmpty(nudge) ? "" : nudge);
            successBody.text = coins + (extra.Length > 0 ? "\n" + extra : "");

            nextButton.gameObject.SetActive(hasNext);
            doubleButton.gameObject.SetActive(canDouble);
            doubleButton.interactable = true;
            Ui.SetFill(doubleButton, new Color(0.85f, 0.50f, 0.15f));
            Ui.SetText(doubleButton, "x2 COINS  (watch test ad)");

            StartCoroutine(PopStars(reward.Stars));
        }

        public void ShowDoubled(int coinsAdded)
        {
            doubleButton.interactable = false;
            Ui.SetFill(doubleButton, Ui.Disabled);
            Ui.SetText(doubleButton, "DOUBLED!  +" + coinsAdded);
        }

        private IEnumerator PopStars(int stars)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                successStars[i].color = i < stars ? new Color(1f, 0.82f, 0.2f) : new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.18f);
                successStars[i].rectTransform.localScale = Vector3.zero;
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                if (i >= stars)
                {
                    successStars[i].rectTransform.localScale = Vector3.one;
                    continue;
                }
                yield return new WaitForSecondsRealtime(0.22f);
                for (float t = 0f; t < 0.22f; t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime)
                {
                    float k = t / 0.22f;
                    float s = Mathf.Sin(k * Mathf.PI * 0.5f) * (1f + 0.25f * Mathf.Sin(k * Mathf.PI));
                    successStars[i].rectTransform.localScale = Vector3.one * s;
                    yield return null;
                }
                successStars[i].rectTransform.localScale = Vector3.one;
            }
        }

        public void HideSuccess()
        {
            StopAllCoroutines();
            successOverlay.SetActive(false);
        }

        /// <summary>True if a tap here would land on a HUD button or an open overlay, so the board must ignore it.</summary>
        public bool IsBlockingPoint(Vector2 screenPosition)
        {
            if (OverlayOpen) return true;
            if (!hudScreen.activeSelf) return false;
            return Contains(hudBackButton, screenPosition) || Contains(hudResetButton, screenPosition) ||
                   Contains(runButton, screenPosition) || Contains(hintButton, screenPosition);
        }

        private static bool Contains(Button button, Vector2 point)
        {
            return RectTransformUtility.RectangleContainsScreenPoint((RectTransform)button.transform, point, null);
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (toast != null && toast.activeSelf && Time.unscaledTime > toastHideAt)
            {
                toast.SetActive(false);
            }
            if (pulseRun && hudScreen.activeSelf)
            {
                float s = 1f + 0.06f * Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 6f);
                ((RectTransform)runButton.transform).localScale = new Vector3(s, s, 1f);
            }
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/GridManager.cs   (312 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Creates and places the tiles of the current level and maps between grid coordinates, world
    /// positions and screen taps. World mapping: grid x -> world X, grid row y -> world -Z (north is +Z).
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class GridManager : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const float ParcelHeight = 0.2f;

        private LevelData level;
        private ConveyorTile[] tiles;
        private MeshRenderer[] floors;
        private Transform root;
        private Transform hintMarker;
        private Vector3 hintBase;

        public LevelData Level
        {
            get { return level; }
        }

        public void Build(LevelData data, int[] rotations, bool props = true)
        {
            Clear();
            level = data;
            root = new GameObject("Board").transform;
            root.SetParent(transform, false);

            tiles = new ConveyorTile[data.CellCount];
            floors = new MeshRenderer[data.CellCount];

            BuildFrame(data, props);

            for (int y = 0; y < data.height; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < data.width; x++)
                {
                    int i = data.IndexOf(x, y);
                    Vector3 center = CellCenter(x, y);
                    Color floor = (x + y) % 2 == 0 ? Palette.FloorLight : Palette.FloorDark;
                    GameObject cell = Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, center + Vector3.down * 0.04f, new Vector3(0.96f, 0.08f, 0.96f),
                        floor, TextureKind.Panel, Vector2.one, "Floor");
                    floors[i] = cell.GetComponent<MeshRenderer>();

                    TileSpec spec = data.tiles[i];
                    if (spec.kind == TileKind.Empty)
                    {
                        continue;
                    }
                    var tile = new GameObject("Tile " + x + "," + y).AddComponent<ConveyorTile>();
                    tile.transform.SetParent(root, false);
                    tile.transform.localPosition = center;
                    tile.Init(spec, rotations[i]);
                    tiles[i] = tile;
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Slab, warning-stripe trim, corner lamps and a few props around the board.</summary>
        private void BuildFrame(LevelData data, bool props)
        {
            float w = data.width;
            float h = data.height;

            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, -0.34f, 0f), new Vector3(w + 1.0f, 0.5f, h + 1.0f), Palette.SlabBase, "SlabBase");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, -0.10f, 0f), new Vector3(w + 0.6f, 0.16f, h + 0.6f), Palette.Slab, "Slab");

            // Warning-stripe trim around the plates (its top sits just under the floor plates).
            float stripe = 0.3f;
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, -0.03f, (h + stripe) * 0.5f), new Vector3(w + 0.6f, 0.04f, stripe), Color.white, TextureKind.Hazard, new Vector2((w + 0.6f) / 1.2f, 0.25f), "TrimN");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, -0.03f, -(h + stripe) * 0.5f), new Vector3(w + 0.6f, 0.04f, stripe), Color.white, TextureKind.Hazard, new Vector2((w + 0.6f) / 1.2f, 0.25f), "TrimS");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3((w + stripe) * 0.5f, -0.03f, 0f), new Vector3(stripe, 0.04f, h), Color.white, TextureKind.Hazard, new Vector2(0.25f, h / 1.2f), "TrimE");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(-(w + stripe) * 0.5f, -0.03f, 0f), new Vector3(stripe, 0.04f, h), Color.white, TextureKind.Hazard, new Vector2(0.25f, h / 1.2f), "TrimW");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, new Vector3(0f, -0.045f, 0f), new Vector3(w, 0.05f, h), Palette.SlabBase, "Gap");

            foreach (int sx in new[] { -1, 1 })
            {
                foreach (int sz in new[] { -1, 1 })
                {
                    Vector3 corner = new Vector3(sx * (w * 0.5f + 0.32f), 0f, sz * (h * 0.5f + 0.32f));
                    Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, corner + Vector3.up * 0.30f, new Vector3(0.16f, 0.62f, 0.16f), Palette.Frame, "Post");
                    Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Sphere, root, corner + Vector3.up * 0.70f, Vector3.one * 0.24f, Palette.Lamp, "Lamp");
                }
            }

            if (props)
            {
                BuildProps(Mathf.Max(w, h) * 0.5f * 1.4142f);
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Set dressing on the factory floor: barrels, a turning gear and a crate stack.</summary>
        private void BuildProps(float extent)
        {
            // Directions on the ground as the fixed camera sees them: forward = up the screen, right = right.
            Vector3 f = new Vector3(0.7071f, 0f, 0.7071f);
            Vector3 r = new Vector3(0.7071f, 0f, -0.7071f);
            const float ground = -0.57f;

            Vector3 barrelA = f * (extent + 0.55f) + r * 1.9f;
            Vector3 barrelB = f * (extent + 0.85f) + r * 2.6f;
            Barrel(barrelA, Palette.Barrel);
            Barrel(barrelB, Palette.BarrelTeal);

            // Gear
            var gear = new GameObject("Gear").transform;
            gear.SetParent(root, false);
            gear.localPosition = f * (extent + 0.3f) - r * 2.6f + Vector3.up * (ground + 0.08f);
            gear.gameObject.AddComponent<Spinner>().DegreesPerSecond = 20f;
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, gear, Vector3.zero, new Vector3(1.0f, 0.05f, 1.0f), Palette.Metal, "Disc");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, gear, new Vector3(0f, 0.07f, 0f), new Vector3(0.30f, 0.04f, 0.30f), Palette.Frame, "Hub");
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
            {
                Quaternion q = Quaternion.Euler(0f, i * 36f, 0f);
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, gear, q * new Vector3(0f, 0f, 0.55f), new Vector3(0.22f, 0.10f, 0.20f),
                    GameMaterials.Get(Palette.Metal), q, "Tooth");
            }

            // Crate stack in front, off to the left.
            Vector3 stack = -f * (extent + 0.8f) - r * 2.7f;
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, stack + Vector3.up * (ground + 0.40f), Vector3.one * 0.8f, Palette.Crate, TextureKind.Wood, Vector2.one, "StackCrate");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, stack + r * 0.9f + Vector3.up * (ground + 0.28f), Vector3.one * 0.56f, Palette.Crate, TextureKind.Wood, Vector2.one, "StackCrate");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, root, stack + Vector3.up * (ground + 1.05f), Vector3.one * 0.5f, Palette.Crate, TextureKind.Wood, Vector2.one, "StackCrate");
        }

        private void Barrel(Vector3 position, Color color)
        {
            const float ground = -0.57f;
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, root, position + Vector3.up * (ground + 0.36f), new Vector3(0.40f, 0.36f, 0.40f), GameMaterials.Get(color), Quaternion.identity, "Barrel");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, root, position + Vector3.up * (ground + 0.50f), new Vector3(0.42f, 0.03f, 0.42f), GameMaterials.Get(Palette.Frame), Quaternion.identity, "BarrelBand");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, root, position + Vector3.up * (ground + 0.22f), new Vector3(0.42f, 0.03f, 0.42f), GameMaterials.Get(Palette.Frame), Quaternion.identity, "BarrelBand");
        }

        public void Clear()
        {
            if (root != null)
            {
                Destroy(root.gameObject);
            }
            root = null;
            hintMarker = null;
            tiles = null;
            floors = null;
            level = null;
        }

        public Vector3 CellCenter(int x, int y)
        {
            return new Vector3(x - (level.width - 1) * 0.5f, 0f, -(y - (level.height - 1) * 0.5f));
        }

        public Vector3 CellCenter(GridPoint p)
        {
            return CellCenter(p.x, p.y);
        }

        public static Vector3 WorldDirection(Direction d)
        {
            return new Vector3(d.DeltaX(), 0f, -d.DeltaY());
        }

        public void SnapRotations(int[] rotations)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < tiles.Length; i++)
            {
                if (tiles[i] != null) tiles[i].SnapTo(rotations[i]);
            }
        }

        public void RotateTile(int x, int y)
        {
            ConveyorTile tile = tiles[level.IndexOf(x, y)];
            if (tile != null) tile.RotateStep();
        }

        /// <summary>Scrolls the belt rollers along a route while the parcel travels it.</summary>
        public void StartFlow(RouteResult route, int[] rotations)
        {
            StopFlow();
            IList<GridPoint> path = route.Path;
            for (int k = 0; k < path.Count; k++)
            {
                ConveyorTile tile = tiles[level.IndexOf(path[k].x, path[k].y)];
                if (tile == null) continue;
                Direction? entry = k > 0 ? DirectionBetween(path[k - 1], path[k]).Opposite() : (Direction?)null;
                Direction? exit = k < path.Count - 1 ? DirectionBetween(path[k], path[k + 1]) : (Direction?)null;
                tile.SetFlow(entry, exit, rotations[level.IndexOf(path[k].x, path[k].y)]);
            }
        }

        public void StopFlow()
        {
            if (tiles == null) return;
            foreach (ConveyorTile tile in tiles)
            {
                if (tile != null) tile.StopFlow();
            }
        }

        private static Direction DirectionBetween(GridPoint from, GridPoint to)
        {
            if (to.x > from.x) return Direction.East;
            if (to.x < from.x) return Direction.West;
            return to.y > from.y ? Direction.South : Direction.North;
        }

        public void FlashCell(GridPoint p, Color color, float seconds)
        {
            if (level == null || !level.InBounds(p.x, p.y)) return;
            int i = level.IndexOf(p.x, p.y);
            if (tiles[i] != null)
            {
                tiles[i].Flash(color, seconds);
            }
            else
            {
                StartCoroutine(FlashFloor(floors[i], color, seconds));
            }
        }

        private System.Collections.IEnumerator FlashFloor(MeshRenderer floor, Color color, float seconds)
        {
            Material original = floor.sharedMaterial;
            floor.sharedMaterial = GameMaterials.Get(color);
            yield return new WaitForSeconds(seconds);
            if (floor != null) floor.sharedMaterial = original;
        }

        /// <summary>Converts a screen position into a cell by intersecting the tap ray with the board plane.</summary>
        public bool TryPickCell(Camera cam, Vector2 screenPosition, out GridPoint cell)
        {
            cell = default(GridPoint);
            if (level == null || cam == null) return false;

            Ray ray = cam.ScreenPointToRay(screenPosition);
            var plane = new Plane(Vector3.up, Vector3.zero);
            float distance;
            if (!plane.Raycast(ray, out distance)) return false;

            Vector3 hit = ray.GetPoint(distance);
            int x = Mathf.FloorToInt(hit.x + level.width * 0.5f);
            int y = Mathf.FloorToInt(-hit.z + level.height * 0.5f);
            if (!level.InBounds(x, y)) return false;

            cell = new GridPoint(x, y);
            return true;
        }

        /// <summary>World-space points the parcel travels through for a route, with softened corners.</summary>
        public List<Vector3> BuildWaypoints(RouteResult route)
        {
            var points = new List<Vector3>();
            Vector3 lift = Vector3.up * ParcelHeight;
            IList<GridPoint> path = route.Path;

            for (int i = 1; i < path.Count; i++)
            {
                Vector3 center = CellCenter(path[i]) + lift;
                bool hasNext = i < path.Count - 1;
                if (hasNext)
                {
                    Vector3 inDir = (CellCenter(path[i]) - CellCenter(path[i - 1])).normalized;
                    Vector3 outDir = (CellCenter(path[i + 1]) - CellCenter(path[i])).normalized;
                    if (Vector3.Dot(inDir, outDir) < 0.5f)
                    {
                        // Cut the corner a little so the turn is a smooth curve.
                        points.Add(center - inDir * 0.22f);
                        points.Add(center + outDir * 0.22f);
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                points.Add(center);
            }
            return points;
        }

        public void ShowHint(GridPoint cell)
        {
            if (level == null) return;
            if (hintMarker == null)
            {
                var marker = new GameObject("Hint").transform;
                marker.SetParent(root, false);
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Sphere, marker, Vector3.zero, Vector3.one * 0.28f, Palette.Hint, "Dot");
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cylinder, marker, new Vector3(0f, -0.27f, 0f), new Vector3(0.06f, 0.14f, 0.06f), Palette.Hint, "Stem");
                hintMarker = marker;
            }
            hintBase = CellCenter(cell) + Vector3.up * 0.95f;
            hintMarker.position = hintBase;
            hintMarker.gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }

        public void HideHint()
        {
            if (hintMarker != null) hintMarker.gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (hintMarker != null && hintMarker.gameObject.activeSelf)
            {
                hintMarker.position = hintBase + Vector3.up * (Mathf.Sin(Time.time * 6f) * 0.12f);
            }
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/InputBridge.cs   (101 lines)
====================================================================================================
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
using UnityEngine.InputSystem;
using UnityEngine.InputSystem.UI;
#endif

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The only place that talks to Unity's input API, so the game works whether the project uses the
    /// new Input System, the legacy Input Manager or both. Mouse (Windows) and touch (Android) behave the same.
    /// </summary>
    public static class InputBridge
    {
        /// <summary>True exactly once per new press/tap, with its screen position. Extra fingers are ignored.</summary>
        public static bool TryGetPress(out Vector2 position)
        {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            Pointer pointer = Pointer.current;
            if (pointer != null && pointer.press.wasPressedThisFrame)
            {
                position = pointer.position.ReadValue();
                return true;
            }
            position = Vector2.zero;
            return false;
#elif ENABLE_LEGACY_INPUT_MANAGER
            if (Input.touchCount > 0)
            {
                for (int i = 0; i < Input.touchCount; i++)
                {
                    Touch touch = Input.GetTouch(i);
                    if (touch.phase == TouchPhase.Began && Input.touchCount == 1)
                    {
                        position = touch.position;
                        return true;
                    }
                }
                position = Vector2.zero;
                return false;
            }
            if (Input.GetMouseButtonDown(0))
            {
                position = Input.mousePosition;
                return true;
            }
            position = Vector2.zero;
            return false;
#else
            position = Vector2.zero;
            return false;
#endif
        }

        /// <summary>The Android back button or the Escape key.</summary>
        public static bool BackPressed()
        {
#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            return Keyboard.current != null && Keyboard.current.escapeKey.wasPressedThisFrame;
#elif ENABLE_LEGACY_INPUT_MANAGER
            return Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape);
#else
            return false;
#endif
        }

        /// <summary>Makes sure a single EventSystem with a module matching the active input backend exists.</summary>
        public static void EnsureEventSystem()
        {
            EventSystem system = EventSystem.current;
            if (system == null)
            {
                system = Object.FindFirstObjectByType<EventSystem>();
            }
            if (system == null)
            {
                system = new GameObject("EventSystem").AddComponent<EventSystem>();
            }

#if ENABLE_INPUT_SYSTEM
            var legacy = system.GetComponent<StandaloneInputModule>();
            if (legacy != null)
            {
                Object.Destroy(legacy);
            }
            if (system.GetComponent<InputSystemUIInputModule>() == null)
            {
                var module = system.gameObject.AddComponent<InputSystemUIInputModule>();
                module.AssignDefaultActions();
            }
#else
            if (system.GetComponent<StandaloneInputModule>() == null)
            {
                system.gameObject.AddComponent<StandaloneInputModule>();
            }
#endif
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Editable level asset. One generic game scene loads these; levels are data, never duplicated scenes.
    /// Generate the 12 assets with the menu Flow Factory > Generate Level Assets.
    /// </summary>
    [CreateAssetMenu(fileName = "Level_00", menuName = "Flow Factory/Level Definition")]
    public sealed class LevelDefinition : ScriptableObject
    {
        public int levelId = 1;
        public string title = "New Level";
        [TextArea] public string hint;
        public bool isTutorial;
        [Range(2, 5)] public int width = 4;
        [Range(2, 5)] public int height = 4;
        [Tooltip("Row-major, top (north) row first: index = y * width + x")]
        public TileSpec[] tiles = new TileSpec[0];
        [Tooltip("Known solution: one rotation (0-3) per cell")]
        public int[] solution = new int[0];

        public LevelData ToLevelData()
        {
            return new LevelData
            {
                id = levelId,
                title = title,
                hint = hint,
                isTutorial = isTutorial,
                width = width,
                height = height,
                tiles = (TileSpec[])tiles.Clone(),
                solution = (int[])solution.Clone()
            };
        }

        public void CopyFrom(LevelData data)
        {
            levelId = data.id;
            title = data.title;
            hint = data.hint;
            isTutorial = data.isTutorial;
            width = data.width;
            height = data.height;
            tiles = (TileSpec[])data.tiles.Clone();
            solution = (int[])data.solution.Clone();
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/LevelRepository.cs   (49 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Loads the level list: LevelDefinition assets from Resources/Levels, or the built-in catalog.</summary>
    public static class LevelRepository
    {
        public const string ResourcesFolder = "Levels";

        public static IList<LevelData> LoadAll()
        {
            LevelDefinition[] assets = Resources.LoadAll<LevelDefinition>(ResourcesFolder);
            if (assets.Length > 0)
            {
                Array.Sort(assets, (a, b) => a.levelId.CompareTo(b.levelId));
                var list = new List<LevelData>(assets.Length);
                bool valid = true;
                for (int i = 0; i < assets.Length; i++)
                {
                    LevelData data = assets[i].ToLevelData();
                    List<string> errors = LevelValidator.Validate(data);
                    if (errors.Count > 0 || data.id != i + 1)
                    {
                        valid = false;
                        Debug.LogError("[FlowFactory] Level asset '" + assets[i].name + "' is invalid: " +
                                       (errors.Count > 0 ? string.Join("; ", errors) : "ids must be 1..N without gaps"));
                        break;
                    }
                    list.Add(data);
                }
                if (valid && list.Count == LevelCatalog.Count)
                {
                    return list;
                }
                if (valid)
                {
                    Debug.LogWarning("[FlowFactory] Only " + list.Count + " of " + LevelCatalog.Count + " level assets exist. " +
                                     "Run Flow Factory > Generate Level Assets (create missing). Using the built-in catalog for now.");
                }
                Debug.LogWarning("[FlowFactory] Using the built-in level catalog instead of the assets.");
            }
            return LevelCatalog.BuildAll();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/LocalTelemetry.cs   (60 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.IO;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Local test log only: appends events to telemetry.log in the app's data folder (and the Console in
    /// debug builds). No network, no account, no device or personal identifiers; the session id is random
    /// and only exists for the current run.
    /// Events: session_start, tutorial_start, tutorial_complete, level_start, level_success, level_retry, session_end.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class LocalTelemetry
    {
        private readonly string sessionId = Guid.NewGuid().ToString("N").Substring(0, 8);
        private readonly string path;
        private bool sessionEnded;

        public LocalTelemetry()
        {
            path = Path.Combine(Application.persistentDataPath, "telemetry.log");
        }

        public string FilePath
        {
            get { return path; }
        }

        /// <summary>Allows another session_end after the app was resumed from the background.</summary>
        public void Reopen()
        {
            sessionEnded = false;
        }

        public void Log(string name, string details = "")
        {
            if (name == "session_end")
            {
                if (sessionEnded) return;
                sessionEnded = true;
            }

            string line = DateTime.UtcNow.ToString("o") + "\t" + sessionId + "\t" + name + "\t" + details;
            if (Debug.isDebugBuild)
            {
                Debug.Log("[telemetry] " + name + " " + details);
            }

            try
            {
                File.AppendAllText(path, line + Environment.NewLine);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("[FlowFactory] Could not write telemetry: " + e.Message);
            }
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/MonetizationController.cs   (296 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Connects the economy rules (PlayerEconomy) with the money UI and the ad/store services.
    /// Principles it enforces: every ad is an optional extra the player taps (never during a puzzle, never
    /// required to progress), real-money items are cosmetics and coin packs only, and there are no countdown
    /// pressure timers or limited-time pop-ups.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MonetizationController
    {
        private readonly PlayerEconomy economy;
        private readonly MonetizationUi view;
        private readonly GameUIController ui;
        private readonly IRewardedAdService ads;
        private readonly IStoreService store;
        private readonly LocalTelemetry telemetry;
        private readonly AudioService audio;
        private ShopTab tab = ShopTab.Coins;

        /// <summary>Raised after a theme or parcel skin was equipped, so the 3D scene can refresh.</summary>
        public event Action AppearanceChanged;
        public event Action ShopClosed;

        public MonetizationController(PlayerEconomy economy, MonetizationUi view, GameUIController ui, IRewardedAdService ads,
            IStoreService store, LocalTelemetry telemetry, AudioService audio)
        {
            this.economy = economy;
            this.view = view;
            this.ui = ui;
            this.ads = ads;
            this.store = store;
            this.telemetry = telemetry;
            this.audio = audio;

            view.AnyButton += () => audio.Play(Sfx.Click);
            view.ShopBack += CloseShop;
            view.TabChanged += t => { tab = t; RefreshShop(); };
            view.ItemClicked += OnItemClicked;
            view.ProductClicked += OnProductClicked;
            view.FreeCoinsClicked += OnFreeCoins;
            RefreshCoins();
        }

        private static int Today
        {
            get { return PlayerEconomy.DayNumber(DateTime.Now); }
        }

        private static double Now
        {
            get { return Time.realtimeSinceStartup; }
        }

        public bool OverlayOpen
        {
            get { return view.BlockingOverlayOpen || view.ShopOpen; }
        }

        public void ApplyEquipped()
        {
            ThemeCatalog.Apply(economy.Data.Theme, economy.Data.Parcel);
        }

        private void RefreshCoins()
        {
            ui.SetCoins(economy.Coins);
            view.SetCoins(economy.Coins);
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- shop

        public void OpenShop(ShopTab startTab = ShopTab.Coins)
        {
            tab = startTab;
            telemetry.Log("shop_open", "tab=" + tab);
            view.ShowShop();
            RefreshShop();
        }

        private void CloseShop()
        {
            view.HideShop();
            RefreshCoins();
            if (ShopClosed != null) ShopClosed();
        }

        private void RefreshShop()
        {
            view.RefreshShop(economy, tab, economy.AdsRemainingToday(Today), economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(Today, Now));
            RefreshCoins();
        }

        private void OnItemClicked(string itemId)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.Find(itemId);
            if (item == null) return;

            if (economy.IsOwned(itemId))
            {
                if (economy.Equip(itemId)) AppearanceApplied();
            }
            else if (economy.Coins < item.CoinPrice)
            {
                view.ShowModal("NOT ENOUGH COINS", item.Name + " costs " + item.CoinPrice + ". You have " + economy.Coins + ".\nEarn coins by playing levels, from the daily gift, or get a coin pack.",
                    "GET COINS", () => { tab = ShopTab.Coins; RefreshShop(); }, "CLOSE", null);
            }
            else if (economy.TryBuyWithCoins(itemId))
            {
                telemetry.Log("item_bought", "item=" + itemId + " price=" + item.CoinPrice);
                audio.Play(Sfx.Coin);
                economy.Equip(itemId);
                AppearanceApplied();
            }
            RefreshShop();
        }

        private void AppearanceApplied()
        {
            ApplyEquipped();
            if (AppearanceChanged != null) AppearanceChanged();
        }

        private void OnProductClicked(string productId)
        {
            if (!economy.CanBuyProduct(productId)) return;
            telemetry.Log("purchase_started", "product=" + productId + " test=1");
            store.Purchase(productId, ok =>
            {
                if (ok && economy.GrantProduct(productId))
                {
                    telemetry.Log("purchase_completed", "product=" + productId + " test=1");
                    audio.Play(Sfx.Success);
                    ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.FindByProduct(productId);
                    if (item != null && item.ItemsGranted.Length > 0)
                    {
                        foreach (string granted in item.ItemsGranted) economy.Equip(granted);
                        AppearanceApplied();
                    }
                    ui.ShowToast("Thank you! (test purchase)", 2f);
                }
                RefreshShop();
            });
        }

        private void OnFreeCoins()
        {
            if (!economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(Today, Now))
            {
                view.ShowModal("COME BACK LATER", economy.AdsRemainingToday(Today) <= 0
                    ? "That is all the free coins for today. Tomorrow there are more!"
                    : "Please wait a moment before the next one.", "OK", null);
                return;
            }
            ShowAd("shop", () =>
            {
                economy.AddCoins(EconomyConfig.FreeCoinsAdReward);
                audio.Play(Sfx.Coin);
                RefreshShop();
            });
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- rewarded ad flow

        private void ShowAd(string placement, Action onReward)
        {
            telemetry.Log("ad_offered", "placement=" + placement + " test=1");
            ads.Show(rewarded =>
            {
                if (rewarded)
                {
                    economy.RegisterAdWatched(Today, Now);
                    telemetry.Log("ad_completed", "placement=" + placement + " test=1");
                    onReward();
                }
                else
                {
                    telemetry.Log("ad_skipped", "placement=" + placement);
                }
            });
        }

        public bool CanOfferAd()
        {
            return economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(Today, Now);
        }

        /// <summary>The success card's optional "double the coins" button.</summary>
        public void OfferDoubleCoins(int coins, Action<int> onDoubled)
        {
            if (coins <= 0 || !CanOfferAd()) return;
            ShowAd("double_coins", () =>
            {
                economy.AddCoins(coins);
                audio.Play(Sfx.Coin);
                RefreshCoins();
                onDoubled(coins);
            });
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- hints

        /// <summary>
        /// A hint costs coins, or one optional test ad when the player has too few. Nothing is charged
        /// when there is nothing to hint at.
        /// </summary>
        public void RequestHint(bool hintAvailable, Action showHint)
        {
            if (!hintAvailable)
            {
                ui.ShowToast("Everything looks right. Press RUN!", 2f);
                return;
            }

            if (economy.TrySpend(EconomyConfig.HintCost))
            {
                telemetry.Log("hint_used", "paid=coins");
                RefreshCoins();
                showHint();
                return;
            }

            if (CanOfferAd())
            {
                view.ShowModal("NEED A HINT?", "A hint costs " + EconomyConfig.HintCost + " coins and you have " + economy.Coins + ".\nWatch a short test ad for a free hint?",
                    "WATCH AD", () => ShowAd("hint", () =>
                    {
                        telemetry.Log("hint_used", "paid=ad");
                        showHint();
                    }), "NO THANKS", null);
            }
            else
            {
                view.ShowModal("NOT ENOUGH COINS", "A hint costs " + EconomyConfig.HintCost + " coins.\nYou earn coins by finishing levels and with the daily gift.",
                    "SHOP", () => OpenShop(), "CLOSE", null);
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- daily gift and nudges

        public bool DailyAvailable
        {
            get { return economy.DailyAvailable(Today); }
        }

        public void ShowDailyGift()
        {
            if (!DailyAvailable) return;
            int streak = economy.NextStreak(Today);
            int coins = economy.NextDailyReward(Today);
            int day = (streak - 1) % EconomyConfig.DailyRewards.Length + 1;
            view.ShowModal("DAILY GIFT", "Day " + day + " of " + EconomyConfig.DailyRewards.Length + "\n+" + coins + " coins\nCome back tomorrow for more!",
                "CLAIM", () =>
                {
                    int granted = economy.ClaimDaily(Today);
                    telemetry.Log("daily_claimed", "streak=" + streak + " coins=" + granted);
                    audio.Play(Sfx.Coin);
                    RefreshCoins();
                    ui.SetShopBadge(HasShopNews());
                });
        }

        /// <summary>A gentle reminder after a level when the player can already afford something new.</summary>
        public string ShopNudge()
        {
            ShopItem best = AffordableCosmetic();
            return best == null ? null : "You can afford " + best.Name + " in the shop!";
        }

        public bool HasShopNews()
        {
            return DailyAvailable || AffordableCosmetic() != null;
        }

        private ShopItem AffordableCosmetic()
        {
            ShopItem best = null;
            foreach (ShopItem item in ShopCatalog.All)
            {
                if ((item.Kind != ItemKind.Theme && item.Kind != ItemKind.Parcel) || item.CoinPrice <= 0 || economy.IsOwned(item.Id)) continue;
                if (economy.Coins >= item.CoinPrice && (best == null || item.CoinPrice < best.CoinPrice)) best = item;
            }
            return best;
        }

        public void RefreshBadges()
        {
            RefreshCoins();
            ui.SetShopBadge(HasShopNews());
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/MonetizationUi.cs   (405 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    public enum ShopTab
    {
        Coins,
        Themes,
        Parcels
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// The view of everything money-related: the shop, confirmation dialogs, the test-ad screen and the coin
    /// badge. No rules live here; it raises events and shows what it is told.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MonetizationUi : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public event Action ShopBack;
        public event Action<ShopTab> TabChanged;
        public event Action<string> ItemClicked;
        public event Action<string> ProductClicked;
        public event Action FreeCoinsClicked;
        public event Action AnyButton;

        private RectTransform safeRoot;
        private GameObject shopScreen;
        private RectTransform cardList;
        private Text shopCoins;
        private readonly Dictionary<ShopTab, Button> tabButtons = new Dictionary<ShopTab, Button>();

        private GameObject modal;
        private Text modalTitle;
        private Text modalBody;
        private Button modalPrimary;
        private Button modalSecondary;
        private Action modalPrimaryAction;
        private Action modalSecondaryAction;

        private GameObject adScreen;
        private Text adCountdown;
        private Button adClaim;
        private Action<bool> adDone;
        private float adEndsAt;
        private bool adRunning;

        public bool ShopOpen
        {
            get { return shopScreen != null && shopScreen.activeSelf; }
        }

        public bool BlockingOverlayOpen
        {
            get { return (modal != null && modal.activeSelf) || (adScreen != null && adScreen.activeSelf); }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- build

        public void Build(RectTransform safeArea)
        {
            safeRoot = safeArea;
            BuildShop();
            BuildModal();
            BuildAd();
        }

        private Button Btn(string name, Transform parent, string text, Color fill, int size, Action action)
        {
            return Ui.NewButton(name, parent, text, fill, size, () =>
            {
                if (AnyButton != null) AnyButton();
                if (action != null) action();
            });
        }

        private GameObject NewFullScreen(string name, Color background, bool opaqueToTaps)
        {
            RectTransform rect = Ui.NewRect(name, safeRoot);
            Ui.Stretch(rect);
            rect.gameObject.AddComponent<ScreenFx>().Pop = name != "Shop";
            Image bg = Ui.NewImage("Background", rect, background);
            bg.raycastTarget = opaqueToTaps;
            bg.rectTransform.anchorMin = new Vector2(-1f, -1f);
            bg.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(2f, 2f);
            bg.rectTransform.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            bg.rectTransform.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
            return rect.gameObject;
        }

        private void BuildShop()
        {
            shopScreen = NewFullScreen("Shop", new Color(0.04f, 0.09f, 0.13f, 0.98f), true);
            Transform t = shopScreen.transform;

            Text title = Ui.NewLabel("Title", t, "SHOP", 84, Ui.TextColor);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(title.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 1f), new Vector2(500f, 130f), new Vector2(0f, -100f));
            Ui.AddShadow(title, 6f, 0.5f);

            shopCoins = CreateCoinBadge(t, new Vector2(1f, 1f), new Vector2(-190f, -100f), new Vector2(330f, 90f));

            string[] names = { "COINS", "THEMES", "PARCELS" };
            for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
                ShopTab tab = (ShopTab)i;
                Button b = Btn("Tab" + names[i], t, names[i], Ui.Secondary, 44, () => { if (TabChanged != null) TabChanged(tab); });
                Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)b.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 1f), new Vector2(290f, 120f), new Vector2((i - 1) * 310f, -270f));
                tabButtons[tab] = b;
            }

            cardList = Ui.NewRect("Cards", t);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(cardList, new Vector2(0.5f, 1f), new Vector2(960f, 1100f), new Vector2(0f, -950f));

            Button back = Btn("Back", t, "BACK", Ui.Secondary, 56, () => { if (ShopBack != null) ShopBack(); });
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)back.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0f), new Vector2(520f, 150f), new Vector2(0f, 120f));
            shopScreen.SetActive(false);
        }

        private void BuildModal()
        {
            modal = NewFullScreen("Modal", new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, 0.62f), true);
            Transform t = modal.transform;

            Image card = Ui.NewImage("Card", t, Ui.Panel, true);
            Ui.AddShadow(card, 14f, 0.5f);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(card.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), new Vector2(860f, 640f), Vector2.zero);

            modalTitle = Ui.NewLabel("Title", card.transform, "", 70, new Color(1f, 0.85f, 0.4f));
            Ui.PlaceCentered(modalTitle.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 1f), new Vector2(760f, 120f), new Vector2(0f, -90f));
            Ui.AddShadow(modalTitle, 5f, 0.5f);

            modalBody = Ui.NewLabel("Body", card.transform, "", 44, Ui.TextColor, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter, FontStyle.Normal);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(modalBody.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 1f), new Vector2(760f, 220f), new Vector2(0f, -270f));

            modalPrimary = Btn("Primary", card.transform, "OK", Ui.Primary, 52, () => Close(true));
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)modalPrimary.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0f), new Vector2(680f, 130f), new Vector2(0f, 220f));

            modalSecondary = Btn("Secondary", card.transform, "CANCEL", Ui.Secondary, 44, () => Close(false));
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)modalSecondary.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0f), new Vector2(480f, 100f), new Vector2(0f, 75f));
            modal.SetActive(false);
        }

        private void BuildAd()
        {
            adScreen = NewFullScreen("TestAd", new Color(0.02f, 0.02f, 0.03f, 1f), true);
            Transform t = adScreen.transform;

            Text banner = Ui.NewLabel("Banner", t, "TEST AD", 110, new Color(1f, 0.85f, 0.4f));
            Ui.PlaceCentered(banner.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.62f), new Vector2(900f, 180f), Vector2.zero);
            Text note = Ui.NewLabel("Note", t, "Simulated screen: no ad SDK, no network.\nA real rewarded ad would play here.", 40, Ui.TextDim, TextAnchor.MiddleCenter, FontStyle.Normal);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(note.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.52f), new Vector2(900f, 160f), Vector2.zero);

            adCountdown = Ui.NewLabel("Countdown", t, "3", 160, Color.white);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(adCountdown.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.40f), new Vector2(400f, 220f), Vector2.zero);

            adClaim = Btn("Claim", t, "CLAIM REWARD", Ui.Primary, 56, () => FinishAd(true));
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)adClaim.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.24f), new Vector2(700f, 150f), Vector2.zero);

            Button skip = Btn("Skip", t, "SKIP (no reward)", Ui.Secondary, 36, () => FinishAd(false));
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)skip.transform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.14f), new Vector2(520f, 100f), Vector2.zero);
            adScreen.SetActive(false);
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- coin badge

        /// <summary>A small rounded panel with a gold coin icon; returns the amount label.</summary>
        public static Text CreateCoinBadge(Transform parent, Vector2 anchor, Vector2 offset, Vector2 size)
        {
            Image panel = Ui.NewImage("CoinBadge", parent, new Color(0.03f, 0.07f, 0.11f, 0.80f), true);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(panel.rectTransform, anchor, size, offset);

            Image coin = Ui.NewImage("Coin", panel.transform, new Color(1f, 0.80f, 0.22f));
            coin.sprite = UiSprites.Circle;
            Ui.PlaceCentered(coin.rectTransform, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(size.y * 0.66f, size.y * 0.66f), new Vector2(size.y * 0.5f, 0f));
            Image shine = Ui.NewImage("Shine", coin.transform, new Color(1f, 0.95f, 0.65f));
            shine.sprite = UiSprites.Circle;
            Ui.PlaceCentered(shine.rectTransform, new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), new Vector2(size.y * 0.40f, size.y * 0.40f), Vector2.zero);

            Text amount = Ui.NewLabel("Amount", panel.transform, "0", Mathf.RoundToInt(size.y * 0.55f), Ui.TextColor, TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
            amount.rectTransform.anchorMin = new Vector2(0f, 0f);
            amount.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(1f, 1f);
            amount.rectTransform.offsetMin = new Vector2(size.y * 1.05f, 4f);
            amount.rectTransform.offsetMax = new Vector2(-14f, -4f);
            return amount;
        }

        public void SetCoins(int coins)
        {
            if (shopCoins != null) shopCoins.text = coins.ToString();
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- shop

        public void ShowShop()
        {
            shopScreen.SetActive(true);
        }

        public void HideShop()
        {
            shopScreen.SetActive(false);
        }

        public void RefreshShop(PlayerEconomy economy, ShopTab tab, int adsLeft, bool adReady)
        {
            foreach (KeyValuePair<ShopTab, Button> pair in tabButtons)
            {
                Ui.SetFill(pair.Value, pair.Key == tab ? Ui.Primary : Ui.Secondary);
            }
            SetCoins(economy.Coins);

            foreach (Transform child in cardList)
            {
                Destroy(child.gameObject);
            }

            var rows = new List<Action<RectTransform>>();
            if (tab == ShopTab.Coins)
            {
                rows.Add(card => BuildFreeCoinsCard(card, adsLeft, adReady));
                foreach (ShopItem item in ShopCatalog.All)
                {
                    ShopItem captured = item;
                    if (item.Kind == ItemKind.CoinPack || item.Kind == ItemKind.Bundle)
                    {
                        rows.Add(card => BuildProductCard(card, captured, economy));
                    }
                }
            }
            else
            {
                foreach (ShopItem item in ShopCatalog.OfKind(tab == ShopTab.Themes ? ItemKind.Theme : ItemKind.Parcel))
                {
                    ShopItem captured = item;
                    rows.Add(card => BuildCosmeticCard(card, captured, economy));
                }
            }

            const float cardHeight = 230f;
            const float gap = 20f;
            for (int i = 0; i < rows.Count; i++)
            {
                RectTransform card = Ui.NewRect("Card" + i, cardList);
                Ui.PlaceCentered(card, new Vector2(0.5f, 1f), new Vector2(940f, cardHeight), new Vector2(0f, -(cardHeight * 0.5f + i * (cardHeight + gap))));
                Image bg = card.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
                bg.sprite = UiSprites.Rounded;
                bg.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
                bg.color = new Color(0.13f, 0.20f, 0.30f, 1f);
                bg.raycastTarget = false;
                Ui.AddShadow(bg, 8f, 0.35f);
                rows[i](card);
            }
        }

        private Text CardText(RectTransform card, string text, int size, Color color, Vector2 anchor, Vector2 offset, Vector2 dimensions, TextAnchor align, FontStyle style = FontStyle.Bold)
        {
            Text label = Ui.NewLabel("Text", card, text, size, color, align, style);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(label.rectTransform, anchor, dimensions, offset);
            return label;
        }

        private void CardBadge(RectTransform card, string badge)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(badge)) return;
            Image tag = Ui.NewImage("Badge", card, new Color(1f, 0.45f, 0.2f), true);
            Ui.PlaceCentered(tag.rectTransform, new Vector2(1f, 1f), new Vector2(220f, 52f), new Vector2(-135f, -4f));
            Text text = Ui.NewLabel("BadgeText", tag.transform, badge, 30, Color.white);
            Ui.Stretch(text.rectTransform);
        }

        private void Swatch(RectTransform card, Color color, Vector2 offset, float size = 64f, bool circle = false)
        {
            Image image = Ui.NewImage("Swatch", card, color, !circle);
            if (circle) image.sprite = UiSprites.Circle;
            Ui.PlaceCentered(image.rectTransform, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(size, size), offset);
        }

        private void BuildFreeCoinsCard(RectTransform card, int adsLeft, bool adReady)
        {
            Swatch(card, new Color(1f, 0.80f, 0.22f), new Vector2(110f, 0f), 120f, true);
            CardText(card, "Free Coins", 46, Ui.TextColor, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(450f, 38f), new Vector2(430f, 70f), TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
            CardText(card, "+" + EconomyConfig.FreeCoinsAdReward + " coins for a short test ad (" + adsLeft + " left today)", 30, Ui.TextDim,
                new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(450f, -34f), new Vector2(430f, 64f), TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, FontStyle.Normal);
            string label = adsLeft <= 0 ? "TOMORROW" : adReady ? "WATCH" : "WAIT...";
            Button action = Btn("Action", card, label, adReady ? Ui.Primary : Ui.Disabled, 40, () => { if (FreeCoinsClicked != null) FreeCoinsClicked(); });
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)action.transform, new Vector2(1f, 0.5f), new Vector2(250f, 120f), new Vector2(-150f, -12f));
        }

        private void BuildProductCard(RectTransform card, ShopItem item, PlayerEconomy economy)
        {
            bool owned = item.Kind == ItemKind.Bundle && !economy.CanBuyProduct(item.ProductId);
            Swatch(card, new Color(1f, 0.80f, 0.22f), new Vector2(110f, 0f), 120f, true);
            CardText(card, item.Name, 44, Ui.TextColor, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(450f, 38f), new Vector2(430f, 70f), TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
            CardText(card, item.Description, 30, Ui.TextDim, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(450f, -34f), new Vector2(430f, 64f), TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, FontStyle.Normal);
            CardBadge(card, item.Badge);
            string productId = item.ProductId;
            Button action = Btn("Action", card, owned ? "OWNED" : item.DisplayPrice, owned ? Ui.Disabled : Ui.Primary, 44,
                () => { if (ProductClicked != null) ProductClicked(productId); });
            action.interactable = !owned;
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)action.transform, new Vector2(1f, 0.5f), new Vector2(250f, 120f), new Vector2(-150f, -12f));
        }

        private void BuildCosmeticCard(RectTransform card, ShopItem item, PlayerEconomy economy)
        {
            bool owned = economy.IsOwned(item.Id);
            bool equipped = economy.IsEquipped(item.Id);

            if (item.Kind == ItemKind.Theme)
            {
                ThemeColors c = ThemeCatalog.Theme(item.Id);
                Swatch(card, c.FloorLight, new Vector2(70f, 0f));
                Swatch(card, c.Slab, new Vector2(145f, 0f));
                Swatch(card, c.BeltRotatable, new Vector2(220f, 0f));
            }
            else
            {
                ParcelColors c = ThemeCatalog.Parcel(item.Id);
                Swatch(card, c.Box, new Vector2(100f, 0f), 96f);
                Swatch(card, c.Tape, new Vector2(100f, 0f), 30f);
            }
            // The text sits between the swatches and the action button (button starts at x = 665 on a 940 wide card).
            float textX = item.Kind == ItemKind.Theme ? 470f : 480f;
            float textWidth = item.Kind == ItemKind.Theme ? 360f : 370f;
            CardText(card, item.Name, 44, Ui.TextColor, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(textX, 38f), new Vector2(textWidth, 70f), TextAnchor.MiddleLeft);
            CardText(card, item.Description, 28, Ui.TextDim, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(textX, -34f), new Vector2(textWidth, 64f), TextAnchor.MiddleLeft, FontStyle.Normal);
            CardBadge(card, item.Badge);

            string label = equipped ? "IN USE" : owned ? "EQUIP" : item.CoinPrice.ToString();
            Color fill = equipped ? Ui.Disabled : owned ? Ui.Secondary : (economy.Coins >= item.CoinPrice ? Ui.Primary : new Color(0.45f, 0.35f, 0.18f));
            string itemId = item.Id;
            Button action = Btn("Action", card, label, fill, 44, () => { if (ItemClicked != null) ItemClicked(itemId); });
            action.interactable = !equipped;
            Ui.PlaceCentered((RectTransform)action.transform, new Vector2(1f, 0.5f), new Vector2(250f, 120f), new Vector2(-150f, -12f));
            if (!owned)
            {
                // A small coin next to the price.
                Image coin = Ui.NewImage("Coin", action.transform, new Color(1f, 0.80f, 0.22f));
                coin.sprite = UiSprites.Circle;
                Ui.PlaceCentered(coin.rectTransform, new Vector2(0f, 0.5f), new Vector2(48f, 48f), new Vector2(46f, 0f));
                action.GetComponentInChildren<Text>().rectTransform.offsetMin = new Vector2(70f, 8f);
            }
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- dialogs

        public void ShowModal(string title, string body, string primary, Action onPrimary, string secondary = null, Action onSecondary = null)
        {
            modalTitle.text = title;
            modalBody.text = body;
            Ui.SetText(modalPrimary, primary);
            modalPrimaryAction = onPrimary;
            modalSecondaryAction = onSecondary;
            modalSecondary.gameObject.SetActive(!string.IsNullOrEmpty(secondary));
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(secondary)) Ui.SetText(modalSecondary, secondary);
            modal.SetActive(true);
        }

        private void Close(bool primary)
        {
            modal.SetActive(false);
            Action action = primary ? modalPrimaryAction : modalSecondaryAction;
            modalPrimaryAction = null;
            modalSecondaryAction = null;
            if (action != null) action();
        }

        /// <summary>The simulated ad: a 3 second countdown, then the reward can be claimed. Skipping gives nothing.</summary>
        public void ShowTestAd(Action<bool> done)
        {
            adDone = done;
            adEndsAt = Time.unscaledTime + 3f;
            adRunning = true;
            adClaim.gameObject.SetActive(false);
            adCountdown.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            adScreen.SetActive(true);
        }

        private void FinishAd(bool rewarded)
        {
            adRunning = false;
            adScreen.SetActive(false);
            Action<bool> callback = adDone;
            adDone = null;
            if (callback != null) callback(rewarded);
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (!adRunning) return;
            float left = adEndsAt - Time.unscaledTime;
            if (left > 0f)
            {
                adCountdown.text = Mathf.CeilToInt(left).ToString();
            }
            else
            {
                adCountdown.gameObject.SetActive(false);
                adClaim.gameObject.SetActive(true);
            }
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/ParcelController.cs   (140 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// The parcel. Moves along a precomputed list of points; no Rigidbody or physics involved.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class ParcelController : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private const float TurnDegreesPerSecond = 900f;

        private Transform body;

        public void Build()
        {
            if (body != null)
            {
                Destroy(body.gameObject);
            }
            Transform oldShadow = transform.Find("Shadow");
            if (oldShadow != null)
            {
                Destroy(oldShadow.gameObject);
            }
            body = new GameObject("Body").transform;
            body.SetParent(transform, false);
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, body, new Vector3(0f, 0.17f, 0f), new Vector3(0.36f, 0.34f, 0.36f), Palette.Parcel, "Box");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, body, new Vector3(0f, 0.17f, 0f), new Vector3(0.38f, 0.07f, 0.38f), Palette.ParcelTape, "TapeH");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, body, new Vector3(0f, 0.17f, 0f), new Vector3(0.07f, 0.36f, 0.38f), Palette.ParcelTape, "TapeV");
            // A soft contact shadow on the belt under the parcel; it stays flat while the box wobbles or falls.
            Material blob = GameMaterials.GetBlobShadow();
            if (blob != null)
            {
                Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Quad, transform, new Vector3(0f, -GridManager.ParcelHeight + 0.125f, 0f), new Vector3(0.62f, 0.62f, 1f),
                    blob, Quaternion.Euler(90f, 0f, 0f), "Shadow");
            }
            // A white shipping label with a dark barcode line on the side.
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, body, new Vector3(0.185f, 0.16f, 0.05f), new Vector3(0.02f, 0.14f, 0.18f), Palette.ParcelLabel, "Label");
            Primitives.Create(PrimitiveType.Cube, body, new Vector3(0.195f, 0.14f, 0.05f), new Vector3(0.02f, 0.03f, 0.13f), Palette.Frame, "Barcode");
        }

        /// <summary>Rebuilds the parcel after a different parcel skin was equipped.</summary>
        public void Rebuild()
        {
            bool wasActive = gameObject.activeSelf;
            Build();
            gameObject.SetActive(wasActive);
        }

        public void PlaceAt(Vector3 position)
        {
            StopAllCoroutines();
            transform.position = position;
            transform.rotation = Quaternion.identity;
            body.localScale = Vector3.one;
            body.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            gameObject.SetActive(true);
        }

        public void Hide()
        {
            StopAllCoroutines();
            gameObject.SetActive(false);
        }

        /// <summary>Moves through the points at a constant speed, turning smoothly to face the way it goes.</summary>
        public IEnumerator Travel(IList<Vector3> points, float speed)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < points.Count; i++)
            {
                Vector3 target = points[i];
                Vector3 flat = target - transform.position;
                flat.y = 0f;
                Quaternion look = flat.sqrMagnitude > 0.0001f ? Quaternion.LookRotation(flat) : transform.rotation;

                while ((transform.position - target).sqrMagnitude > 0.0001f)
                {
                    transform.position = Vector3.MoveTowards(transform.position, target, speed * Time.deltaTime);
                    transform.rotation = Quaternion.RotateTowards(transform.rotation, look, TurnDegreesPerSecond * Time.deltaTime);
                    yield return null;
                }
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Ends a failed run against an obstacle: a short lurch and wobble.</summary>
        public IEnumerator Bump(Vector3 direction)
        {
            Vector3 start = transform.position;
            Vector3 forward = start + direction.normalized * 0.12f;
            yield return MoveOver(start, forward, 0.08f);
            yield return MoveOver(forward, start - direction.normalized * 0.08f, 0.12f);
            for (float t = 0f; t < 0.35f; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                body.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, Mathf.Sin(t * 50f) * 14f * (1f - t / 0.35f), 0f);
                yield return null;
            }
            body.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
        }

        /// <summary>Ends a failed run that left the board: the parcel tips over the edge and drops.</summary>
        public IEnumerator FallOff(Vector3 direction)
        {
            Vector3 flat = direction.normalized;
            Vector3 start = transform.position;
            for (float t = 0f; t < 0.7f; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                transform.position = start + flat * (t * 0.9f) + Vector3.down * (t * t * 7f);
                body.localRotation = Quaternion.AngleAxis(t * 260f, Vector3.Cross(Vector3.up, flat));
                yield return null;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Success: slide to the middle of the bin and shrink into it.</summary>
        public IEnumerator Deliver(Vector3 center)
        {
            yield return MoveOver(transform.position, center, 0.18f);
            for (float t = 0f; t < 0.3f; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                float k = 1f - t / 0.3f;
                body.localScale = Vector3.one * k;
                transform.position = center + Vector3.down * (0.18f * (1f - k));
                yield return null;
            }
            body.localScale = Vector3.zero;
        }

        private IEnumerator MoveOver(Vector3 from, Vector3 to, float seconds)
        {
            for (float t = 0f; t < seconds; t += Time.deltaTime)
            {
                transform.position = Vector3.Lerp(from, to, t / seconds);
                yield return null;
            }
            transform.position = to;
        }
    }
}

using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    public sealed class PlayerPrefsStore : IKeyValueStore
    {
        public bool TryGetString(string key, out string value)
        {
            if (PlayerPrefs.HasKey(key))
            {
                value = PlayerPrefs.GetString(key);
                return true;
            }
            value = null;
            return false;
        }

        public void SetString(string key, string value)
        {
            PlayerPrefs.SetString(key, value);
        }

        public void DeleteKey(string key)
        {
            PlayerPrefs.DeleteKey(key);
        }

        public void Flush()
        {
            PlayerPrefs.Save();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/ProceduralTextures.cs   (135 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    public enum TextureKind
    {
        None,
        Panel,
        Rollers,
        RollersH,
        Wood,
        Hazard,
        Grid,
        Blob
    }

    /// <summary>Small textures drawn in code (no image files), created once and cached.</summary>
    public static class ProceduralTextures
    {
        private const int Size = 64;
        private static readonly Dictionary<TextureKind, Texture2D> Cache = new Dictionary<TextureKind, Texture2D>();

        public static Texture2D Get(TextureKind kind)
        {
            if (kind == TextureKind.None)
            {
                return null;
            }
            Texture2D texture;
            if (Cache.TryGetValue(kind, out texture) && texture != null)
            {
                return texture;
            }

            texture = new Texture2D(Size, Size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, true)
            {
                name = "FF_" + kind,
                wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Repeat,
                filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear,
                anisoLevel = 2
            };
            var pixels = new Color[Size * Size];
            for (int y = 0; y < Size; y++)
            {
                for (int x = 0; x < Size; x++)
                {
                    float u = (x + 0.5f) / Size;
                    float v = (y + 0.5f) / Size;
                    pixels[y * Size + x] = Sample(kind, x, y, u, v);
                }
            }
            texture.SetPixels(pixels);
            texture.Apply(true);
            Cache[kind] = texture;
            return texture;
        }

        private static Color Gray(float v)
        {
            return new Color(v, v, v, 1f);
        }

        /// <summary>Deterministic pseudo noise in 0..1 so textures look the same on every run.</summary>
        private static float Noise(int x, int y, int seed)
        {
            unchecked
            {
                uint h = (uint)(x * 374761393 + y * 668265263 + seed * 144665);
                h = (h ^ (h >> 13)) * 1274126177u;
                return ((h ^ (h >> 16)) & 1023u) / 1023f;
            }
        }

        private static Color Sample(TextureKind kind, int x, int y, float u, float v)
        {
            switch (kind)
            {
                case TextureKind.Panel:
                {
                    // Metal floor plate: bevelled edge, inner groove, four rivets, light speckle.
                    float edge = Mathf.Min(Mathf.Min(u, 1f - u), Mathf.Min(v, 1f - v));
                    float value = 0.96f + (Noise(x, y, 1) - 0.5f) * 0.05f;
                    if (edge < 0.04f) value = 0.62f;
                    else if (edge < 0.08f) value = 1.0f;
                    else if (edge < 0.11f) value = 0.80f;
                    float rivet = Mathf.Min(Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(u, v), new Vector2(0.2f, 0.2f)),
                        Mathf.Min(Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(u, v), new Vector2(0.8f, 0.2f)),
                        Mathf.Min(Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(u, v), new Vector2(0.2f, 0.8f)),
                                  Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(u, v), new Vector2(0.8f, 0.8f)))));
                    if (rivet < 0.045f) value *= 0.72f;
                    return Gray(value);
                }
                case TextureKind.Rollers:
                case TextureKind.RollersH:
                {
                    // Belt surface: transverse rollers with a bright ridge and a dark gap.
                    // Rollers varies along v, RollersH along u (for belts that run along the other axis).
                    float t = Mathf.Repeat((kind == TextureKind.Rollers ? v : u) * 4f, 1f);
                    float value = t < 0.12f ? 0.55f : 0.78f + 0.22f * Mathf.Sin(t * Mathf.PI);
                    return Gray(value + (Noise(x, y, 2) - 0.5f) * 0.04f);
                }
                case TextureKind.Wood:
                {
                    // Vertical planks with grain.
                    float plank = Mathf.Repeat(u * 3f, 1f);
                    float value = plank < 0.06f ? 0.50f : 0.86f + (Noise(x / 2, y * 3, 3) - 0.5f) * 0.18f;
                    return Gray(value);
                }
                case TextureKind.Hazard:
                {
                    // Orange and graphite diagonal warning stripes (colours baked in).
                    bool orange = Mathf.Repeat((u + v) * 4f, 1f) < 0.5f;
                    return orange ? new Color(1f, 0.62f, 0.14f, 1f) : new Color(0.16f, 0.18f, 0.22f, 1f);
                }
                case TextureKind.Grid:
                {
                    // Factory floor: faint tiles with joints.
                    float edge = Mathf.Min(Mathf.Min(u, 1f - u), Mathf.Min(v, 1f - v));
                    float value = edge < 0.02f ? 0.78f : 0.94f + (Noise(x, y, 4) - 0.5f) * 0.08f;
                    return Gray(value);
                }
                case TextureKind.Blob:
                {
                    // Radial falloff in the alpha channel: a soft round contact shadow.
                    float d = Mathf.Clamp01(Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(u, v), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f)) * 2f);
                    float alpha = Mathf.Pow(1f - d, 1.6f);
                    return new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, alpha);
                }
            }
            return Color.white;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/SafeAreaFitter.cs   (43 lines)
====================================================================================================
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Keeps a full-stretch RectTransform inside Screen.safeArea (notches, camera cut-outs, gesture bars).</summary>
    [RequireComponent(typeof(RectTransform))]
    public sealed class SafeAreaFitter : MonoBehaviour
    {
        private Rect lastSafeArea;
        private Vector2Int lastScreen;

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            Apply();
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (Screen.safeArea != lastSafeArea || lastScreen.x != Screen.width || lastScreen.y != Screen.height)
            {
                Apply();
            }
        }

        private void Apply()
        {
            Rect safe = Screen.safeArea;
            lastSafeArea = safe;
            lastScreen = new Vector2Int(Screen.width, Screen.height);
            if (Screen.width <= 0 || Screen.height <= 0)
            {
                return;
            }

            var rect = (RectTransform)transform;
            rect.anchorMin = new Vector2(safe.xMin / Screen.width, safe.yMin / Screen.height);
            rect.anchorMax = new Vector2(safe.xMax / Screen.width, safe.yMax / Screen.height);
            rect.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        }
    }
}
