FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/ScreenshotTour.cs   (131 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Walks through the screens of the real game and saves screenshots plus a short log with the number of
    /// Console errors. Start it from the menu Flow Factory > Capture Screenshot Tour (in the Editor, with the
    /// Game view set to 1080x1920) or from a build with the command line arguments  -fftour &lt;folder&gt;.
    /// </summary>
    public static class ScreenshotTour
    {
        public static bool Requested(out string folder)
        {
            folder = null;
            string[] args = Environment.GetCommandLineArgs();
            for (int i = 0; i < args.Length - 1; i++)
            {
                if (args[i] == "-fftour")
                {
                    folder = args[i + 1];
                    return true;
                }
            }
#if UNITY_EDITOR
            string session = UnityEditor.SessionState.GetString("ff.tour", "");
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(session))
            {
                UnityEditor.SessionState.EraseString("ff.tour");
                folder = session;
                return true;
            }
#endif
            return false;
        }

        public static IEnumerator Run(FlowFactoryApp app, string folder)
        {
            Directory.CreateDirectory(folder);
            var log = new StringBuilder();
            int errors = 0;
            int exceptions = 0;
            Application.LogCallback counter = (message, stack, type) =>
            {
                if (type == LogType.Error || type == LogType.Assert) errors++;
                if (type == LogType.Exception) { errors++; exceptions++; }
                if (type == LogType.Error || type == LogType.Exception) log.AppendLine("  CONSOLE " + type + ": " + message);
            };
            Application.logMessageReceived += counter;

            log.AppendLine("FlowFactory screenshot tour " + DateTime.Now.ToString("s"));
            log.AppendLine("Unity " + Application.unityVersion + ", platform " + Application.platform + ", screen " + Screen.width + "x" + Screen.height);

            yield return null;
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_ResetEverything();
            app.Automation_UnlockAll();

            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "01_menu", 1.5f);
            app.Automation_ShowDailyGift();
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "02_daily_gift", 0.5f);
            app.Automation_ShowMenu();
            app.Automation_ShowLevelSelect();
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "03_level_select", 0.5f);
            app.Automation_ShowSettings();
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "04_settings", 0.5f);

            app.Automation_OpenLevel(1);
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "05_level01_start", 0.8f);
            app.Automation_ApplySolutionTaps();
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_PressRun();
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "06_level01_running", 0.9f);
            float waited = 0f;
            while (!app.SuccessCardOpen && waited < 20f) { waited += Time.unscaledDeltaTime; yield return null; }
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "07_level01_success", 1.3f);

            foreach (int id in new[] { 5, 12, 18, 30, 36 })
            {
                app.Automation_OpenLevel(id);
                yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "08_level" + id.ToString("00") + "_start", 0.8f);
                app.Automation_ApplySolutionTaps();
                yield return null;
                app.Automation_PressRun();
                waited = 0f;
                while (!app.SuccessCardOpen && waited < 40f) { waited += Time.unscaledDeltaTime; yield return null; }
                yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "09_level" + id.ToString("00") + "_success", 1.4f);
            }

            app.Automation_OpenLevel(2);
            app.Automation_PressRun(); // unsolved: shows the friendly failure
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "10_failure_feedback", 1.6f);

            app.Economy.AddCoins(400);
            app.Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab.Coins);
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "11_shop_coins", 0.6f);
            app.Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab.Themes);
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "12_shop_themes", 0.6f);
            app.Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab.Parcels);
            yield return Shot(app, folder, log, "13_shop_parcels", 0.6f);

            Application.logMessageReceived -= counter;
            log.AppendLine("Console errors/exceptions during the tour: " + errors + " / " + exceptions);
            File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(folder, "tour_log.txt"), log.ToString());
            Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Screenshot tour finished: " + folder + " (errors: " + errors + ")");

#if UNITY_EDITOR
            UnityEditor.EditorApplication.isPlaying = false;
#else
            Application.Quit();
#endif
        }

        private static IEnumerator Shot(FlowFactoryApp app, string folder, StringBuilder log, string name, float settleSeconds)
        {
            float t = 0f;
            while (t < settleSeconds) { t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime; yield return null; }
            yield return new WaitForEndOfFrame();
            string path = Path.Combine(folder, name + ".png");
            ScreenCapture.CaptureScreenshot(path);
            yield return null;
            yield return null;
            log.AppendLine(name + ": screen=" + app.ScreenName + " level=" + app.CurrentLevelId + " state=" + app.CurrentLevelState +
                           " coins=" + app.Economy.Coins + " " + Screen.width + "x" + Screen.height);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using FlowFactory.Core;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>
    /// TEST rewarded ad: shows a fake countdown screen. No SDK, no network. Replace with a real
    /// IRewardedAdService (for example LevelPlay) once the developer has ad accounts and a privacy review.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SimulatedAdService : IRewardedAdService
    {
        private readonly MonetizationUi ui;

        public SimulatedAdService(MonetizationUi ui)
        {
            this.ui = ui;
        }

        public bool IsReady
        {
            get { return true; }
        }

        public void Show(Action<bool> onCompleted)
        {
            ui.ShowTestAd(onCompleted);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>
    /// TEST store: asks for confirmation in a dialog that says no real money is involved, then reports success.
    /// Replace with a real IStoreService (for example Unity IAP) with the developer's own store products.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class SimulatedStoreService : IStoreService
    {
        private readonly MonetizationUi ui;

        public SimulatedStoreService(MonetizationUi ui)
        {
            this.ui = ui;
        }

        public bool IsReady
        {
            get { return true; }
        }

        public string PriceFor(string productId)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.FindByProduct(productId);
            return item != null ? item.DisplayPrice : null;
        }

        public void Purchase(string productId, Action<bool> onCompleted)
        {
            ShopItem item = ShopCatalog.FindByProduct(productId);
            if (item == null)
            {
                onCompleted(false);
                return;
            }
            ui.ShowModal("TEST PURCHASE", item.Name + " for " + item.DisplayPrice + "\n(simulated: no real money is charged)",
                "BUY", () => onCompleted(true), "CANCEL", () => onCompleted(false));
        }
    }
}

using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    public struct ThemeColors
    {
        public Color FloorLight, FloorDark, Slab, SlabBase, BeltRotatable, RailRotatable, GroundInner, GroundOuter, Lamp;
    }

    public struct ParcelColors
    {
        public Color Box, Tape;
    }

    /// <summary>Cosmetic looks sold in the shop. Themes recolour the board; parcel skins recolour the parcel.</summary>
    public static class ThemeCatalog
    {
        public static ThemeColors Theme(string id)
        {
            switch (id)
            {
                case "theme.candy":
                    return new ThemeColors
                    {
                        FloorLight = new Color(1.00f, 0.91f, 0.94f), FloorDark = new Color(0.96f, 0.80f, 0.87f),
                        Slab = new Color(0.78f, 0.35f, 0.55f), SlabBase = new Color(0.52f, 0.20f, 0.38f),
                        BeltRotatable = new Color(0.20f, 0.74f, 0.62f), RailRotatable = new Color(1.00f, 0.55f, 0.75f),
                        GroundInner = new Color(0.58f, 0.34f, 0.52f), GroundOuter = new Color(0.20f, 0.09f, 0.20f),
                        Lamp = new Color(1.00f, 0.80f, 0.90f)
                    };
                case "theme.midnight":
                    return new ThemeColors
                    {
                        FloorLight = new Color(0.34f, 0.38f, 0.52f), FloorDark = new Color(0.26f, 0.29f, 0.42f),
                        Slab = new Color(0.08f, 0.09f, 0.17f), SlabBase = new Color(0.04f, 0.05f, 0.11f),
                        BeltRotatable = new Color(0.10f, 0.78f, 0.98f), RailRotatable = new Color(0.95f, 0.25f, 0.85f),
                        GroundInner = new Color(0.14f, 0.16f, 0.32f), GroundOuter = new Color(0.02f, 0.02f, 0.07f),
                        Lamp = new Color(0.50f, 0.95f, 1.00f)
                    };
                case "theme.gold":
                    return new ThemeColors
                    {
                        FloorLight = new Color(0.96f, 0.93f, 0.86f), FloorDark = new Color(0.85f, 0.81f, 0.72f),
                        Slab = new Color(0.62f, 0.46f, 0.15f), SlabBase = new Color(0.36f, 0.26f, 0.08f),
                        BeltRotatable = new Color(0.68f, 0.16f, 0.22f), RailRotatable = new Color(1.00f, 0.86f, 0.38f),
                        GroundInner = new Color(0.38f, 0.30f, 0.19f), GroundOuter = new Color(0.08f, 0.06f, 0.04f),
                        Lamp = new Color(1.00f, 0.90f, 0.50f)
                    };
                default: // theme.classic
                    return new ThemeColors
                    {
                        FloorLight = new Color(0.97f, 0.93f, 0.84f), FloorDark = new Color(0.88f, 0.83f, 0.73f),
                        Slab = new Color(0.10f, 0.25f, 0.31f), SlabBase = new Color(0.07f, 0.17f, 0.22f),
                        BeltRotatable = new Color(0.10f, 0.58f, 0.78f), RailRotatable = new Color(1.00f, 0.80f, 0.20f),
                        GroundInner = new Color(0.24f, 0.40f, 0.46f), GroundOuter = new Color(0.04f, 0.09f, 0.12f),
                        Lamp = new Color(1.00f, 0.88f, 0.45f)
                    };
            }
        }

        public static ParcelColors Parcel(string id)
        {
            switch (id)
            {
                case "parcel.gift": return new ParcelColors { Box = new Color(0.86f, 0.16f, 0.22f), Tape = new Color(1.00f, 0.82f, 0.30f) };
                case "parcel.ocean": return new ParcelColors { Box = new Color(0.20f, 0.55f, 0.88f), Tape = new Color(0.95f, 0.98f, 1.00f) };
                case "parcel.gold": return new ParcelColors { Box = new Color(0.98f, 0.80f, 0.25f), Tape = new Color(1.00f, 0.96f, 0.72f) };
                default: return new ParcelColors { Box = new Color(0.96f, 0.64f, 0.18f), Tape = new Color(0.98f, 0.92f, 0.78f) };
            }
        }

        /// <summary>Copies a theme and a parcel skin into the shared palette. Boards built afterwards use them.</summary>
        public static void Apply(string themeId, string parcelId)
        {
            ThemeColors t = Theme(themeId);
            Palette.FloorLight = t.FloorLight;
            Palette.FloorDark = t.FloorDark;
            Palette.Slab = t.Slab;
            Palette.SlabBase = t.SlabBase;
            Palette.BeltRotatable = t.BeltRotatable;
            Palette.RailRotatable = t.RailRotatable;
            Palette.GroundInner = t.GroundInner;
            Palette.GroundOuter = t.GroundOuter;
            Palette.Lamp = t.Lamp;

            ParcelColors p = Parcel(parcelId);
            Palette.Parcel = p.Box;
            Palette.ParcelTape = p.Tape;

            GameMaterials.RefreshGround();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/Ui.cs   (160 lines)
====================================================================================================
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Tiny helpers for building uGUI by code. Layout is in a 1080x1920 reference space.</summary>
    public static class Ui
    {
        public static readonly Color Panel = new Color(0.13f, 0.17f, 0.27f, 0.96f);
        public static readonly Color Primary = new Color(0.20f, 0.65f, 0.40f);
        public static readonly Color Secondary = new Color(0.25f, 0.34f, 0.55f);
        public static readonly Color Danger = new Color(0.75f, 0.28f, 0.28f);
        public static readonly Color Disabled = new Color(0.22f, 0.25f, 0.32f);
        public static readonly Color TextColor = new Color(0.96f, 0.97f, 1f);
        public static readonly Color TextDim = new Color(0.70f, 0.76f, 0.88f);

        private static Font font;

        public static Font DefaultFont
        {
            get
            {
                if (font == null)
                {
                    font = Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
                }
                if (font == null)
                {
                    font = Font.CreateDynamicFontFromOSFont("Arial", 32);
                }
                return font;
            }
        }

        public static RectTransform NewRect(string name, Transform parent)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(name, typeof(RectTransform));
            var rect = (RectTransform)go.transform;
            rect.SetParent(parent, false);
            return rect;
        }

        public static void Stretch(RectTransform rect)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rect.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            rect.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
        }

        /// <summary>Anchors to a point of the parent (0-1) and sets a pixel size and offset from that point.</summary>
        public static void Place(RectTransform rect, Vector2 anchor, Vector2 size, Vector2 offset)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = anchor;
            rect.anchorMax = anchor;
            rect.pivot = anchor;
            rect.sizeDelta = size;
            rect.anchoredPosition = offset;
        }

        /// <summary>Like Place but the pivot stays centred (offset is from the anchor to the rect's centre).</summary>
        public static void PlaceCentered(RectTransform rect, Vector2 anchor, Vector2 size, Vector2 offset)
        {
            rect.anchorMin = anchor;
            rect.anchorMax = anchor;
            rect.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rect.sizeDelta = size;
            rect.anchoredPosition = offset;
        }

        public static Image NewImage(string name, Transform parent, Color color, bool rounded = false)
        {
            RectTransform rect = NewRect(name, parent);
            var image = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
            image.color = color;
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            if (rounded)
            {
                image.sprite = UiSprites.Rounded;
                image.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
            }
            return image;
        }

        public static Text NewLabel(string name, Transform parent, string text, int size, Color color, TextAnchor anchor = TextAnchor.MiddleCenter, FontStyle style = FontStyle.Bold)
        {
            RectTransform rect = NewRect(name, parent);
            var label = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Text>();
            label.font = DefaultFont;
            label.text = text;
            label.fontSize = size;
            label.fontStyle = style;
            label.color = color;
            label.alignment = anchor;
            label.horizontalOverflow = HorizontalWrapMode.Wrap;
            label.verticalOverflow = VerticalWrapMode.Truncate;
            label.resizeTextForBestFit = true;
            label.resizeTextMinSize = Mathf.Max(18, size / 2);
            label.resizeTextMaxSize = size;
            label.raycastTarget = false;
            return label;
        }

        /// <summary>Adds a soft drop shadow behind a graphic.</summary>
        public static void AddShadow(Graphic graphic, float distance = 8f, float alpha = 0.40f)
        {
            var shadow = graphic.gameObject.AddComponent<Shadow>();
            shadow.effectColor = new Color(0f, 0f, 0f, alpha);
            shadow.effectDistance = new Vector2(0f, -distance);
        }

        public static Button NewButton(string name, Transform parent, string text, Color fill, int fontSize, UnityAction onClick)
        {
            RectTransform rect = NewRect(name, parent);
            var image = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Image>();
            image.color = fill;
            image.sprite = UiSprites.Rounded;
            image.type = Image.Type.Sliced;
            AddShadow(image);
            var button = rect.gameObject.AddComponent<Button>();
            button.targetGraphic = image;
            rect.gameObject.AddComponent<ButtonJuice>();

            ColorBlock colors = button.colors;
            colors.normalColor = Color.white;
            colors.highlightedColor = new Color(1.1f, 1.1f, 1.1f);
            colors.pressedColor = new Color(0.78f, 0.78f, 0.78f);
            colors.disabledColor = new Color(0.6f, 0.6f, 0.6f);
            button.colors = colors;

            // A light strip across the top half gives the button a glossy look.
            Image gloss = NewImage("Gloss", rect, new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, 0.16f), true);
            gloss.rectTransform.anchorMin = new Vector2(0.03f, 0.52f);
            gloss.rectTransform.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.97f, 0.97f);
            gloss.rectTransform.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            gloss.rectTransform.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;

            Text label = NewLabel("Label", rect, text, fontSize, TextColor);
            Stretch(label.rectTransform);
            label.rectTransform.offsetMin = new Vector2(12f, 8f);
            label.rectTransform.offsetMax = new Vector2(-12f, -8f);
            AddShadow(label, 4f, 0.35f);

            if (onClick != null) button.onClick.AddListener(onClick);
            return button;
        }

        public static void SetText(Button button, string text)
        {
            button.GetComponentInChildren<Text>().text = text;
        }

        public static void SetFill(Button button, Color fill)
        {
            ((Image)button.targetGraphic).color = fill;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/UiFx.cs   (103 lines)
====================================================================================================
using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Squashes a button a little while it is pressed and lets it spring back.</summary>
    public sealed class ButtonJuice : MonoBehaviour, IPointerDownHandler, IPointerUpHandler, IPointerExitHandler
    {
        private const float PressedScale = 0.93f;

        private RectTransform rect;
        private float current = 1f;
        private float target = 1f;
        private float velocity;

        private void Awake()
        {
            rect = (RectTransform)transform;
        }

        private void OnDisable()
        {
            current = target = 1f;
            velocity = 0f;
        }

        public void OnPointerDown(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            target = PressedScale;
        }

        public void OnPointerUp(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            target = 1f;
        }

        public void OnPointerExit(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            target = 1f;
        }

        private void Update()
        {
            if (Mathf.Approximately(current, target) && Mathf.Abs(velocity) < 0.001f)
            {
                return;
            }

            // A damped spring: quick squash, small overshoot on release.
            float dt = Mathf.Min(Time.unscaledDeltaTime, 0.05f);
            velocity += (target - current) * 320f * dt;
            velocity *= Mathf.Exp(-14f * dt);
            current += velocity * dt;
            if (Mathf.Abs(target - current) < 0.002f && Mathf.Abs(velocity) < 0.02f)
            {
                current = target;
                velocity = 0f;
            }
            rect.localScale = new Vector3(current, current, 1f);
        }
    }

    /// <summary>Fades a screen in (and optionally pops it up slightly) each time it is shown.</summary>
    [RequireComponent(typeof(CanvasGroup))]
    public sealed class ScreenFx : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public bool Pop;

        private CanvasGroup group;

        private void Awake()
        {
            group = GetComponent<CanvasGroup>();
        }

        private void OnEnable()
        {
            if (group == null) group = GetComponent<CanvasGroup>();
            StartCoroutine(Play());
        }

        private IEnumerator Play()
        {
            const float seconds = 0.22f;
            var rect = (RectTransform)transform;
            for (float t = 0f; t < seconds; t += Time.unscaledDeltaTime)
            {
                float k = Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, t / seconds);
                group.alpha = k;
                if (Pop)
                {
                    float s = 0.92f + 0.08f * k + 0.03f * Mathf.Sin(k * Mathf.PI);
                    rect.localScale = new Vector3(s, s, 1f);
                }
                yield return null;
            }
            group.alpha = 1f;
            rect.localScale = Vector3.one;
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Scripts/Runtime/UiSprites.cs   (168 lines)

using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory
{
    /// <summary>Sprites drawn in code: a nine-sliceable rounded rectangle and a play triangle.</summary>
    public static class UiSprites
    {
        private const int Size = 64;
        private static Sprite rounded;
        private static Sprite triangle;
        private static Sprite circle;
        private static Sprite star;

        public static Sprite Rounded
        {
            get
            {
                if (rounded == null)
                {
                    Texture2D texture = NewTexture("FF_Rounded");
                    const float radius = 22f;
                    var pixels = new Color[Size * Size];
                    for (int y = 0; y < Size; y++)
                    {
                        for (int x = 0; x < Size; x++)
                        {
                            // Distance to the rounded-rectangle outline (negative inside), anti-aliased over one pixel.
                            float dx = Mathf.Abs(x + 0.5f - Size * 0.5f) - (Size * 0.5f - radius);
                            float dy = Mathf.Abs(y + 0.5f - Size * 0.5f) - (Size * 0.5f - radius);
                            float outside = new Vector2(Mathf.Max(dx, 0f), Mathf.Max(dy, 0f)).magnitude + Mathf.Min(Mathf.Max(dx, dy), 0f) - radius;
                            pixels[y * Size + x] = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, Mathf.Clamp01(0.5f - outside));
                        }
                    }
                    texture.SetPixels(pixels);
                    texture.Apply();
                    rounded = Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, Size, Size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), 100f, 0, SpriteMeshType.FullRect, new Vector4(24f, 24f, 24f, 24f));
                }
                return rounded;
            }
        }

        public static Sprite Triangle
        {
            get
            {
                if (triangle == null)
                {
                    Texture2D texture = NewTexture("FF_Triangle");
                    var a = new Vector2(16f, 8f);
                    var b = new Vector2(16f, 56f);
                    var c = new Vector2(56f, 32f);
                    var pixels = new Color[Size * Size];
                    for (int y = 0; y < Size; y++)
                    {
                        for (int x = 0; x < Size; x++)
                        {
                            var p = new Vector2(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f);
                            float d = Mathf.Min(Edge(a, b, c, p), Mathf.Min(Edge(b, c, a, p), Edge(c, a, b, p)));
                            pixels[y * Size + x] = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, Mathf.Clamp01(d + 0.5f));
                        }
                    }
                    texture.SetPixels(pixels);
                    texture.Apply();
                    triangle = Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, Size, Size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), 100f);
                }
                return triangle;
            }
        }

        public static Sprite Circle
        {
            get
            {
                if (circle == null)
                {
                    Texture2D texture = NewTexture("FF_Circle");
                    var pixels = new Color[Size * Size];
                    for (int y = 0; y < Size; y++)
                    {
                        for (int x = 0; x < Size; x++)
                        {
                            float d = Vector2.Distance(new Vector2(x + 0.5f, y + 0.5f), new Vector2(Size * 0.5f, Size * 0.5f)) - (Size * 0.5f - 1f);
                            pixels[y * Size + x] = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, Mathf.Clamp01(0.5f - d));
                        }
                    }
                    texture.SetPixels(pixels);
                    texture.Apply();
                    circle = Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, Size, Size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), 100f);
                }
                return circle;
            }
        }

        /// <summary>A five-pointed star, used for level ratings.</summary>
        public static Sprite Star
        {
            get
            {
                if (star == null)
                {
                    Texture2D texture = NewTexture("FF_Star");
                    var points = new Vector2[10];
                    for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
                    {
                        float radius = (i % 2 == 0 ? 30f : 13f);
                        float angle = Mathf.PI * 0.5f + i * Mathf.PI / 5f;
                        points[i] = new Vector2(32f + Mathf.Cos(angle) * radius, 34f + Mathf.Sin(angle) * radius);
                    }
                    var pixels = new Color[Size * Size];
                    for (int y = 0; y < Size; y++)
                    {
                        for (int x = 0; x < Size; x++)
                        {
                            // 3x3 supersampling gives smooth edges.
                            int inside = 0;
                            for (int sy = 0; sy < 3; sy++)
                            {
                                for (int sx = 0; sx < 3; sx++)
                                {
                                    if (Inside(points, new Vector2(x + (sx + 0.5f) / 3f, y + (sy + 0.5f) / 3f))) inside++;
                                }
                            }
                            pixels[y * Size + x] = new Color(1f, 1f, 1f, inside / 9f);
                        }
                    }
                    texture.SetPixels(pixels);
                    texture.Apply();
                    star = Sprite.Create(texture, new Rect(0, 0, Size, Size), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), 100f);
                }
                return star;
            }
        }

        private static bool Inside(Vector2[] polygon, Vector2 p)
        {
            bool result = false;
            for (int i = 0, j = polygon.Length - 1; i < polygon.Length; j = i++)
            {
                if ((polygon[i].y > p.y) != (polygon[j].y > p.y) &&
                    p.x < (polygon[j].x - polygon[i].x) * (p.y - polygon[i].y) / (polygon[j].y - polygon[i].y) + polygon[i].x)
                {
                    result = !result;
                }
            }
            return result;
        }

        /// <summary>Signed distance of p to the edge a-b, positive on the side of the third corner.</summary>
        private static float Edge(Vector2 a, Vector2 b, Vector2 inside, Vector2 p)
        {
            Vector2 ab = b - a;
            Vector2 normal = new Vector2(-ab.y, ab.x).normalized;
            if (Vector2.Dot(normal, inside - a) < 0f) normal = -normal;
            return Vector2.Dot(normal, p - a);
        }

        private static Texture2D NewTexture(string name)
        {
            return new Texture2D(Size, Size, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false)
            {
                name = name,
                wrapMode = TextureWrapMode.Clamp,
                filterMode = FilterMode.Bilinear
            };
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using UnityEditor;
using UnityEditor.Build;
using UnityEditor.Build.Reporting;
using UnityEditor.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory.EditorTools
{
    /// <summary>Editor menu: level generation and validation, one-click project setup, Android build.</summary>
    public static class FlowFactoryMenu
    {
        private const string LevelFolder = "Assets/Resources/Levels";
        private const string ScenePath = "Assets/Scenes/Main.unity";
        private const string DevApkPath = "Builds/Android/FlowFactory3D-dev.apk";
        private const string ReleaseApkPath = "Builds/Android/FlowFactory3D.apk";

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ levels

        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Generate Level Assets (create missing)")]
        public static void GenerateMissingLevels()
        {
            GenerateLevels(false);
        }

        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Regenerate All Level Assets (overwrites edits)")]
        public static void RegenerateAllLevels()
        {
            if (!EditorUtility.DisplayDialog("Regenerate levels",
                    "This overwrites every level asset in " + LevelFolder + " with the built-in catalog, discarding manual edits. Continue?",
                    "Overwrite", "Cancel"))
            {
                return;
            }
            GenerateLevels(true);
        }

        private static void GenerateLevels(bool overwrite)
        {
            EnsureFolder("Assets/Resources");
            EnsureFolder(LevelFolder);

            int created = 0;
            int updated = 0;
            foreach (LevelData data in LevelCatalog.BuildAll())
            {
                string path = LevelFolder + "/Level_" + data.id.ToString("00") + ".asset";
                var asset = AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<LevelDefinition>(path);
                if (asset == null)
                {
                    asset = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<LevelDefinition>();
                    asset.CopyFrom(data);
                    AssetDatabase.CreateAsset(asset, path);
                    created++;
                }
                else if (overwrite)
                {
                    asset.CopyFrom(data);
                    EditorUtility.SetDirty(asset);
                    updated++;
                }
            }
            AssetDatabase.SaveAssets();
            AssetDatabase.Refresh();
            Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Level assets: " + created + " created, " + updated + " overwritten.");
            ValidateLevels();
        }

        /// <summary>Checks every level asset and its known solution. Returns the number of problems.</summary>
        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Validate All Levels (known solutions)")]
        public static int ValidateLevels()
        {
            string[] guids = AssetDatabase.FindAssets("t:LevelDefinition", new[] { "Assets" });
            var assets = new List<LevelDefinition>();
            foreach (string guid in guids)
            {
                assets.Add(AssetDatabase.LoadAssetAtPath<LevelDefinition>(AssetDatabase.GUIDToAssetPath(guid)));
            }
            assets.Sort((a, b) => a.levelId.CompareTo(b.levelId));

            int problems = 0;
            if (assets.Count == 0)
            {
                Debug.LogWarning("[FlowFactory] No LevelDefinition assets found. Use Flow Factory > Generate Level Assets. (The game falls back to the built-in catalog.)");
                return 0;
            }

            for (int i = 0; i < assets.Count; i++)
            {
                LevelData data = assets[i].ToLevelData();
                List<string> errors = LevelValidator.Validate(data);
                if (data.id != i + 1) errors.Add("Level ids must be 1..N without gaps (found " + data.id + " at position " + (i + 1) + ")");

                if (errors.Count == 0)
                {
                    RouteResult solved = RouteValidator.Validate(data, data.solution);
                    Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Level " + data.id + " '" + data.title + "' OK: " + data.width + "x" + data.height +
                              ", solution path " + solved.Path.Count + " cells, minimum " + data.MinimumTaps() + " taps.", assets[i]);
                }
                else
                {
                    problems += errors.Count;
                    foreach (string error in errors) Debug.LogError("[FlowFactory] " + error, assets[i]);
                }
            }

            Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Level validation finished: " + assets.Count + " levels, " + problems + " problem(s).");
            return problems;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ setup

        /// <summary>Command line: Unity -batchmode -quit -projectPath . -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.SetUpProjectBatch</summary>
        public static void SetUpProjectBatch()
        {
            try
            {
                SetUpProject();
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                Debug.LogError("[FlowFactory] Setup failed: " + e);
                EditorApplication.Exit(1);
            }
        }

        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Setup Project (portrait, Android, Main scene)")]
        public static void SetUpProject()
        {
            PlayerSettings.productName = "Flow Factory 3D";
            PlayerSettings.defaultInterfaceOrientation = UIOrientation.Portrait;
            PlayerSettings.allowedAutorotateToPortrait = true;
            PlayerSettings.allowedAutorotateToPortraitUpsideDown = false;
            PlayerSettings.allowedAutorotateToLandscapeLeft = false;
            PlayerSettings.allowedAutorotateToLandscapeRight = false;

            PlayerSettings.SetApplicationIdentifier(NamedBuildTarget.Android, "com.flowfactory.prototype3d");
            PlayerSettings.SetScriptingBackend(NamedBuildTarget.Android, ScriptingImplementation.IL2CPP);
            PlayerSettings.Android.targetArchitectures = AndroidArchitecture.ARM64;
            PlayerSettings.Android.minSdkVersion = AndroidSdkVersions.AndroidApiLevel26;
            PlayerSettings.Android.targetSdkVersion = AndroidSdkVersions.AndroidApiLevelAuto;
            PlayerSettings.SetManagedStrippingLevel(NamedBuildTarget.Android, ManagedStrippingLevel.Medium);
            PlayerSettings.SetUseDefaultGraphicsAPIs(BuildTarget.Android, false);
            PlayerSettings.SetGraphicsAPIs(BuildTarget.Android, new[] { UnityEngine.Rendering.GraphicsDeviceType.OpenGLES3, UnityEngine.Rendering.GraphicsDeviceType.Vulkan });
            PlayerSettings.bundleVersion = "0.3.1";
            PlayerSettings.Android.bundleVersionCode = 3;
            PlayerSettings.gpuSkinning = false;

            GenerateLevels(false);
            EnsureMainScene();
            Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Project set up: portrait, Android IL2CPP/ARM64, level assets, " + ScenePath + " in Build Settings.");
        }

        private static void EnsureMainScene()
        {
            if (!File.Exists(ScenePath))
            {
                // In batch mode there is nobody to ask, and the freshly created project has nothing unsaved.
                if (!Application.isBatchMode && !EditorSceneManager.SaveCurrentModifiedScenesIfUserWantsTo())
                {
                    return;
                }
                EnsureFolder("Assets/Scenes");
                var scene = EditorSceneManager.NewScene(NewSceneSetup.DefaultGameObjects, NewSceneMode.Single);
                new GameObject("FlowFactoryApp").AddComponent<FlowFactoryApp>();
                EditorSceneManager.SaveScene(scene, ScenePath);
            }
            EditorBuildSettings.scenes = new[] { new EditorBuildSettingsScene(ScenePath, true) };
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ build

        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Build Android APK (development, FPS display)")]
        public static void BuildAndroidDevelopment()
        {
            Build(DevApkPath, BuildOptions.Development);
        }

        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Build Android APK (release)")]
        public static void BuildAndroidRelease()
        {
            Build(ReleaseApkPath, BuildOptions.None);
        }

        /// <summary>Command line: Unity -batchmode -quit -projectPath . -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.BuildAndroidBatch</summary>
        public static void BuildAndroidBatch()
        {
            bool ok = Build(DevApkPath, BuildOptions.Development);
            EditorApplication.Exit(ok ? 0 : 1);
        }

        private static bool Build(string outputPath, BuildOptions options)
        {
            if (ValidateLevels() > 0)
            {
                Debug.LogError("[FlowFactory] Build cancelled: fix the level problems first.");
                return false;
            }

            EnsureMainScene();
            EditorUserBuildSettings.buildAppBundle = false;
            Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(outputPath));

            var buildOptions = new BuildPlayerOptions
            {
                scenes = new[] { ScenePath },
                locationPathName = outputPath,
                target = BuildTarget.Android,
                targetGroup = BuildTargetGroup.Android,
                options = options
            };

            BuildReport report = BuildPipeline.BuildPlayer(buildOptions);
            bool ok = report.summary.result == BuildResult.Succeeded;
            if (ok)
            {
                Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Android build succeeded: " + outputPath + " (" + report.summary.totalSize / (1024 * 1024) + " MB)");
            }
            else
            {
                Debug.LogError("[FlowFactory] Android build failed: " + report.summary.result + ", errors: " + report.summary.totalErrors);
            }
            return ok;
        }

        // ------------------------------------------------------------ diagnostics

        /// <summary>Prints (and saves) what this Unity installation offers, so problems can be diagnosed from a copy of the text.</summary>
        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Environment Report")]
        public static void EnvironmentReport()
        {
            var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
            sb.AppendLine("FlowFactory environment report " + DateTime.Now.ToString("s"));
            sb.AppendLine("Unity " + Application.unityVersion + " on " + Application.platform);
            sb.AppendLine("Active build target: " + EditorUserBuildSettings.activeBuildTarget);
            sb.AppendLine("Android Build Support installed: " + BuildPipeline.IsBuildTargetSupported(BuildTargetGroup.Android, BuildTarget.Android));
            sb.AppendLine("Render pipeline asset: " + (UnityEngine.Rendering.GraphicsSettings.defaultRenderPipeline != null ? UnityEngine.Rendering.GraphicsSettings.defaultRenderPipeline.name : "none (Built-in)"));
            sb.AppendLine("Color space: " + PlayerSettings.colorSpace);
            sb.AppendLine("Orientation: " + PlayerSettings.defaultInterfaceOrientation);
            sb.AppendLine("Android scripting backend: " + PlayerSettings.GetScriptingBackend(NamedBuildTarget.Android));
            sb.AppendLine("Android min SDK: " + PlayerSettings.Android.minSdkVersion + ", architectures: " + PlayerSettings.Android.targetArchitectures);
            sb.AppendLine("Application id: " + PlayerSettings.GetApplicationIdentifier(NamedBuildTarget.Android));
            sb.AppendLine("Scenes in build: " + EditorBuildSettings.scenes.Length);
            sb.AppendLine("Level assets: " + AssetDatabase.FindAssets("t:LevelDefinition").Length + " (catalog has " + LevelCatalog.Count + ")");
            sb.AppendLine("Level problems: " + ValidateLevels());
            string text = sb.ToString();
            Debug.Log(text);
            Directory.CreateDirectory("Logs");
            File.WriteAllText("Logs/flowfactory_environment.txt", text);
        }

        [MenuItem("Flow Factory/Capture Screenshot Tour (Game view 1080x1920)")]
        public static void CaptureScreenshotTour()
        {
            string folder = Path.GetFullPath("Logs/ScreenshotTour");
            SessionState.SetString("ff.tour", folder);
            Debug.Log("[FlowFactory] Screenshot tour starts. Output: " + folder);
            EditorApplication.EnterPlaymode();
        }

        private static void EnsureFolder(string path)
        {
            if (AssetDatabase.IsValidFolder(path))
            {
                return;
            }
            string parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(path).Replace('\\', '/');
            EnsureFolder(parent);
            AssetDatabase.CreateFolder(parent, Path.GetFileName(path));
        }
    }
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Resources/Shaders/FlowFactoryBlob.shader   (57 lines)
====================================================================================================
// Soft round contact shadow: a flat quad with an alpha texture, blended over the floor.
Shader "FlowFactory/BlobShadow"
{
    Properties
    {
        _Color ("Shadow colour", Color) = (0, 0, 0, 0.4)
        _MainTex ("Alpha texture", 2D) = "white" {}
    }
    SubShader
    {
        Tags { "RenderType" = "Transparent" "Queue" = "Transparent" "IgnoreProjector" = "True" }
        Pass
        {
            Blend SrcAlpha OneMinusSrcAlpha
            ZWrite Off
            Offset -1, -1

            CGPROGRAM
            #pragma vertex vert
            #pragma fragment frag
            #include "UnityCG.cginc"

            fixed4 _Color;
            sampler2D _MainTex;
            float4 _MainTex_ST;

            struct appdata
            {
                float4 vertex : POSITION;
                float2 uv : TEXCOORD0;
            };

            struct v2f
            {
                float4 pos : SV_POSITION;
                float2 uv : TEXCOORD0;
            };

            v2f vert (appdata v)
            {
                v2f o;
                o.pos = UnityObjectToClipPos(v.vertex);
                o.uv = TRANSFORM_TEX(v.uv, _MainTex);
                return o;
            }

            fixed4 frag (v2f i) : SV_Target
            {
                fixed a = tex2D(_MainTex, i.uv).a;
                return fixed4(_Color.rgb, _Color.a * a);
            }
            ENDCG
        }
    }
    Fallback Off
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Resources/Shaders/FlowFactoryGround.shader   (57 lines)
====================================================================================================
// Factory floor: a tiled texture in world space, bright near the board and fading to dark at the edges.
Shader "FlowFactory/Ground"
{
    Properties
    {
        _Inner ("Inner colour", Color) = (0.20, 0.36, 0.42, 1)
        _Outer ("Outer colour", Color) = (0.05, 0.10, 0.14, 1)
        _Radius ("Fade radius", Float) = 9
        _MainTex ("Texture", 2D) = "white" {}
    }
    SubShader
    {
        Tags { "RenderType" = "Opaque" "Queue" = "Geometry" }
        Pass
        {
            CGPROGRAM
            #pragma vertex vert
            #pragma fragment frag
            #include "UnityCG.cginc"

            fixed4 _Inner;
            fixed4 _Outer;
            float _Radius;
            sampler2D _MainTex;

            struct appdata
            {
                float4 vertex : POSITION;
            };

            struct v2f
            {
                float4 pos : SV_POSITION;
                float2 world : TEXCOORD0;
            };

            v2f vert (appdata v)
            {
                v2f o;
                o.pos = UnityObjectToClipPos(v.vertex);
                o.world = mul(unity_ObjectToWorld, v.vertex).xz;
                return o;
            }

            fixed4 frag (v2f i) : SV_Target
            {
                float fade = saturate(length(i.world) / _Radius);
                fixed3 tint = lerp(_Inner.rgb, _Outer.rgb, fade * fade);
                fixed4 tex = tex2D(_MainTex, i.world * 0.5);
                return fixed4(tint * tex.rgb, 1);
            }
            ENDCG
        }
    }
    Fallback "Unlit/Color"
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: Assets/Resources/Shaders/FlowFactoryToon.shader   (62 lines)
====================================================================================================
// Flat colour (optionally times a texture) with a fixed fake light, so the game looks the same in the
// Built-in and URP pipelines and needs no scene lights, shadows or imported textures.
// Lives in Resources so it is always included in builds.
Shader "FlowFactory/ToonColor"
{
    Properties
    {
        _Color ("Color", Color) = (1, 1, 1, 1)
        _MainTex ("Texture", 2D) = "white" {}
    }
    SubShader
    {
        Tags { "RenderType" = "Opaque" "Queue" = "Geometry" }
        Pass
        {
            CGPROGRAM
            #pragma vertex vert
            #pragma fragment frag
            #include "UnityCG.cginc"

            fixed4 _Color;
            sampler2D _MainTex;
            float4 _MainTex_ST;

            struct appdata
            {
                float4 vertex : POSITION;
                float3 normal : NORMAL;
                float2 uv : TEXCOORD0;
            };

            struct v2f
            {
                float4 pos : SV_POSITION;
                float2 uv : TEXCOORD0;
                half shade : TEXCOORD1;
            };

            v2f vert (appdata v)
            {
                v2f o;
                o.pos = UnityObjectToClipPos(v.vertex);
                o.uv = TRANSFORM_TEX(v.uv, _MainTex);
                float3 n = UnityObjectToWorldNormal(v.normal);
                float3 l = normalize(float3(-0.35, 0.85, -0.4));
                float height = mul(unity_ObjectToWorld, v.vertex).y;
                // Light from above, slightly darker near the floor (cheap ambient occlusion).
                o.shade = (saturate(dot(n, l)) * 0.55 + 0.45) * lerp(0.80, 1.0, saturate(height * 1.6 + 0.9));
                return o;
            }

            fixed4 frag (v2f i) : SV_Target
            {
                fixed4 tex = tex2D(_MainTex, i.uv);
                return fixed4(_Color.rgb * tex.rgb * i.shade, 1);
            }
            ENDCG
        }
    }
    Fallback "Unlit/Color"
}

FILE: Assets/Tests/EditMode/CoreTests.cs   (395 lines)

using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using NUnit.Framework;

namespace FlowFactory.Tests
{
    public class RouteValidatorTests
    {
        private static LevelData Level(int number)
        {
            return LevelCatalog.BuildAll()[number - 1];
        }

        [Test]
        public void ValidPath_ReachesTarget()
        {
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            RouteResult result = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.solution);

            Assert.IsTrue(result.Success);
            Assert.AreEqual(FailureReason.None, result.Reason);
            // source (1,2) -> corner (1,1) -> target (2,1)
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(
                new[] { new GridPoint(1, 2), new GridPoint(1, 1), new GridPoint(2, 1) },
                result.Path);
        }

        [Test]
        public void InitialState_IsNotSolved_ForEveryLevel()
        {
            foreach (LevelData level in LevelCatalog.BuildAll())
            {
                Assert.IsFalse(RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.InitialRotations()).Success, "level " + level.id);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void BlockedPath_FailsAtBlockedCell()
        {
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            level.tiles[level.IndexOf(1, 1)] = new TileSpec(TileKind.Block, 0, false);

            RouteResult result = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.solution);

            Assert.IsFalse(result.Success);
            Assert.AreEqual(FailureReason.BlockedCell, result.Reason);
            Assert.AreEqual(new GridPoint(1, 1), result.FailCell);
        }

        [Test]
        public void MissingConnection_EmptyCellAhead_Fails()
        {
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            level.tiles[level.IndexOf(1, 1)] = new TileSpec(TileKind.Empty, 0, false);

            RouteResult result = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.solution);

            Assert.IsFalse(result.Success);
            Assert.AreEqual(FailureReason.EmptyCell, result.Reason);
            Assert.AreEqual(new GridPoint(1, 1), result.FailCell);
            Assert.AreEqual(Direction.North, result.FailDirection);
            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(new[] { new GridPoint(1, 2) }, result.Path);
        }

        [Test]
        public void WrongConnection_BeltFacingAway_Fails()
        {
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            int[] rotations = level.InitialRotations(); // corner still at N+E: no opening on the south side

            RouteResult result = RouteValidator.Validate(level, rotations);

            Assert.IsFalse(result.Success);
            Assert.AreEqual(FailureReason.WrongConnection, result.Reason);
            Assert.AreEqual(new GridPoint(1, 1), result.FailCell);
        }

        [Test]
        public void LeavingTheBoard_Fails()
        {
            // Source opens to the north at the top row of a board: the parcel leaves immediately.
            var level = new LevelData
            {
                id = 99, width = 2, height = 2,
                tiles = new[]
                {
                    new TileSpec(TileKind.Source, 0, false), new TileSpec(TileKind.Empty, 0, false),
                    new TileSpec(TileKind.Empty, 0, false), new TileSpec(TileKind.Target, 0, false)
                },
                solution = new int[4]
            };

            RouteResult result = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.InitialRotations());

            Assert.AreEqual(FailureReason.OffBoard, result.Reason);
            Assert.AreEqual(new GridPoint(0, 0), result.FailCell);
        }

        [Test]
        public void StepLimit_StopsRunawayRoutes()
        {
            LevelData level = Level(9); // a long snake
            RouteResult result = RouteValidator.Validate(level, level.solution, 3);

            Assert.IsFalse(result.Success);
            Assert.AreEqual(FailureReason.StepLimit, result.Reason);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Conveyor_ExitIsTheOtherOpening()
        {
            Direction exit;
            Assert.IsTrue(TileGeometry.TryGetExit(TileKind.Corner, 1, Direction.East, out exit)); // E+S
            Assert.AreEqual(Direction.South, exit);
            Assert.IsTrue(TileGeometry.TryGetExit(TileKind.Straight, 1, Direction.West, out exit)); // E+W
            Assert.AreEqual(Direction.East, exit);
            Assert.IsFalse(TileGeometry.TryGetExit(TileKind.Straight, 1, Direction.North, out exit));
        }
    }

    public class LevelCatalogTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void Has36ValidLevels_WithKnownSolutions()
        {
            IList<LevelData> levels = LevelCatalog.BuildAll();
            Assert.AreEqual(36, levels.Count);
            for (int i = 0; i < levels.Count; i++)
            {
                Assert.AreEqual(i + 1, levels[i].id);
                CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(LevelValidator.Validate(levels[i]), "level " + (i + 1));
                Assert.GreaterOrEqual(levels[i].MinimumTaps(), 1, "level " + (i + 1));
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void BoardSizes_Are4x4Early_And5x5Later()
        {
            IList<LevelData> levels = LevelCatalog.BuildAll();
            for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) Assert.AreEqual(4, levels[i].width, "level " + (i + 1));
            for (int i = 4; i < levels.Count; i++) Assert.AreEqual(5, levels[i].width, "level " + (i + 1));
            foreach (LevelData level in levels) Assert.AreEqual(level.width, level.height);
        }

        [Test]
        public void OnlyFirstTwoLevels_AreTutorials()
        {
            IList<LevelData> levels = LevelCatalog.BuildAll();
            for (int i = 0; i < levels.Count; i++) Assert.AreEqual(i < 2, levels[i].isTutorial);
        }

        [Test]
        public void LevelOne_NeedsExactlyOneTap()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(1, LevelCatalog.BuildAll()[0].MinimumTaps());
        }

        [Test]
        public void Difficulty_IsNonDecreasingInRotatableTiles_Roughly()
        {
            IList<LevelData> levels = LevelCatalog.BuildAll();
            Assert.Less(levels[0].RotatableCount, levels[4].RotatableCount);
            Assert.Less(levels[4].RotatableCount, levels[10].RotatableCount);
        }
    }

    public class LevelManagerTests
    {
        private static LevelManager Loaded(int number)
        {
            var manager = new LevelManager();
            manager.Load(LevelCatalog.BuildAll()[number - 1]);
            return manager;
        }

        [Test]
        public void Tap_RotatesExactlyOneTileOnce()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(1);
            int rotations = 0;
            manager.TileRotated += (x, y) => rotations++;
            int index = manager.Level.IndexOf(1, 1);
            int before = manager.Rotations[index];

            Assert.IsTrue(manager.TryRotate(1, 1));

            Assert.AreEqual(1, rotations);
            Assert.AreEqual((before + 1) & 3, manager.Rotations[index]);
            for (int i = 0; i < manager.Rotations.Length; i++)
            {
                if (i != index) Assert.AreEqual(manager.Level.tiles[i].rotation, manager.Rotations[i]);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void Tap_OnFixedOrEmptyTile_DoesNothing()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(1);
            Assert.IsFalse(manager.TryRotate(1, 2)); // source
            Assert.IsFalse(manager.TryRotate(0, 0)); // empty
            Assert.IsFalse(manager.TryRotate(-1, 0));
            Assert.AreEqual(0, manager.TapCount);
        }

        [Test]
        public void ValidRun_EndsInSuccess()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(1);
            manager.TryRotate(1, 1);

            RouteResult result = manager.Run();
            Assert.IsTrue(result.Success);
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Running, manager.State);

            manager.CompleteRun();
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Success, manager.State);
        }

        [Test]
        public void InvalidRun_FailsFriendly_AndReturnsToEditing()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(1);

            RouteResult result = manager.Run();
            Assert.IsFalse(result.Success);
            manager.CompleteRun();
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Failure, manager.State);

            manager.ReturnToEditing();
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Idle, manager.State);
            Assert.IsTrue(manager.TryRotate(1, 1)); // still editable, no penalty
            Assert.AreEqual(1, manager.Attempts);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SecondRun_WhileRunning_IsIgnored()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(1);
            int changes = 0;
            manager.StateChanged += s => changes++;

            Assert.IsNotNull(manager.Run());
            Assert.IsNull(manager.Run());

            Assert.AreEqual(1, manager.Attempts);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, changes);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Rotate_WhileRunning_IsIgnored()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(1);
            manager.Run();
            Assert.IsFalse(manager.TryRotate(1, 1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void EveryLevel_IsSolvableByTapping_InMinimumTaps()
        {
            foreach (LevelData level in LevelCatalog.BuildAll())
            {
                var manager = new LevelManager();
                manager.Load(level);

                for (int i = 0; i < level.tiles.Length; i++)
                {
                    TileSpec tile = level.tiles[i];
                    if (!tile.rotatable) continue;
                    int x = i % level.width;
                    int y = i / level.width;
                    while (TileGeometry.Openings(tile.kind, manager.Rotations[i]) != TileGeometry.Openings(tile.kind, level.solution[i]))
                    {
                        Assert.IsTrue(manager.TryRotate(x, y));
                    }
                }

                Assert.AreEqual(level.MinimumTaps(), manager.TapCount, "level " + level.id);
                RouteResult result = manager.Run();
                Assert.IsTrue(result.Success, "level " + level.id);
                manager.CompleteRun();
                Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Success, manager.State, "level " + level.id);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void Reset_RestoresAllRotations_FromAnyState()
        {
            LevelManager manager = Loaded(5);
            int[] initial = (int[])manager.Rotations.Clone();
            manager.TryRotate(1, 1);
            manager.TryRotate(2, 1);
            manager.TryRotate(2, 1);
            CollectionAssert.AreNotEqual(initial, manager.Rotations);

            manager.Run();
            manager.ResetLevel();

            CollectionAssert.AreEqual(initial, manager.Rotations);
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Idle, manager.State);
        }
    }

    public class SaveServiceTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void NewGame_OnlyFirstLevelUnlocked()
        {
            var save = new SaveService(new MemoryStore(), 12);
            Assert.IsTrue(save.Data.IsUnlocked(1));
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsUnlocked(2));
        }

        [Test]
        public void CompletingLevel_UnlocksTheNext_ButNotBeyond()
        {
            var save = new SaveService(new MemoryStore(), 12);
            save.CompleteLevel(1);
            Assert.IsTrue(save.Data.IsUnlocked(2));
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsUnlocked(3));
            Assert.IsTrue(save.Data.IsCompleted(1));

            save.CompleteLevel(2);
            Assert.IsTrue(save.Data.IsUnlocked(3));
        }

        [Test]
        public void CompletingLastLevel_DoesNotUnlockLevel13()
        {
            var save = new SaveService(new MemoryStore(), 12);
            for (int i = 1; i <= 12; i++) save.CompleteLevel(i);
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsUnlocked(13));
            Assert.AreEqual(12, save.Data.HighestUnlocked);
        }

        [Test]
        public void SaveAndLoad_RoundTrips_AfterRestart()
        {
            var store = new MemoryStore();
            var first = new SaveService(store, 12);
            first.CompleteLevel(1);
            first.CompleteLevel(2);
            first.SetLastLevel(3);
            first.Data.SoundOn = false;
            first.Data.VolumeStep = 1;
            first.Save();

            var second = new SaveService(store, 12); // "app restart": new service over the same storage

            Assert.AreEqual(3, second.Data.HighestUnlocked);
            Assert.AreEqual(3, second.Data.LastLevel);
            Assert.IsTrue(second.Data.IsCompleted(1));
            Assert.IsTrue(second.Data.IsCompleted(2));
            Assert.IsFalse(second.Data.IsCompleted(3));
            Assert.IsFalse(second.Data.SoundOn);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, second.Data.VolumeStep);
        }

        [Test]
        public void DamagedOrNewerSave_FallsBackToDefaults()
        {
            foreach (string text in new[] { "garbage", "v=1;unlocked", "v=99;unlocked=7", "v=1;unlocked=abc;completed=x,y", "" })
            {
                var store = new MemoryStore();
                store.SetString(SaveService.Key, text);
                var save = new SaveService(store, 12);
                Assert.GreaterOrEqual(save.Data.HighestUnlocked, 1, text);
                Assert.LessOrEqual(save.Data.HighestUnlocked, 12, text);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void OutOfRangeValues_AreClamped()
        {
            var store = new MemoryStore();
            store.SetString(SaveService.Key, "v=1;unlocked=500;last=-4;sound=1;volume=9;completed=1,2,77");
            var save = new SaveService(store, 12);
            Assert.AreEqual(12, save.Data.HighestUnlocked);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, save.Data.LastLevel);
            Assert.AreEqual(2, save.Data.VolumeStep);
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsCompleted(77));
        }

        [Test]
        public void ResetAll_ReturnsToFirstLevel()
        {
            var store = new MemoryStore();
            var save = new SaveService(store, 12);
            save.CompleteLevel(1);
            save.ResetAll();
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsUnlocked(2));
            Assert.IsFalse(new SaveService(store, 12).Data.IsUnlocked(2));
        }
    }
}

FILE: Assets/Tests/EditMode/EconomyTests.cs   (335 lines)

using System;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using NUnit.Framework;

namespace FlowFactory.Tests
{
    public class EconomyTests
    {
        private static PlayerEconomy NewEconomy(out SaveService save, MemoryStore store = null)
        {
            save = new SaveService(store ?? new MemoryStore(), 36);
            return new PlayerEconomy(save);
        }

        private static LevelData Level(int id)
        {
            return LevelCatalog.BuildAll()[id - 1];
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- stars and level rewards

        [Test]
        public void Stars_DependOnTapsOnly()
        {
            Assert.AreEqual(3, StarRating.Calculate(4, 4));
            Assert.AreEqual(3, StarRating.Calculate(2, 4));
            Assert.AreEqual(2, StarRating.Calculate(5, 4));
            Assert.AreEqual(2, StarRating.Calculate(6, 4));
            Assert.AreEqual(1, StarRating.Calculate(7, 4));
            Assert.AreEqual(2, StarRating.Calculate(3, 1)); // at least 2 extra taps allowed
            Assert.AreEqual(1, StarRating.Calculate(4, 1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void FirstClear_GivesCoinsStarsAndUnlocksNext()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            LevelData level = Level(1);

            LevelReward reward = economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps());

            Assert.IsTrue(reward.FirstClear);
            Assert.AreEqual(3, reward.Stars);
            Assert.AreEqual(10 + 15 + 10 + 0, reward.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(reward.Coins, economy.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(3, save.Data.StarsFor(1));
            Assert.IsTrue(save.Data.IsUnlocked(2));
        }

        [Test]
        public void Replay_WithoutImprovement_GivesSmallFixedReward_AndNeverLowersStars()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps());
            int before = economy.Coins;

            LevelReward worse = economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps() + 10);

            Assert.IsFalse(worse.FirstClear);
            Assert.IsFalse(worse.ImprovedStars);
            Assert.AreEqual(5, worse.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(before + 5, economy.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(3, save.Data.StarsFor(1));
        }

        [Test]
        public void Replay_WithMoreStars_PaysTheImprovement()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            LevelData level = Level(5);
            economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps() + 20); // 1 star
            LevelReward better = economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps()); // 3 stars

            Assert.IsTrue(better.ImprovedStars);
            Assert.AreEqual(5 + 5 * 2, better.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(3, save.Data.StarsFor(5));
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- coins

        [Test]
        public void Spending_NeverGoesNegative()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            economy.AddCoins(40);
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.TrySpend(41));
            Assert.AreEqual(40, economy.Coins);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.TrySpend(40));
            Assert.AreEqual(0, economy.Coins);
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.TrySpend(-5));
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- shop

        [Test]
        public void FreeItems_AreOwnedAndEquippedFromTheStart()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsOwned(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme));
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsOwned(ShopCatalog.DefaultParcel));
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsEquipped(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.IsOwned("theme.candy"));
        }

        [Test]
        public void BuyWithCoins_NeedsEnoughCoins_AndOnlyOnce()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.TryBuyWithCoins("theme.candy"));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.IsOwned("theme.candy"));

            economy.AddCoins(200);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.TryBuyWithCoins("theme.candy"));
            Assert.AreEqual(50, economy.Coins);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsOwned("theme.candy"));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.TryBuyWithCoins("theme.candy")); // no double charge
            Assert.AreEqual(50, economy.Coins);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Equip_RequiresOwnership()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.Equip("theme.gold"));
            Assert.AreEqual(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme, save.Data.Theme);

            economy.AddCoins(600);
            economy.TryBuyWithCoins("theme.gold");
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.Equip("theme.gold"));
            Assert.AreEqual("theme.gold", save.Data.Theme);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.Equip(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.Equip("coins.small")); // not a cosmetic
        }

        [Test]
        public void CoinPack_GrantsCoins_Repeatably()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.GrantProduct("ff_coins_500"));
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.GrantProduct("ff_coins_500"));
            Assert.AreEqual(1000, economy.Coins);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.GrantProduct("ff_coins_1500"));
            Assert.AreEqual(2800, economy.Coins); // 1500 + 300 bonus
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.GrantProduct("not_a_product"));
        }

        [Test]
        public void StarterPack_GrantsItemsOnce()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.CanBuyProduct("ff_starter"));
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.GrantProduct("ff_starter"));

            Assert.AreEqual(1000, economy.Coins);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsOwned("theme.candy"));
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsOwned("parcel.gift"));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.CanBuyProduct("ff_starter"));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.GrantProduct("ff_starter"));
            Assert.AreEqual(1000, economy.Coins);
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- daily gift

        [Test]
        public void DailyGift_OncePerDay_StreakGrows_AndWrapsAfterSeven()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            int day = 100;

            Assert.IsTrue(economy.DailyAvailable(day));
            Assert.AreEqual(20, economy.ClaimDaily(day));
            Assert.AreEqual(0, economy.ClaimDaily(day)); // same day
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.DailyAvailable(day));

            Assert.AreEqual(25, economy.ClaimDaily(day + 1));
            Assert.AreEqual(2, save.Data.DailyStreak);
            for (int i = 2; i < 7; i++) economy.ClaimDaily(day + i);
            Assert.AreEqual(7, save.Data.DailyStreak);
            Assert.AreEqual(20, economy.ClaimDaily(day + 7)); // day 8 starts the cycle again
        }

        [Test]
        public void DailyGift_MissingADay_ResetsTheStreak()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            economy.ClaimDaily(10);
            economy.ClaimDaily(11);
            Assert.AreEqual(2, save.Data.DailyStreak);
            Assert.AreEqual(20, economy.ClaimDaily(14));
            Assert.AreEqual(1, save.Data.DailyStreak);
        }

        [Test]
        public void DayNumber_ChangesAtMidnight()
        {
            int a = PlayerEconomy.DayNumber(new DateTime(2026, 11, 4, 23, 59, 0));
            int b = PlayerEconomy.DayNumber(new DateTime(2026, 11, 5, 0, 1, 0));
            Assert.AreEqual(a + 1, b);
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- rewarded ads

        [Test]
        public void RewardedAds_AreCappedPerDay_WithCooldown_AndResetNextDay()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            int day = 50;
            double now = 1000;

            for (int i = 0; i < EconomyConfig.DailyAdLimit; i++)
            {
                Assert.IsTrue(economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(day, now), "ad " + i);
                economy.RegisterAdWatched(day, now);
                Assert.IsFalse(economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(day, now + 1), "cooldown after ad " + i);
                now += EconomyConfig.AdCooldownSeconds + 1;
            }

            Assert.AreEqual(0, economy.AdsRemainingToday(day));
            Assert.IsFalse(economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(day, now));
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.CanOfferRewardedAd(day + 1, now));
            Assert.AreEqual(EconomyConfig.DailyAdLimit, economy.AdsRemainingToday(day + 1));
        }

        // ---------------------------------------------------------------- saving

        [Test]
        public void Schema2_RoundTripsEverything()
        {
            var store = new MemoryStore();
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save, store);
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps());
            economy.GrantProduct("ff_starter");
            economy.Equip("theme.candy");
            economy.Equip("parcel.gift");
            economy.ClaimDaily(77);
            economy.RegisterAdWatched(77, 10);
            int coins = economy.Coins;

            SaveService again;
            PlayerEconomy restored = NewEconomy(out again, store);

            Assert.AreEqual(coins, restored.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(3, again.Data.StarsFor(1));
            Assert.IsTrue(restored.IsOwned("parcel.gift"));
            Assert.AreEqual("theme.candy", again.Data.Theme);
            Assert.AreEqual("parcel.gift", again.Data.Parcel);
            Assert.AreEqual(77, again.Data.LastDailyDay);
            Assert.AreEqual(1, again.Data.DailyStreak);
            Assert.AreEqual(EconomyConfig.DailyAdLimit - 1, restored.AdsRemainingToday(77));
        }

        [Test]
        public void OldSchema1Save_StillLoads_WithEmptyEconomy()
        {
            var store = new MemoryStore();
            store.SetString(SaveService.Key, "v=1;unlocked=5;last=4;sound=0;volume=1;completed=1,2,3,4");
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save, store);

            Assert.AreEqual(5, save.Data.HighestUnlocked);
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.SoundOn);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, economy.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme, save.Data.Theme);
        }

        [Test]
        public void EquippedItemThatIsNotOwned_FallsBackToDefault()
        {
            var store = new MemoryStore();
            store.SetString(SaveService.Key, "v=2;unlocked=1;coins=-50;theme=theme.gold;parcel=parcel.gold;owned=;stars=1:9,2:2");
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save, store);

            Assert.AreEqual(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme, save.Data.Theme);
            Assert.AreEqual(ShopCatalog.DefaultParcel, save.Data.Parcel);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, economy.Coins); // never negative
            Assert.AreEqual(0, save.Data.StarsFor(1)); // 9 stars is not valid
        }

        [Test]
        public void ResetLevelProgress_KeepsCoinsPurchasesAndSettings()
        {
            SaveService save;
            PlayerEconomy economy = NewEconomy(out save);
            LevelData level = Level(1);
            economy.AwardLevel(level, level.MinimumTaps());
            economy.GrantProduct("ff_starter");
            economy.Equip("theme.candy");
            save.Data.SoundOn = false;
            int coins = economy.Coins;

            save.ResetLevelProgress();

            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsCompleted(1));
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.IsUnlocked(2));
            Assert.AreEqual(0, save.Data.StarsFor(1));
            Assert.AreEqual(coins, economy.Coins);
            Assert.IsTrue(economy.IsOwned("theme.candy"));
            Assert.AreEqual("theme.candy", save.Data.Theme);
            Assert.IsFalse(save.Data.SoundOn);
        }

        [Test]
        public void Catalog_HasConsistentItems()
        {
            var ids = new System.Collections.Generic.HashSet<string>();
            foreach (ShopItem item in ShopCatalog.All)
            {
                Assert.IsTrue(ids.Add(item.Id), "duplicate id " + item.Id);
                bool realMoney = item.ProductId != null;
                Assert.AreEqual(realMoney, item.CoinPrice < 0, item.Id);
                if (realMoney) Assert.Greater(item.CoinsGranted, 0, item.Id);
                foreach (string granted in item.ItemsGranted) Assert.IsNotNull(ShopCatalog.Find(granted), granted);
            }
            Assert.IsNotNull(ShopCatalog.Find(ShopCatalog.DefaultTheme));
            Assert.IsNotNull(ShopCatalog.Find(ShopCatalog.DefaultParcel));
        }
    }

using System.Collections.Generic;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;

namespace FlowFactory.Tests
{
    /// <summary>Tests that need Unity types (ScriptableObject, Resources). Run them in the Test Runner, EditMode tab.</summary>
    public class UnityLevelAssetTests
    {
        [Test]
        public void LevelDefinition_RoundTripsLevelData()
        {
            LevelData data = LevelCatalog.BuildAll()[4];
            var definition = ScriptableObject.CreateInstance<LevelDefinition>();
            try
            {
                definition.CopyFrom(data);
                LevelData back = definition.ToLevelData();

                Assert.AreEqual(data.id, back.id);
                Assert.AreEqual(data.width, back.width);
                Assert.AreEqual(data.height, back.height);
                CollectionAssert.AreEqual(data.tiles, back.tiles);
                CollectionAssert.AreEqual(data.solution, back.solution);
                CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(LevelValidator.Validate(back));
            }
            finally
            {
                Object.DestroyImmediate(definition);
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void Repository_ProvidesAllValidLevels()
        {
            IList<LevelData> levels = LevelRepository.LoadAll();
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelCatalog.Count, levels.Count);
            for (int i = 0; i < levels.Count; i++)
            {
                Assert.AreEqual(i + 1, levels[i].id);
                CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(LevelValidator.Validate(levels[i]), "level " + (i + 1));
            }
        }

        [Test]
        public void GeneratedAssets_MatchTheCatalog_WhenPresent()
        {
            LevelDefinition[] assets = Resources.LoadAll<LevelDefinition>(LevelRepository.ResourcesFolder);
            if (assets.Length == 0)
            {
                Assert.Ignore("No level assets yet: run the menu Flow Factory > Generate Level Assets.");
            }

            Assert.AreEqual(LevelCatalog.Count, assets.Length);
            foreach (LevelDefinition asset in assets)
            {
                CollectionAssert.IsEmpty(LevelValidator.Validate(asset.ToLevelData()), asset.name);
            }
        }
    }
}
