using System.Collections;
using FlowFactory.Core;
using NUnit.Framework;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.TestTools;

namespace FlowFactory.Tests
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Runs the real game in Play Mode (Test Runner > PlayMode, or the command line  -runTests -testPlatform PlayMode).
    /// The game starts itself (GameBootstrap) in the test scene; these tests drive it through the same code paths
    /// as a player and fail on any Console error or exception.
    /// </summary>
    public class PlayModeSmokeTests
    {
        private FlowFactoryApp app;

        [UnitySetUp]
        public IEnumerator SetUp()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            float waited = 0f;
            while (FlowFactoryApp.Instance == null && waited < 10f)
            {
                waited += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                yield return null;
            }
            app = FlowFactoryApp.Instance;
            Assert.IsNotNull(app, "The game did not start (GameBootstrap).");
            app.Automation_ResetEverything();
            yield return null;
        }

        [TearDown]
        public void TearDown()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Boots_ToMenu_WithoutErrors()
        {
            yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual("Menu", app.ScreenName);
            Assert.AreEqual(36, app.LevelCount);
            Assert.IsNotNull(Camera.main);
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Screens_Navigate_WithoutErrors()
        {
            app.Automation_ShowLevelSelect();
            yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual("LevelSelect", app.ScreenName);
            app.Automation_ShowSettings();
            yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual("Settings", app.ScreenName);
            app.Automation_ShowMenu();
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab.Coins);
            yield return null;
            Assert.IsTrue(app.AnyMoneyOverlayOpen);
            app.Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab.Themes);
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_OpenShop(ShopTab.Parcels);
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_OpenLevel(1);
            yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual("Playing", app.ScreenName);
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator AllLevels_PlayThrough_InOrder()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 8f; // the parcel animation uses scaled time; this keeps the test short
            int previousCoins = app.Economy.Coins;
            for (int id = 1; id <= app.LevelCount; id++)
            {
                Assert.IsTrue(app.Progress.Data.IsUnlocked(id), "level " + id + " should be unlocked after finishing level " + (id - 1));
                app.Automation_OpenLevel(id);
                yield return null;
                Assert.AreEqual(id, app.CurrentLevelId);
                Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Idle, app.CurrentLevelState);

                yield return app.Automation_SolveAndWait(120f);
                Assert.IsTrue(app.AutomationResult, "level " + id + " did not finish");
                Assert.IsTrue(app.Progress.Data.IsCompleted(id), "level " + id + " not saved as completed");
                Assert.GreaterOrEqual(app.Progress.Data.StarsFor(id), 1);
                Assert.Greater(app.Economy.Coins, previousCoins, "level " + id + " paid no coins");
                previousCoins = app.Economy.Coins;
            }
            Assert.AreEqual(app.LevelCount, app.Progress.Data.Completed.Count);
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Progress_IsWrittenToStorage_AndReloads()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 8f;
            for (int id = 1; id <= 3; id++)
            {
                app.Automation_OpenLevel(id);
                yield return app.Automation_SolveAndWait(60f);
                Assert.IsTrue(app.AutomationResult);
            }
            app.Economy.AddCoins(77);
            int coins = app.Economy.Coins;

            // A fresh service over the same PlayerPrefs is what the next app start sees.
            var reloaded = new SaveService(new PlayerPrefsStore(), app.LevelCount);
            Assert.IsTrue(reloaded.Data.IsCompleted(3));
            Assert.IsTrue(reloaded.Data.IsUnlocked(4));
            Assert.AreEqual(coins, reloaded.Data.Coins);
            Assert.AreEqual(app.Progress.Data.StarsFor(2), reloaded.Data.StarsFor(2));
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator UnsolvedRun_FailsFriendly_AndAllowsRetry()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 6f;
            app.Automation_OpenLevel(1);
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_PressRun();
            float waited = 0f;
            while (app.CurrentLevelState != LevelState.Idle && waited < 30f)
            {
                waited += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                yield return null;
            }
            Assert.AreEqual(LevelState.Idle, app.CurrentLevelState, "the game must return to editing after a failed run");
            Assert.IsFalse(app.Progress.Data.IsCompleted(1));

            yield return app.Automation_SolveAndWait(60f);
            Assert.IsTrue(app.AutomationResult);
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Shop_BuyEquipTheme_ThenPlayALevel()
        {
            app.Economy.AddCoins(300);
            Assert.IsTrue(app.Economy.TryBuyWithCoins("theme.candy"));
            Assert.IsTrue(app.Economy.Equip("theme.candy"));
            ThemeCatalog.Apply("theme.candy", app.Progress.Data.Parcel);
            app.Automation_OpenLevel(1);
            yield return null;
            Time.timeScale = 8f;
            yield return app.Automation_SolveAndWait(60f);
            Assert.IsTrue(app.AutomationResult);
            Assert.AreEqual("theme.candy", app.Progress.Data.Theme);
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator Hint_CostsCoins()
        {
            app.Economy.AddCoins(100);
            int before = app.Economy.Coins;
            app.Automation_OpenLevel(5);
            yield return null;
            app.Automation_PressHint();
            yield return null;
            Assert.AreEqual(before - EconomyConfig.HintCost, app.Economy.Coins);
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }

        [UnityTest]
        public IEnumerator DailyGift_CanBeClaimedOncePerDay()
        {
            int today = PlayerEconomy.DayNumber(System.DateTime.Now);
            Assert.IsTrue(app.Economy.DailyAvailable(today));
            int coins = app.Economy.ClaimDaily(today);
            Assert.Greater(coins, 0);
            Assert.AreEqual(0, app.Economy.ClaimDaily(today));
            yield return null;
            LogAssert.NoUnexpectedReceived();
        }
    }
}

FILE: Tools/LevelGen/gen_levels.py   (192 lines)
====================================================================================================
"""One-off authoring tool: generates levels 13-36 as ASCII specs for LevelCatalog.cs.

The output is committed as static data (the game never generates levels at runtime). Re-running with the same
seed reproduces the same levels. Every level is checked here (known solution works, initial state is not solved)
and again by the C# tests. Usage: python3 gen_levels.py > levels_13_36.txt
"""
import random, sys

N, E, S, W = 0, 1, 2, 3
DX = {N: 0, E: 1, S: 0, W: -1}
DY = {N: -1, E: 0, S: 1, W: 0}
OPP = lambda d: (d + 2) % 4
BASE = {"straight": {N, S}, "corner": {N, E}, "source": {N}, "target": {N}}


def openings(kind, rot):
    return {(d + rot) % 4 for d in BASE[kind]}


def validate(w, h, tiles, rots):
    """tiles: dict (x,y)->kind ; rots: dict (x,y)->rotation. Returns True if the route reaches the target."""
    src = next(p for p, k in tiles.items() if k == "source")
    pos, heading = src, next(iter(openings("source", rots[src])))
    for _ in range(w * h * 4 + 4):
        nx, ny = pos[0] + DX[heading], pos[1] + DY[heading]
        if not (0 <= nx < w and 0 <= ny < h) or (nx, ny) not in tiles:
            return False
        kind = tiles[(nx, ny)]
        if kind == "block":
            return False
        entry = OPP(heading)
        if entry not in openings(kind, rots[(nx, ny)]):
            return False
        if kind == "target":
            return True
        if kind == "source":
            return False
        heading = next(iter(openings(kind, rots[(nx, ny)]) - {entry}))
        pos = (nx, ny)
    return False


def random_path(rng, w, h, lo, hi):
    for _ in range(4000):
        start = (rng.randrange(w), rng.randrange(h))
        path = [start]
        seen = {start}
        target_len = rng.randint(lo, hi)

        def extend():
            if len(path) >= target_len:
                return True
            x, y = path[-1]
            dirs = [N, E, S, W]
            rng.shuffle(dirs)
            for d in dirs:
                nxt = (x + DX[d], y + DY[d])
                if 0 <= nxt[0] < w and 0 <= nxt[1] < h and nxt not in seen:
                    path.append(nxt)
                    seen.add(nxt)
                    if extend():
                        return True
                    path.pop()
                    seen.discard(nxt)
            return False

        if extend():
            return path
    raise RuntimeError("no path")


def dir_between(a, b):
    for d in (N, E, S, W):
        if (a[0] + DX[d], a[1] + DY[d]) == b:
            return d


CORNER_NAME = {0: "NE", 1: "ES", 2: "SW", 3: "WN"}
ARROW = {N: "^", E: ">", S: "v", W: "<"}


def build(rng, w, h, lo, hi, rot_prob, decoy_prob, block_prob, fixed_decoy_prob, zero_tap_prob):
    for _ in range(500):
        path = random_path(rng, w, h, lo, hi)
        tiles, sol, init, token = {}, {}, {}, {}
        turns = 0
        for i, p in enumerate(path):
            if i == 0:
                d = dir_between(p, path[1])
                tiles[p] = "source"; sol[p] = init[p] = d
                token[p] = "S" + ARROW[d]
            elif i == len(path) - 1:
                d = dir_between(p, path[-2])
                tiles[p] = "target"; sol[p] = init[p] = d
                token[p] = "T" + ARROW[d]
            else:
                a, b = dir_between(p, path[i - 1]), dir_between(p, path[i + 1])
                if OPP(a) == b:
                    tiles[p] = "straight"
                    horizontal = a in (E, W)
                    sol[p] = 1 if horizontal else 0
                    name = "H" if horizontal else "V"
                else:
                    turns += 1
                    tiles[p] = "corner"
                    want = {a, b}
                    sol[p] = next(r for r in range(4) if openings("corner", r) == want)
                    name = CORNER_NAME[sol[p]]
                rotatable = rng.random() < rot_prob
                if rotatable:
                    if tiles[p] == "straight":
                        taps = 0 if rng.random() < zero_tap_prob else 1
                    else:
                        taps = 0 if rng.random() < zero_tap_prob else rng.choice([1, 2, 3])
                    init[p] = (sol[p] - taps) % 4
                    token[p] = name + (":%d" % taps if taps else "")
                else:
                    init[p] = sol[p]
                    token[p] = name.lower()
        # decoys and crates on the other cells
        for y in range(h):
            for x in range(w):
                p = (x, y)
                if p in tiles:
                    continue
                r = rng.random()
                if r < block_prob:
                    tiles[p] = "block"; sol[p] = init[p] = 0; token[p] = "#"
                elif r < block_prob + decoy_prob:
                    kind = rng.choice(["straight", "corner"])
                    tiles[p] = kind
                    s = rng.randrange(4) if kind == "corner" else rng.randrange(2)
                    sol[p] = s
                    name = ("H" if s == 1 else "V") if kind == "straight" else CORNER_NAME[s]
                    if rng.random() < fixed_decoy_prob:
                        init[p] = s; token[p] = name.lower()
                    else:
                        taps = rng.choice([0, 1, 2, 3]) if kind == "corner" else rng.choice([0, 1])
                        init[p] = (s - taps) % 4
                        token[p] = name + (":%d" % taps if taps else "")
                else:
                    token[p] = "."
        needed = sum(1 for p in path[1:-1] if tiles[p] != "x" and init[p] != sol[p] and
                     openings(tiles[p], init[p]) != openings(tiles[p], sol[p]))
        if not validate(w, h, tiles, sol):
            continue
        if validate(w, h, tiles, init):
            continue
        if needed < 2 or turns < 2:
            continue
        rows = [" ".join(token[(x, y)] for x in range(w)) for y in range(h)]
        return rows, len(path), turns, needed
    raise RuntimeError("could not build")


TITLES = [
    "Overtime", "Night Shift", "Loading Bay", "Conveyor Maze", "Fast Lane", "Sorting Hall",
    "Detour", "Zig Zag", "Packing Line", "Mixed Up", "Tight Squeeze", "Grand Opening",
    "Second Wind", "Rush Order", "Back Alley", "Crossroads", "Spiral Stairs", "Long Haul",
    "Hidden Path", "Clockwork", "Double Back", "Foreman's Test", "Master Plan", "Factory Legend",
]
HINTS = [
    "Trace the route from the source first.", "Count the turns before you tap.",
    "Belts that are already right can be left alone.", "Crates never move. Plan around them.",
    "Some belts only look useful.", "Work backwards from the target.",
    "A long way round can be the only way.", "Fix the corners, then the straights.",
]


def main():
    rng = random.Random(20261104)
    out = []
    for i in range(24):
        world = 2 if i < 12 else 3
        t = (i % 12) / 11.0
        if world == 2:
            lo, hi = 8 + int(t * 3), 11 + int(t * 3)
            params = dict(rot_prob=0.80 + 0.12 * t, decoy_prob=0.30 + 0.20 * t, block_prob=0.08 + 0.04 * t, fixed_decoy_prob=0.30, zero_tap_prob=0.10)
        else:
            lo, hi = 11 + int(t * 3), 15 + int(t * 3)
            params = dict(rot_prob=0.92 + 0.08 * t, decoy_prob=0.50 + 0.30 * t, block_prob=0.06 + 0.04 * t, fixed_decoy_prob=0.20, zero_tap_prob=0.18)
        rows, length, turns, needed = build(rng, 5, 5, lo, hi, **params)
        out.append((TITLES[i], HINTS[i % len(HINTS)], rows, length, turns, needed))
    for title, hint, rows, length, turns, needed in out:
        print('            new Spec("%s", "%s", false,' % (title, hint))
        for j, row in enumerate(rows):
            print('                "%s"%s' % (row, "),\n" if j == len(rows) - 1 else ","))
        sys.stderr.write("%-18s path=%2d turns=%2d wrongTiles=%2d\n" % (title, length, turns, needed))


main()

====================================================================================================
FILE: Tools/Install/install_flowfactory.ps1   (234 lines)
====================================================================================================
<#
FlowFactory3D installer for Windows (PowerShell 5.1 or 7).

Turns the FlowFactory3D zip (v0.3.1 or newer) into a working Unity 6.3 (Universal 3D) project and then runs the real checks:
Setup Project, EditMode tests, PlayMode tests and (if Android Build Support is installed) an APK build.
It never overwrites an existing project. Everything it ran, and its result, is written to a report file.

NOTE: this PowerShell version could not be run by its author (no PowerShell and no Unity where it was written).
The bash version of the same logic was tested with a simulated editor. Run this one and send back the report.

Usage:
  .\install_flowfactory.ps1 [-Zip FILE] [-Unity Unity.exe] [-Project DIR] [-Update] [-SkipTests] [-SkipApk]
If scripts are blocked:  powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\install_flowfactory.ps1
#>
param(
  [string]$Zip = "",
  [string]$Unity = "",
  [string]$Project = (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE "UnityProjects\FlowFactory3D"),
  [switch]$Update,
  [switch]$SkipTests,
  [switch]$SkipApk
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$report = "$Project.install_report.txt"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path (Split-Path $Project) | Out-Null
Set-Content -Path $report -Value "" -Encoding UTF8

function Say([string]$text) { Write-Host $text; Add-Content -Path $report -Value $text -Encoding UTF8 }
function Step([string]$text) { Say ""; Say "== $text ==" }
function Result([string]$text) { Say "   RESULT: $text" }
function Stop-Install([string]$text, [int]$code = 1) { Say "STOPPED: $text"; Say "Report: $report"; exit $code }

Say "FlowFactory3D install report - $(Get-Date -Format 'yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss')"
Say "Only steps marked PASSED or FAILED were actually executed."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1. find the zip
Step "1. Find the ZIP"
if (-not $Zip) {
  $dirs = @((Get-Location).Path, (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE "Downloads"), (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE "Desktop"), (Join-Path $env:USERPROFILE "Documents"))
  # The newest FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip wins (so v0.3.1 is preferred over an older v0.3 download).
  $any = $dirs | ForEach-Object { Get-ChildItem -Path $_ -Filter "FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } |
    Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
  if ($any) { $Zip = $any.FullName }
}
if (-not $Zip -or -not (Test-Path $Zip)) { Stop-Install "The FlowFactory3D zip was not found. Run again with  -Zip C:\full\path\FlowFactory3D_v0.3.1.zip" 2 }
Result "PASSED - $Zip"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2. find Unity
Step "2. Find Unity"
if (-not $Unity) {
  $roots = @("C:\Program Files\Unity\Hub\Editor", "$env:ProgramFiles\Unity\Hub\Editor", "D:\Unity\Hub\Editor")
  $found = @()
  foreach ($r in $roots) {
    if (Test-Path $r) { $found += Get-ChildItem -Path $r -Directory | Where-Object { Test-Path (Join-Path $_.FullName "Editor\Unity.exe") } }
  }
  $found = $found | Sort-Object Name
  $pick = $found | Where-Object { $_.Name -like "6000.3.*" } | Select-Object -Last 1
  if (-not $pick) { $pick = $found | Select-Object -Last 1 }
  if ($pick) { $Unity = Join-Path $pick.FullName "Editor\Unity.exe" }
}
if (-not $Unity -or -not (Test-Path $Unity)) { Stop-Install "Unity editor not found. Install Unity 6.3 LTS with Unity Hub, or run with  -Unity C:\path\Unity.exe" 2 }
$unityVersion = if ($Unity -match "6000\.\d+\.[0-9a-z]+") { $Matches[0] } else { "unknown" }
Result "PASSED - $Unity (version $unityVersion)"
if ($unityVersion -notlike "6000.3.*") { Say "   WARNING: Unity 6.3 LTS (6000.3.x) is the supported version; this one may differ." }

$editorDir = Split-Path $Unity
$templateDir = Join-Path $editorDir "Data\Resources\PackageManager\ProjectTemplates"
$androidDir = Join-Path $editorDir "Data\PlaybackEngines\AndroidPlayer"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. existing project?
Step "3. Check for an existing project"
$mode = "new"
if ((Test-Path $Project) -and (Get-ChildItem -Path $Project -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Select-Object -First 1)) {
  if (Test-Path (Join-Path $Project "Assets\Scripts\Core\LevelCatalog.cs")) {
    $mode = if ($Update) { "update" } else { "existing" }
  } elseif (Test-Path (Join-Path $Project "ProjectSettings\ProjectVersion.txt")) {
    Stop-Install "$Project is another Unity project. Not touching it. Choose a different folder with -Project." 3
  } else {
    Stop-Install "$Project exists and is not empty. Not touching it. Choose a different folder with -Project." 3
  }
}
switch ($mode) {
  "new" { Result "PASSED - no project at $Project; a new one will be created" }
  "existing" { Result "PASSED - FlowFactory is already installed at $Project. Nothing is overwritten (use -Update to copy newer code with a backup)" }
  "update" { Result "PASSED - existing FlowFactory project; will back up and update scripts" }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- unpack the zip
$tmp = Join-Path ([System.IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ("flowfactory_" + [guid]::NewGuid().ToString("N"))
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $tmp | Out-Null
Expand-Archive -Path $Zip -DestinationPath (Join-Path $tmp "zip") -Force
$src = Join-Path $tmp "zip\FlowFactory3D"
if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $src "Assets\Scripts"))) { Stop-Install "The zip does not contain FlowFactory3D\Assets\Scripts." 4 }

function Copy-GameFiles {
  foreach ($part in "Scripts", "Resources", "Tests") {
    $dest = Join-Path $Project "Assets\$part"
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $dest | Out-Null
    Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $src "Assets\$part\*") -Destination $dest -Recurse -Force
  }
  $tools = Join-Path $Project "Tools"
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $tools | Out-Null
  Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $src "Tools\*") -Destination $tools -Recurse -Force
  if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $Project "README_HE.md"))) { Copy-Item (Join-Path $src "README_HE.md") $Project }
}

function Invoke-Unity([string[]]$unityArgs) {
  $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Unity -ArgumentList $unityArgs -Wait -PassThru -NoNewWindow
  return $p.ExitCode
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 4. create the project
if ($mode -eq "new") {
  Step "4. Create a Unity 6.3 Universal 3D project"
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $Project | Out-Null
  $template = Get-ChildItem -Path $templateDir -Filter "com.unity.template.universal-3d*.tgz" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
    Sort-Object LastWriteTime -Descending | Select-Object -First 1
  if ($template) {
    $extract = Join-Path $tmp "template"
    New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $extract | Out-Null
    & tar.exe -xzf $template.FullName -C $extract
    $data = Join-Path $extract "package\ProjectData~"
    if (Test-Path $data) {
      Copy-Item -Path (Join-Path $data "*") -Destination $Project -Recurse -Force
      Result "PASSED - project files copied from the editor's Universal 3D template ($($template.Name))"
    } else {
      Stop-Install "The Universal 3D template has an unexpected layout. Create the project with Unity Hub (template: Universal 3D) and re-run with -Project." 5
    }
  } else {
    Say "   Universal 3D template not found in this editor; creating an empty Built-in-pipeline project instead."
    $null = Invoke-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-quit", "-createProject", "`"$Project`"", "-logFile", "`"$Project.createproject.log`"")
    if (Test-Path (Join-Path $Project "ProjectSettings\ProjectVersion.txt")) {
      Result "PASSED - empty project created (NOT Universal 3D; the game uses its own shaders and still works)"
    } else {
      Result "FAILED - Unity could not create a project; see $Project.createproject.log"
      Stop-Install "Project creation failed." 5
    }
  }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 5. copy files and packages
Step "5. Copy game files and check packages"
if ($mode -eq "update") {
  $backup = Join-Path $Project ("Backups\" + (Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd_HHmmss"))
  New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $backup | Out-Null
  foreach ($part in "Scripts", "Resources", "Tests") {
    $from = Join-Path $Project "Assets\$part"
    if (Test-Path $from) { Copy-Item -Path $from -Destination (Join-Path $backup $part) -Recurse }
  }
  Say "   Backed up Assets\Scripts, Resources, Tests to $backup"
}
if ($mode -ne "existing") {
  Copy-GameFiles
  Result "PASSED - Assets\Scripts, Assets\Resources, Assets\Tests copied"
}

$manifestPath = Join-Path $Project "Packages\manifest.json"
if (Test-Path $manifestPath) {
  $manifest = Get-Content $manifestPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
  $wanted = [ordered]@{ "com.unity.inputsystem" = "1.14.0"; "com.unity.test-framework" = "1.5.1"; "com.unity.ugui" = "2.0.0" }
  $added = @()
  foreach ($name in $wanted.Keys) {
    if (-not ($manifest.dependencies.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains $name)) {
      $manifest.dependencies | Add-Member -NotePropertyName $name -NotePropertyValue $wanted[$name]
      $added += "$name@$($wanted[$name])"
    }
  }
  $manifest | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 | Set-Content -Path $manifestPath -Encoding UTF8
  Say ("   Packages already present kept as they are; added: " + $(if ($added.Count) { $added -join ", " } else { "nothing" }))
  Result "PASSED - manifest checked (Unity resolves the packages on first open; if a version is rejected, use Package Manager)"
} else {
  Result "SKIPPED - Packages\manifest.json missing; in Unity install Input System and Test Framework from Window > Package Manager"
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 6. setup project in Unity
$logs = Join-Path $Project "Logs\Install"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $logs | Out-Null
Step "6. Unity: import, compile and Flow Factory > Setup Project (batch mode)"
$exit = Invoke-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-quit", "-projectPath", "`"$Project`"", "-logFile", "`"$logs\setup.log`"",
  "-executeMethod", "FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.SetUpProjectBatch")
$setupLog = Join-Path $logs "setup.log"
$compileErrors = if (Test-Path $setupLog) { @(Select-String -Path $setupLog -Pattern "error CS").Count } else { -1 }
$levelAssets = @(Get-ChildItem -Path (Join-Path $Project "Assets\Resources\Levels") -Filter "*.asset" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue).Count
$sceneExists = Test-Path (Join-Path $Project "Assets\Scenes\Main.unity")
if ($exit -eq 0 -and $compileErrors -eq 0 -and $sceneExists -and $levelAssets -eq 36) {
  Result "PASSED - exit 0, 0 compile errors, Assets\Scenes\Main.unity exists, 36 level assets"
} else {
  Result "FAILED - exit=$exit, compile errors=$compileErrors, Main.unity=$sceneExists, level assets=$levelAssets. See $setupLog"
  if (Test-Path $setupLog) { Select-String -Path $setupLog -Pattern "error CS" | Select-Object -First 5 | ForEach-Object { Say ("     " + $_.Line) } }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 7-8. tests
function Get-TestSummary([string]$file) {
  if (-not (Test-Path $file)) { return "no result file" }
  $head = (Select-String -Path $file -Pattern "<test-run " | Select-Object -First 1).Line
  $total = if ($head -match ' total="(\d+)"') { $Matches[1] } else { "?" }
  $passed = if ($head -match ' passed="(\d+)"') { $Matches[1] } else { "?" }
  $failed = if ($head -match ' failed="(\d+)"') { $Matches[1] } else { "?" }
  return "total=$total passed=$passed failed=$failed"
}
if (-not $SkipTests) {
  foreach ($platform in "EditMode", "PlayMode") {
    Step "7/8. Unity Test Runner: $platform"
    $code = Invoke-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-projectPath", "`"$Project`"", "-runTests", "-testPlatform", $platform,
      "-testResults", "`"$logs\$platform.xml`"", "-logFile", "`"$logs\$platform.log`"")
    $summary = Get-TestSummary (Join-Path $logs "$platform.xml")
    if ($code -eq 0) { Result "PASSED - $summary (Unity exit 0)" }
    else { Result "FAILED - Unity exit $code, $summary. See $logs\$platform.log and $logs\$platform.xml" }
  }
} else {
  Step "7/8. Tests"; Result "SKIPPED (-SkipTests)"
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 9. APK
Step "9. Android APK"
if ($SkipApk) {
  Result "SKIPPED (-SkipApk)"
} elseif (-not (Test-Path $androidDir)) {
  Result "SKIPPED - Android Build Support is not installed in this editor. Unity Hub > Installs > (gear) > Add modules > Android Build Support (with OpenJDK and Android SDK & NDK Tools)"
} else {
  $code = Invoke-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-quit", "-projectPath", "`"$Project`"", "-buildTarget", "Android", "-logFile", "`"$logs\apk.log`"",
    "-executeMethod", "FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.BuildAndroidBatch")
  $apk = Join-Path $Project "Builds\Android\FlowFactory3D-dev.apk"
  if ($code -eq 0 -and (Test-Path $apk)) { Result ("PASSED - $apk (" + [math]::Round((Get-Item $apk).Length / 1MB, 1) + " MB)") }
  else { Result "FAILED - exit $code, APK present: $(Test-Path $apk). See $logs\apk.log" }
}

Say ""
Say "Not covered by this script: the screenshot tour (needs a graphics window: in Unity use Flow Factory > Capture Screenshot Tour),"
Say "touch input and FPS on a real phone. Project: $Project"
Say "Report: $report"
Remove-Item -Recurse -Force $tmp -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

====================================================================================================
FILE: Tools/Install/install_flowfactory.sh   (226 lines)
====================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# FlowFactory3D installer for macOS / Linux.
#
# Turns the FlowFactory3D zip (v0.3.1 or newer) into a working Unity 6.3 (Universal 3D) project and then runs the real checks:
# Setup Project, EditMode tests, PlayMode tests and (if Android Build Support is installed) an APK build.
# It never overwrites an existing project. Everything it ran, and its result, is written to a report file.
#
# Usage:
#   ./install_flowfactory.sh [--zip FILE] [--unity EDITOR_EXE] [--project DIR] [--update] [--skip-tests] [--skip-apk]
#
#   --zip      the FlowFactory3D zip (default: searched in ~/Downloads, ~/Desktop, ~/Documents, current folder)
#   --unity    Unity editor executable (default: searched in the usual Unity Hub folders; 6000.3.x preferred)
#   --project  where the Unity project goes (default: ~/UnityProjects/FlowFactory3D)
#   --update   the project already has FlowFactory: back up Assets/Scripts, Resources, Tests and copy the new ones
set -u

ZIP=""; UNITY="${UNITY:-}"; PROJECT="$HOME/UnityProjects/FlowFactory3D"
UPDATE=0; SKIP_TESTS=0; SKIP_APK=0
while [[ $# -gt 0 ]]; do
  case "$1" in
    --zip) ZIP="$2"; shift 2;;
    --unity) UNITY="$2"; shift 2;;
    --project) PROJECT="$2"; shift 2;;
    --update) UPDATE=1; shift;;
    --skip-tests) SKIP_TESTS=1; shift;;
    --skip-apk) SKIP_APK=1; shift;;
    *) echo "Unknown option: $1"; exit 64;;
  esac
done

REPORT="${PROJECT}.install_report.txt"
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$PROJECT")"
: > "$REPORT"
say() { echo "$*"; echo "$*" >> "$REPORT"; }
step() { say ""; say "== $* =="; }
result() { say "   RESULT: $*"; }
die() { say "STOPPED: $1"; say "Report: $REPORT"; exit "${2:-1}"; }

say "FlowFactory3D install report - $(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')"
say "Only steps marked PASSED or FAILED were actually executed."

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1. find the zip
step "1. Find the ZIP"
if [[ -z "$ZIP" ]]; then
  # The newest FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip wins (so v0.3.1 is preferred over an older v0.3 download).
  ZIP="$(ls -t "$PWD"/FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip "$HOME"/Downloads/FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip "$HOME"/Desktop/FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip "$HOME"/Documents/FlowFactory3D_v0.*.zip 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
fi
[[ -n "$ZIP" && -f "$ZIP" ]] || die "The FlowFactory3D zip was not found. Run again with  --zip /full/path/FlowFactory3D_v0.3.1.zip" 2
result "PASSED - $ZIP"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2. find Unity
step "2. Find Unity"
if [[ -z "$UNITY" ]]; then
  candidates=()
  while IFS= read -r line; do candidates+=("$line"); done < <(
    ls -d /Applications/Unity/Hub/Editor/*/Unity.app/Contents/MacOS/Unity \
          "$HOME"/Unity/Hub/Editor/*/Editor/Unity \
          /opt/Unity/Hub/Editor/*/Editor/Unity /opt/unity*/Editor/Unity 2>/dev/null | sort -V)
  for c in "${candidates[@]:-}"; do [[ "$c" == *"/6000.3."* ]] && UNITY="$c"; done
  [[ -z "$UNITY" && ${#candidates[@]} -gt 0 ]] && UNITY="${candidates[${#candidates[@]}-1]}"
fi
[[ -n "$UNITY" && -x "$UNITY" ]] || die "Unity editor not found. Install Unity 6.3 LTS with Unity Hub, or run with  --unity /path/to/Unity" 2
UNITY_VERSION="$(echo "$UNITY" | grep -o '6000\.[0-9]*\.[0-9a-z]*' | head -1)"
result "PASSED - $UNITY (version ${UNITY_VERSION:-unknown})"
[[ "$UNITY_VERSION" == 6000.3.* ]] || say "   WARNING: Unity 6.3 LTS (6000.3.x) is the supported version; this one may differ."

# Locate editor data folders (they differ per platform).
EDITOR_DIR="$(dirname "$UNITY")"
TEMPLATE_DIR=""; ANDROID_DIR=""
for base in "$EDITOR_DIR/Data" "$EDITOR_DIR/.." "$EDITOR_DIR/../.."; do
  [[ -d "$base/Resources/PackageManager/ProjectTemplates" ]] && TEMPLATE_DIR="$base/Resources/PackageManager/ProjectTemplates"
  [[ -d "$base/PlaybackEngines/AndroidPlayer" ]] && ANDROID_DIR="$base/PlaybackEngines/AndroidPlayer"
done
for base in "$EDITOR_DIR/../Resources"; do
  [[ -d "$base/PackageManager/ProjectTemplates" ]] && TEMPLATE_DIR="$base/PackageManager/ProjectTemplates"
done

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3. existing project?
step "3. Check for an existing project"
MODE="new"
if [[ -d "$PROJECT" && -n "$(ls -A "$PROJECT" 2>/dev/null)" ]]; then
  if [[ -f "$PROJECT/Assets/Scripts/Core/LevelCatalog.cs" ]]; then
    if [[ $UPDATE -eq 1 ]]; then MODE="update"; else MODE="existing"; fi
  elif [[ -f "$PROJECT/ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt" ]]; then
    die "$PROJECT is another Unity project. Not touching it. Choose a different folder with --project." 3
  else
    die "$PROJECT exists and is not empty. Not touching it. Choose a different folder with --project." 3
  fi
fi
case "$MODE" in
  new) result "PASSED - no project at $PROJECT; a new one will be created";;
  existing) result "PASSED - FlowFactory is already installed at $PROJECT. Nothing is overwritten (use --update to copy newer code with a backup)";;
  update) result "PASSED - existing FlowFactory project; will back up and update scripts";;
esac

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- unpack the zip into a temp folder
TMP="$(mktemp -d)"; trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT
if command -v unzip >/dev/null 2>&1; then unzip -q "$ZIP" -d "$TMP/zip"; else python3 -m zipfile -e "$ZIP" "$TMP/zip"; fi
SRC="$TMP/zip/FlowFactory3D"
[[ -d "$SRC/Assets/Scripts" ]] || die "The zip does not contain FlowFactory3D/Assets/Scripts." 4

copy_game_files() {
  mkdir -p "$PROJECT/Assets"
  for part in Scripts Resources Tests; do
    mkdir -p "$PROJECT/Assets/$part"
    cp -R "$SRC/Assets/$part/." "$PROJECT/Assets/$part/"
  done
  mkdir -p "$PROJECT/Tools"; cp -R "$SRC/Tools/." "$PROJECT/Tools/"
  [[ -f "$PROJECT/README_HE.md" ]] || cp "$SRC/README_HE.md" "$PROJECT/README_HE.md"
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 4. create the project
if [[ "$MODE" == "new" ]]; then
  step "4. Create a Unity 6.3 Universal 3D project"
  TEMPLATE="$(ls -t "$TEMPLATE_DIR"/com.unity.template.universal-3d*.tgz 2>/dev/null | head -1)"
  mkdir -p "$PROJECT"
  if [[ -n "$TEMPLATE" ]]; then
    mkdir -p "$TMP/template" && tar -xzf "$TEMPLATE" -C "$TMP/template"
    if [[ -d "$TMP/template/package/ProjectData~" ]]; then
      cp -R "$TMP/template/package/ProjectData~/." "$PROJECT/"
      result "PASSED - project files copied from the editor's Universal 3D template ($(basename "$TEMPLATE"))"
    else
      die "The Universal 3D template has an unexpected layout. Create the project with Unity Hub (template: Universal 3D) and re-run with --project." 5
    fi
  else
    say "   Universal 3D template not found in this editor; creating an empty Built-in-pipeline project instead."
    "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -quit -createProject "$PROJECT" -logFile "$PROJECT.createproject.log"
    if [[ -f "$PROJECT/ProjectSettings/ProjectVersion.txt" ]]; then
      result "PASSED - empty project created (NOT Universal 3D; the game uses its own shaders and still works)"
    else
      result "FAILED - Unity could not create a project; see $PROJECT.createproject.log"; die "Project creation failed." 5
    fi
  fi
fi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 5. copy files and packages
step "5. Copy game files and check packages"
if [[ "$MODE" == "update" ]]; then
  BACKUP="$PROJECT/Backups/$(date +%Y%m%d_%H%M%S)"; mkdir -p "$BACKUP"
  for part in Scripts Resources Tests; do [[ -d "$PROJECT/Assets/$part" ]] && cp -R "$PROJECT/Assets/$part" "$BACKUP/$part"; done
  say "   Backed up Assets/Scripts, Resources, Tests to $BACKUP"
fi
if [[ "$MODE" != "existing" ]]; then
  copy_game_files
  result "PASSED - Assets/Scripts, Assets/Resources, Assets/Tests copied (existing files with the same name were replaced only in update mode)"
fi

MANIFEST="$PROJECT/Packages/manifest.json"
if [[ -f "$MANIFEST" ]] && command -v python3 >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  python3 - "$MANIFEST" >> "$REPORT" <<'PY'
import json, sys
path = sys.argv[1]
data = json.load(open(path))
deps = data.setdefault("dependencies", {})
wanted = {"com.unity.inputsystem": "1.14.0", "com.unity.test-framework": "1.5.1", "com.unity.ugui": "2.0.0"}
added = []
for name, version in wanted.items():
    if name not in deps:
        deps[name] = version
        added.append(name + "@" + version)
json.dump(data, open(path, "w"), indent=2)
print("   Packages already present kept as they are; added: " + (", ".join(added) if added else "nothing"))
PY
  cat "$REPORT" | tail -1
  result "PASSED - manifest checked (Unity resolves the packages on first open; if a version is rejected, use Package Manager)"
else
  result "SKIPPED - python3 or manifest missing; in Unity install Input System and Test Framework from Window > Package Manager"
fi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 6. setup project in Unity
LOGS="$PROJECT/Logs/Install"; mkdir -p "$LOGS"
step "6. Unity: import, compile and Flow Factory > Setup Project (batch mode)"
"$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -quit -projectPath "$PROJECT" -logFile "$LOGS/setup.log" \
  -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.SetUpProjectBatch
UNITY_EXIT=$?
COMPILE_ERRORS="$(grep -c 'error CS' "$LOGS/setup.log" 2>/dev/null || true)"
LEVEL_ASSETS="$(ls "$PROJECT"/Assets/Resources/Levels/*.asset 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
if [[ $UNITY_EXIT -eq 0 && "${COMPILE_ERRORS:-0}" == "0" && -f "$PROJECT/Assets/Scenes/Main.unity" && "$LEVEL_ASSETS" == "36" ]]; then
  result "PASSED - exit 0, 0 compile errors, Assets/Scenes/Main.unity exists, 36 level assets"
else
  result "FAILED - exit=$UNITY_EXIT, compile errors=${COMPILE_ERRORS:-?}, Main.unity=$([[ -f $PROJECT/Assets/Scenes/Main.unity ]] && echo yes || echo no), level assets=$LEVEL_ASSETS. See $LOGS/setup.log"
  grep -m5 'error CS' "$LOGS/setup.log" 2>/dev/null | sed 's/^/     /' | tee -a "$REPORT"
fi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 7-8. tests
parse_xml() { # file -> "total=.. passed=.. failed=.."
  local f="$1"
  [[ -f "$f" ]] || { echo "no result file"; return; }
  local line; line="$(grep -o '<test-run [^>]*>' "$f" | head -1)"
  echo "total=$(echo "$line" | grep -o ' total="[0-9]*"' | grep -o '[0-9]*') passed=$(echo "$line" | grep -o ' passed="[0-9]*"' | grep -o '[0-9]*') failed=$(echo "$line" | grep -o ' failed="[0-9]*"' | grep -o '[0-9]*')"
}
if [[ $SKIP_TESTS -eq 0 ]]; then
  for platform in EditMode PlayMode; do
    step "7/8. Unity Test Runner: $platform"
    "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -projectPath "$PROJECT" -runTests -testPlatform "$platform" \
      -testResults "$LOGS/$platform.xml" -logFile "$LOGS/$platform.log"
    code=$?
    summary="$(parse_xml "$LOGS/$platform.xml")"
    errs="$(grep -c -E '^\[?(Exception|Error)|NullReferenceException' "$LOGS/$platform.log" 2>/dev/null || true)"
    if [[ $code -eq 0 ]]; then result "PASSED - $summary (Unity exit 0; Console error-like lines in log: ${errs:-0})"
    else result "FAILED - Unity exit $code, $summary. See $LOGS/$platform.log and $LOGS/$platform.xml"; fi
  done
else
  step "7/8. Tests"; result "SKIPPED (--skip-tests)"
fi

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 9. APK
step "9. Android APK"
if [[ $SKIP_APK -eq 1 ]]; then
  result "SKIPPED (--skip-apk)"
elif [[ -z "$ANDROID_DIR" ]]; then
  result "SKIPPED - Android Build Support is not installed in this editor. Unity Hub > Installs > (gear) > Add modules > Android Build Support (with OpenJDK and Android SDK & NDK Tools)"
else
  "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -quit -projectPath "$PROJECT" -buildTarget Android -logFile "$LOGS/apk.log" \
    -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.BuildAndroidBatch
  code=$?
  APK="$PROJECT/Builds/Android/FlowFactory3D-dev.apk"
  if [[ $code -eq 0 && -f "$APK" ]]; then result "PASSED - $APK ($(du -h "$APK" | cut -f1))"
  else result "FAILED - exit $code, APK present: $([[ -f $APK ]] && echo yes || echo no). See $LOGS/apk.log"; fi
fi

say ""
say "Not covered by this script: the screenshot tour (needs a graphics window: in Unity use Flow Factory > Capture Screenshot Tour),"
say "touch input and FPS on a real phone. Project: $PROJECT"
say "Report: $REPORT"

FILE: Tools/UnityChecks/run_unity_checks.ps1   (34 lines)
====================================================================================================
# Runs the real Unity checks from the command line (Windows PowerShell).
# Usage:  .\run_unity_checks.ps1 -Unity "C:\Program Files\Unity\Hub\Editor\6000.3.0f1\Editor\Unity.exe" -What all
# What: editmode | playmode | levels | apk | all   (the screenshot tour needs a graphics device: use the menu
#       Flow Factory > Capture Screenshot Tour inside the editor)
param(
  [Parameter(Mandatory = $true)][string]$Unity,
  [string]$What = "all"
)
$project = (Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "..\..")).Path
$out = Join-Path $project "Logs\UnityChecks"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $out | Out-Null
$status = 0

function Run-Unity([string[]]$arguments) {
  Write-Host ">>> Unity $($arguments -join ' ')"
  $p = Start-Process -FilePath $Unity -ArgumentList $arguments -Wait -PassThru -NoNewWindow
  if ($p.ExitCode -ne 0) { Write-Host "FAILED (exit $($p.ExitCode))"; $script:status = 1 }
}

if ($What -in "levels", "all") {
  Run-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-quit", "-projectPath", $project, "-logFile", "$out\levels.log", "-executeMethod", "FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.GenerateMissingLevels")
}
if ($What -in "editmode", "all") {
  Run-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-projectPath", $project, "-runTests", "-testPlatform", "EditMode", "-testResults", "$out\editmode.xml", "-logFile", "$out\editmode.log")
}
if ($What -in "playmode", "all") {
  Run-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-projectPath", $project, "-runTests", "-testPlatform", "PlayMode", "-testResults", "$out\playmode.xml", "-logFile", "$out\playmode.log")
}
if ($What -in "apk", "all") {
  Run-Unity @("-batchmode", "-nographics", "-quit", "-projectPath", $project, "-buildTarget", "Android", "-logFile", "$out\apk.log", "-executeMethod", "FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.BuildAndroidBatch")
}
Write-Host "Done. Results in $out (status $status)"
exit $status

====================================================================================================
FILE: Tools/UnityChecks/run_unity_checks.sh   (38 lines)
====================================================================================================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runs the real Unity checks from the command line (macOS / Linux).
# Usage: UNITY=/path/to/Unity ./run_unity_checks.sh [editmode|playmode|levels|tour|apk|all]
# Results are written to ../../Logs/UnityChecks (test XML files, editor logs, screenshots, the APK path).
set -u
PROJECT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)"
UNITY="${UNITY:?Set UNITY to the Unity editor executable, e.g. /Applications/Unity/Hub/Editor/6000.3.0f1/Unity.app/Contents/MacOS/Unity}"
OUT="$PROJECT/Logs/UnityChecks"
mkdir -p "$OUT"
WHAT="${1:-all}"
status=0

run() { echo ">>> $*"; "$@" || { echo "FAILED: $*"; status=1; }; }

if [[ "$WHAT" == "levels" || "$WHAT" == "all" ]]; then
  run "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -quit -projectPath "$PROJECT" -logFile "$OUT/levels.log" \
      -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.GenerateMissingLevels
fi
if [[ "$WHAT" == "editmode" || "$WHAT" == "all" ]]; then
  run "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -projectPath "$PROJECT" -runTests -testPlatform EditMode \
      -testResults "$OUT/editmode.xml" -logFile "$OUT/editmode.log"
fi
if [[ "$WHAT" == "playmode" || "$WHAT" == "all" ]]; then
  run "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -projectPath "$PROJECT" -runTests -testPlatform PlayMode \
      -testResults "$OUT/playmode.xml" -logFile "$OUT/playmode.log"
fi
if [[ "$WHAT" == "tour" ]]; then
  # Needs a real graphics device (not -nographics): run on a machine with a display.
  run "$UNITY" -projectPath "$PROJECT" -screen-width 1080 -screen-height 1920 -logFile "$OUT/tour.log" \
      -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.CaptureScreenshotTour
fi
if [[ "$WHAT" == "apk" || "$WHAT" == "all" ]]; then
  run "$UNITY" -batchmode -nographics -quit -projectPath "$PROJECT" -buildTarget Android -logFile "$OUT/apk.log" \
      -executeMethod FlowFactory.EditorTools.FlowFactoryMenu.BuildAndroidBatch
fi
echo "Done. Results in $OUT (exit status $status)"
exit $status

Approximate three.js preview of the game (NOT Unity): it re-implements the tile geometry, camera framing and
HUD layout of the C# code so the layout can be checked without the Unity Editor. levels.json is exported from
LevelCatalog + RouteValidator. Usage: npm i three@0.160.0 playwright, then node shoot.js <outputDir>.

function parcelAt(p,headingDeg){const g=new THREE.Group();g.position.set(p[0],p[1],-p[2]);g.rotation.y=headingDeg*Math.PI/180;scene.add(g);

function along(points,t){const segs=[];let tot=0;for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++){const d=Math.hypot(points[i][0]-points[i-1][0],points[i][2]-points[i-1][2]);segs.push(d);tot+=d;}
 let d=t*tot;for(let i=0;i<segs.length;i++){if(d<=segs[i]||i===segs.length-1){const k=Math.min(1,d/segs[i]);const a=points[i],b=points[i+1];
  return {p:[a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*k,.2,a[2]+(b[2]-a[2])*k],hd:Math.atan2(b[0]-a[0],b[2]-a[2])*180/Math.PI};}d-=segs[i];}}

}

const { chromium } = require('playwright');
const http = require('http'), fs = require('fs'), path = require('path');
const root = __dirname, out = process.argv[2];
const srv = http.createServer((req,res)=>{const f=path.join(root,decodeURIComponent(req.url.split('?')[0]));if(!fs.existsSync(f)||fs.statSync(f).isDirectory()){res.writeHead(404);return res.end();}
 const t=f.endsWith('.js')||f.endsWith('.mjs')?'text/javascript':f.endsWith('.json')?'application/json':'text/html';res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':t});fs.createReadStream(f).pipe(res);}).listen(0);
(async()=>{
 const port=srv.address().port;
 const shots=[['01_menu','screen=menu&fy=0.57'],['02_level_select','screen=select&page=0'],['03_level_select_world3','screen=select&page=2'],['04_hud_level1','screen=game&level=1&state=init'],['05_level1_run','screen=game&level=1&state=run&t=0.55'],['06_level1_fail_feedback','screen=game&level=1&state=fail'],['07_success_stars_coins','screen=success&level=1&state=solved&stars=3'],['08_level5','screen=game&level=5&state=init'],['09_level18_world2','screen=game&level=18&state=init'],['10_level30_world3','screen=game&level=30&state=init'],['11_level12_factory_awake','screen=game&level=12&state=solved'],['12_shop_coins','screen=shop&tab=coins'],['13_shop_themes','screen=shop&tab=themes'],['14_shop_parcels','screen=shop&tab=parcels'],['15_shop_not_enough_coins','screen=shop&tab=themes&modal=coins'],['16_shop_test_purchase','screen=shop&tab=coins&modal=buy'],['17_daily_gift','screen=daily&fy=0.57'],['18_test_ad','screen=ad'],['19_theme_candy','screen=game&level=18&state=init&theme=candy&parcel=gift'],['20_theme_midnight','screen=game&level=30&state=init&theme=midnight&parcel=ocean'],['21_theme_gold','screen=game&level=24&state=init&theme=gold&parcel=gold']];
 const b=await chromium.launch({args:['--use-gl=swiftshader','--enable-webgl','--ignore-gpu-blocklist']});
 const p=await b.newPage({viewport:{width:540,height:960},deviceScaleFactor:2});
 p.on('console',m=>console.log('console:',m.text()));p.on('pageerror',e=>console.log('pageerror:',e.message));
 for(const [n,qs] of shots){await p.goto(`http://localhost:${port}/index.html?${qs}`);await p.waitForFunction('window.READY===true',null,{timeout:20000});await p.waitForTimeout(300);await p.screenshot({path:`${out}/${n}.png`});console.log('ok',n);}
 await b.close();srv.close();
})();
